- **Coding choice (labelled):** `wtdClean`/`mtdClean` switch the layer off for the rest of a week or month that holds a roll. The catalogue says multi-day anchors reset at rolls. The other reading is to re-anchor both VWAPs at `rollOn(1)`; it is not coded (Appendix F).

**Full strategy** — `_Ex03PeriodAvwapLayer.Strategy.CS`:

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 3 - AV-08 week-open / month-open VWAP regime layer (aboveWTD && aboveMTD for longs only).
// The host trigger below is ILLUSTRATIVE ONLY (not a catalogue rule): AV-08 is a layer for PS-01 / PS-07 / MT-01 / AV-02.
// Chart: @ES 1m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED, 24h CME Globex template.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex03PeriodAvwapLayer : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Av08Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1;
			public int hostLookback = 20;                       // illustrative host: close above the prior 20-bar high
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), to = new TimeSpan(15, 0, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public TimeSpan halfDayExitBefore = new TimeSpan(0, 5, 0);   // early-close days: flat at RthCloseEt - 5 min
			public double stopTicks = 16;                       // illustrative host stop (>= the ES cost floor of 14 ticks)
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;            // Ettienne, 2026-10-23: skip if q x stop x $50 > 1% of equity (16 ticks, 1 ct = $200: equity >= $20,000)
		}
		protected Av08Config Cfg { get; } = new Av08Config();

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_lx;
		private IOrderPriced m_lxStp;
		private DateTime m_lastRollDay = DateTime.MinValue;
		private double m_stop = double.NaN;

		public _Ex03PeriodAvwapLayer(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "AV08LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "AV08LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "AV08LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			// The VWAP slots are [Input]s of the VP base: these are defaults, the Format Signal dialog can override them.
			Vwap1Type = EVwapType.Week;  Vwap1DataStream = 1;   // WTD: resets on the first bar of a new Monday-start Globex week
			Vwap2Type = EVwapType.Month; Vwap2DataStream = 1;   // MTD
			Vwap3Type = EVwapType.Disabled;
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			base.StartCalc();
			if (Vwap1Type != EVwapType.Week || Vwap2Type != EVwapType.Month || Vwap1DataStream != 1 || Vwap2DataStream != 1)
				throw new ArgumentException("_Ex03PeriodAvwapLayer: needs Vwap1Type = Week and Vwap2Type = Month on Data1");
			m_lastRollDay = DateTime.MinValue; m_stop = double.NaN;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime t = Bars.Time[0], day = GlobexDate(t);
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(t).TimeOfDay;
			if (rollOn(1)) m_lastRollDay = day;
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			TimeSpan flat = flatAt(day);

			if (pos > 0)
			{
				if (tod >= flat && tod < new TimeSpan(17, 0, 0)) m_lx.Send();
				else if (!double.IsNaN(m_stop)) m_lxStp.Send(m_stop);
				return;
			}
			if (tod < Cfg.from || tod >= Cfg.to || tod >= flat || Bars.CurrentBar <= Cfg.hostLookback + 1) return;

			// --- the AV-08 layer (closed bar: the VWAP slots include this bar) ---
			double wtd = CurrentVwap1, mtd = CurrentVwap2, c = Bars.Close[0];
			DateTime weekStart = day.AddDays(-(((int)day.DayOfWeek + 6) % 7));   // Monday of the Globex week
			DateTime monthStart = new DateTime(day.Year, day.Month, 1);
			// Week/Month VWAPs do NOT reset at a roll. CODING CHOICE: the layer is off for the rest of a week/month that holds a roll
			// (the catalogue says multi-day anchors reset at rolls; the alternative is to re-anchor the VWAPs at rollOn(1))
			bool wtdClean = m_lastRollDay < weekStart, mtdClean = m_lastRollDay < monthStart;
			bool layerLong = Vwap1Active && Vwap2Active && wtdClean && mtdClean && c > wtd && c > mtd;

			// --- illustrative host event ---
			double hh = double.MinValue;
			for (int i = 1; i <= Cfg.hostLookback; i++) hh = Math.Max(hh, Bars.High[i]);
			if (!(c > hh)) return;

			var ctx = new Dictionary<string, string> { { "wtd", wtd.ToString("F2") }, { "mtd", mtd.ToString("F2") },
				{ "wtdClean", wtdClean.ToString() }, { "mtdClean", mtdClean.ToString() } };
			if (!layerLong) { if (journalOn()) logSignal("AV-08", +1, c, double.NaN, ctx, false); return; }
			m_stop = c - Cfg.stopTicks * tickSize();
			if (Cfg.contracts * (c - m_stop) * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction))
			{
				if (journalOn()) { trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"AV-08 {t:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} skip: risk budget {Cfg.riskBudgetFraction:P2}"); logSignal("AV-08", +1, c, m_stop, ctx, false); }
				m_stop = double.NaN; return;
			}
			if (journalOn()) { journalArm("AV08LE", c, m_stop, double.NaN, ctx); logSignal("AV-08", +1, c, m_stop, ctx, true); }
			m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts);
			m_lxStp.Send(m_stop);
		}

		/// <summary>Flat time for a Globex day: Cfg.flat, or on a CME early-close day RthCloseEt - halfDayExitBefore if earlier
		/// (_CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day).RthCloseEt; same rule as A263Config.halfDayExitBefore).</summary>
		private TimeSpan flatAt(DateTime day)
		{
			_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day);
			TimeSpan f = Cfg.flat;
			if (cme.RthCloseEt.HasValue && cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore < f) f = cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore;
			return f;
		}
	}
}
```

**Compile status:** compiled — type-checked with Roslyn against the repo sources (`origin/main` a07257a) plus MC API stubs (stubs not authoritative). Not run in MultiCharts; no backtest results.

### 3.7 Example 14 (`_Ex14LateDayVwap`) — VW-05 late-day continuation (v1 15:00 arm): sign(15:00 price - prior RTH close) == sign(price - session VWAP) (easy–medium)

**Features (direct).** F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-VWAP (§5.10), F-JRNL (§5.13), F-EVT (§5.14), M-MTF (§5.17).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> VW-05 late-day continuation (v1 15:00 arm): sign(15:00 price - prior RTH close) == sign(price - session VWAP)
> -> 15:00 market in that direction, exit 15:55, stop 1 x ATR30m floored. (A2-06's 15:29 primary rule is a variant; not coded here.)
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m, Data2 = @ES 30m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
> Features: F-VWAP (Vwap1Type = Session -> CurrentVwap1, Vwap1Active), F-SESS (Session.priorRthClose), F-TM, F-CAL half-days.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 14 - VW-05 late-day continuation (v1 15:00 arm): sign(15:00 price - prior RTH close) == sign(price - session VWAP)
// -> 15:00 market in that direction, exit 15:55, stop 1 x ATR30m floored. (A2-06's 15:29 primary rule is a variant; not coded here.)
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m, Data2 = @ES 30m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
// Features: F-VWAP (Vwap1Type = Session -> CurrentVwap1, Vwap1Active), F-SESS (Session.priorRthClose), F-TM, F-CAL half-days.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex14LateDayVwap : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Vw05Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1;
			public TimeSpan entryAt = new TimeSpan(15, 0, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public TimeSpan halfDayExitBefore = new TimeSpan(0, 5, 0);
			public int atrLength = 14;
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Vw05Config Cfg { get; } = new Vw05Config();

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_se, m_lx, m_sx;
		private IOrderPriced m_lxStp, m_sxStp;
		private double m_stop = double.NaN;
		private DateTime m_tradedDay = DateTime.MinValue;

		public _Ex14LateDayVwap(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			// VWAP slot 1 = session VWAP on Data1. These VP-base settings are [Input]s on main: set them in code AND do not edit them in
			// the Format Signal dialog (see the F-VWAP pitfalls).
			Vwap1Type = EVwapType.Session; Vwap1DataStream = 1;
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "VW05LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_se = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "VW05SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "VW05LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "VW05SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "VW05LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "VW05SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			HtfStreams = new[] { 2 };
			base.StartCalc();
			if (Vwap1Type != EVwapType.Session) throw new ArgumentException("_Ex14LateDayVwap: Vwap1Type must be Session");
			m_stop = double.NaN; m_tradedDay = DateTime.MinValue;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay, flat = flatAt(day);
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				if (tod >= flat && tod < new TimeSpan(17, 0, 0)) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); else m_sxStp.Send(m_stop);
				return;
			}
			if (rollOn(1)) { m_tradedDay = day; return; }                         // prior close is the old contract on the splice day
			if (tod != Cfg.entryAt || m_tradedDay == day || tod >= flat) return;
			SessionStats s = Session;
			if (s == null || s.priorRthClose <= 0 || !Vwap1Active) return;
			double px = Bars.Close[0], vwap = CurrentVwap1;
			if (vwap <= 0) return;
			int a = Math.Sign(px - s.priorRthClose), b = Math.Sign(px - vwap);
			m_tradedDay = day;
			if (a == 0 || a != b) { if (journalOn()) logSignal("VW-05", a, px, double.NaN, new Dictionary<string, string> { { "vwapSide", b.ToString() } }, false); return; }
			double tick = tickSize(), atr = atr30();
			if (double.IsNaN(atr)) return;
			double r = Math.Max(atr, Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick);
			if (overRiskBudget(r)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"VW-05 {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd} skip: stop {r / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget {Cfg.riskBudgetFraction:P2}"); return; }
			m_stop = px - a * r;
			if (a > 0) m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts); else m_se.Send(Cfg.contracts);
			if (journalOn())
			{
				var ctx = new Dictionary<string, string> { { "vwap", vwap.ToString("F2") }, { "priorClose", s.priorRthClose.ToString("F2") }, { "atr30", atr.ToString("F2") } };
				journalArm(a > 0 ? "VW05LE" : "VW05SE", px, m_stop, double.NaN, ctx);
				logSignal("VW-05", a, px, m_stop, ctx, true);
			}
		}

		private double atr30()
		{
			int n = Cfg.atrLength;
			if (BarsOfData(2).CurrentBar < n + 2) return double.NaN;
			double sum = 0;
			for (int k = 0; k < n; k++)
			{
				DateTime t; double o, h, l, c, v, o1, h1, l1, pc, v1;
				HtfClosedBar(2, k, out t, out o, out h, out l, out c, out v);
				HtfClosedBar(2, k + 1, out t, out o1, out h1, out l1, out pc, out v1);
				sum += Math.Max(h, pc) - Math.Min(l, pc);
			}
			return sum / n;
		}

		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }

		private TimeSpan flatAt(DateTime day)
		{
			_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day);
			TimeSpan f = Cfg.flat;
			if (cme.RthCloseEt.HasValue && cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore < f) f = cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore;
			return f;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.8 Example 18 (`_Ex18FirstHourBreak`) — A2-56 first-hour breakout (core arm, no LBR setup filter): after 10:30 ET a buy stop at FH high + 1 tick and a (easy–medium)

**Features (direct).** F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> A2-56 first-hour breakout (core arm, no LBR setup filter): after 10:30 ET a buy stop at FH high + 1 tick and a
> sell stop at FH low - 1 tick until 15:00; stop at the other side of the first hour (+/- 1 tick), floored; flat 15:55.
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
> Features: F-TM (NoEntryBefore/After, FlatBy, MaxTradesPerDay, entryAllowed, mustFlatten), CME clock adapter, F-JRNL.
> Extension point: hostGate() is virtual; example 24 (RG-07) derives from this class and adds the exclusions.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 18 - A2-56 first-hour breakout (core arm, no LBR setup filter): after 10:30 ET a buy stop at FH high + 1 tick and a
// sell stop at FH low - 1 tick until 15:00; stop at the other side of the first hour (+/- 1 tick), floored; flat 15:55.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
// Features: F-TM (NoEntryBefore/After, FlatBy, MaxTradesPerDay, entryAllowed, mustFlatten), CME clock adapter, F-JRNL.
// Extension point: hostGate() is virtual; example 24 (RG-07) derives from this class and adds the exclusions.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex18FirstHourBreak : _CustomSignal
	{
		protected sealed class A256Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1;
			public TimeSpan open = new TimeSpan(9, 30, 0), fhEnd = new TimeSpan(10, 30, 0);
			public TimeSpan lastEntry = new TimeSpan(15, 0, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public TimeSpan halfDayExitBefore = new TimeSpan(0, 5, 0);
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected A256Config Cfg { get; } = new A256Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leStp, m_seStp, m_lxStp, m_sxStp;
		private IOrderMarket m_lx, m_sx;
		private _CmeSessionClock m_clock;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private double m_fhH = double.NaN, m_fhL = double.NaN, m_stop = double.NaN;
		private bool m_skip;

		public _Ex18FirstHourBreak(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		private _CmeSessionClock Clock { get { if (m_clock == null) m_clock = new _CmeSessionClock(resolveBarTimeZone(Bars.Info)); return m_clock; } }
		protected override DateTime tradingDayKey(DateTime t) { return Clock.GlobexDate(t); }
		protected override TimeSpan sessionClock(DateTime t) { return Clock.ToEastern(t).TimeOfDay; }
		protected override bool isRthBar(DateTime t) { return Clock.IsRthValueBarEt(Clock.ToEastern(t)); }

		/// <summary>Extra entry gate for derived strategies (true = allowed). Called once per bar while the stops would be sent.</summary>
		protected virtual bool hostGate(DateTime day, out string why) { why = null; return true; }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			NoEntryBefore = Cfg.fhEnd; NoEntryAfter = Cfg.lastEntry; FlatBy = Cfg.flat; MaxTradesPerDay = 1;
			m_leStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "A256LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_seStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "A256SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A256LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A256SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A256LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A256SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			m_clock = null;
			base.StartCalc();
			m_day = DateTime.MinValue; m_fhH = m_fhL = m_stop = double.NaN; m_skip = false;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = tradingDayKey(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) { m_day = day; m_fhH = m_fhL = double.NaN; m_skip = false; FlatBy = flatAt(day); }
			if (rollOn(1)) m_skip = true;
			TimeSpan tod = sessionClock(Bars.Time[0]);
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (mustFlatten()) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else if (pos < 0) m_sx.Send(); return; }
			if (tod > Cfg.open && tod <= Cfg.fhEnd && isRthBar(Bars.Time[0]))
			{
				m_fhH = double.IsNaN(m_fhH) ? Bars.High[0] : Math.Max(m_fhH, Bars.High[0]);
				m_fhL = double.IsNaN(m_fhL) ? Bars.Low[0] : Math.Min(m_fhL, Bars.Low[0]);
			}
			double tick = tickSize();
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				if (double.IsNaN(m_stop)) m_stop = pos > 0 ? m_fhL - tick : m_fhH + tick;
				double e = StrategyInfo.AvgEntryPrice;                                     // floor from the actual fill
				if (Math.Abs(e - m_stop) < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) m_stop = e - Math.Sign(pos) * Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick;
				if (pos > 0) m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); else m_sxStp.Send(m_stop);
				return;
			}
			m_stop = double.NaN;
			if (m_skip || double.IsNaN(m_fhH) || tod <= Cfg.fhEnd) return;
			string why;
			if (!entryAllowed(out why) || !hostGate(day, out why)) return;
			double buy = m_fhH + tick, sell = m_fhL - tick;
			double r = Math.Max(buy - sell, Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick);   // either side risks the first-hour range (+2 ticks), floored
			if (overRiskBudget(r)) { if (!m_skip && journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-56 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} skip: stop {r / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget {Cfg.riskBudgetFraction:P2}"); m_skip = true; return; }
			m_leStp.Send(buy, Cfg.contracts); m_seStp.Send(sell, Cfg.contracts);
			if (journalOn() && Bars.High[0] < buy && Bars.Low[0] > sell && tod == Cfg.fhEnd + TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1))
			{
				var ctx = new Dictionary<string, string> { { "fhHigh", m_fhH.ToString("F2") }, { "fhLow", m_fhL.ToString("F2") } };
				journalArm("A256LE", buy, sell, double.NaN, ctx);
				journalArm("A256SE", sell, buy, double.NaN, ctx);
			}
		}

		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }

		private TimeSpan flatAt(DateTime day)
		{
			_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day);
			TimeSpan f = Cfg.flat;
			if (cme.RthCloseEt.HasValue && cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore < f) f = cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore;
			return f;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.9 Example 20 (`_Ex20EmaVwapPullback`) — DV-15 EMA20 pullback with a session-VWAP slope filter (easy–medium)

**Features (direct).** F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-VWAP (§5.10), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13), F-EVT (§5.14), M-DAILY (§5.19).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> DV-15 EMA20 pullback with a session-VWAP slope filter.
> Side = sign(close - EMA20) held for >= 3 closed 30m bars. On a touch of the EMA (bar range contains it) while the session-VWAP slope
> over 8 bars has the same sign with |slope| >= s x ATR(14, 30m) and price is on the same side of VWAP: a limit at the EMA for the next bar.
> Pitfall: stop/target are sent from the bar after the fill, so the fill bar itself is unprotected.
> Bracket 1:1 at f x ATR20d (DailyContext), floored at 14 ticks. RTH 10:00-15:00 entries, flat 15:55. s = 0.05, f = 0.15 (grid values from
> the catalogue, not tuned results).
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 30m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
> Features: F-VWAP (Vwap1Type/Vwap1DataStream set in code, CurrentVwap1, Vwap1Active), M-DAILY (NeedsDaily, Atr20d), F-TM (FlatBy).

**Pitfalls.**

- The fill bar is unprotected: the stop is first sent on the bar after the fill.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 20 - DV-15 EMA20 pullback with a session-VWAP slope filter.
// Side = sign(close - EMA20) held for >= 3 closed 30m bars. On a touch of the EMA (bar range contains it) while the session-VWAP slope
// over 8 bars has the same sign with |slope| >= s x ATR(14, 30m) and price is on the same side of VWAP: a limit at the EMA for the next bar.
// Pitfall: stop/target are sent from the bar after the fill, so the fill bar itself is unprotected.
// Bracket 1:1 at f x ATR20d (DailyContext), floored at 14 ticks. RTH 10:00-15:00 entries, flat 15:55. s = 0.05, f = 0.15 (grid values from
// the catalogue, not tuned results).
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 30m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
// Features: F-VWAP (Vwap1Type/Vwap1DataStream set in code, CurrentVwap1, Vwap1Active), M-DAILY (NeedsDaily, Atr20d), F-TM (FlatBy).
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex20EmaVwapPullback : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Dv15Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, emaLength = 20, sideBars = 3, slopeBars = 8, atrLength = 14;
			public double slopeAtr = 0.05, bracketAtr20d = 0.15;
			public TimeSpan firstEntry = new TimeSpan(10, 0, 0), lastEntry = new TimeSpan(15, 0, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Dv15Config Cfg { get; } = new Dv15Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leLmt, m_seLmt, m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private IOrderMarket m_lx, m_sx;
		private double m_ema = double.NaN, m_stop, m_target;
		private int m_sideRun, m_side, m_emaBars;
		private readonly List<double> m_vwap = new List<double>();   // closed-bar VWAP values, newest last (session-reset aware)
		private DateTime m_vwapDay = DateTime.MinValue;

		public _Ex20EmaVwapPullback(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			Vwap1Type = EVwapType.Session; Vwap1DataStream = 1;     // [Input]s on the base: set in code
			FlatBy = Cfg.flat;
			m_leLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "DV15LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_seLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "DV15SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV15LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV15SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV15LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV15SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV15LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV15ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			NeedsDaily = true;                                      // before base.StartCalc (M-DAILY is built there)
			base.StartCalc();
			m_ema = double.NaN; m_emaBars = 0; m_sideRun = 0; m_side = 0; m_vwap.Clear(); m_vwapDay = DateTime.MinValue;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			double c = Bars.Close[0], tick = tickSize();
			double k = 2.0 / (Cfg.emaLength + 1);
			m_ema = double.IsNaN(m_ema) ? c : m_ema + k * (c - m_ema); m_emaBars++;
			int prevSide = m_side, prevRun = m_sideRun;              // side state up to the previous bar
			int s = Math.Sign(c - m_ema);
			m_sideRun = s != 0 && s == m_side ? m_sideRun + 1 : 1; m_side = s;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_vwapDay) { m_vwapDay = day; m_vwap.Clear(); }
			if (Vwap1Active) m_vwap.Add(CurrentVwap1);

			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				if (mustFlatten()) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); } else { m_sxStp.Send(m_stop); m_sxLmt.Send(m_target); }
				return;
			}
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
			if (!IsRth() || tod < Cfg.firstEntry || tod > Cfg.lastEntry || m_emaBars < Cfg.emaLength || rollOn(1)) return;
			if (m_vwap.Count <= Cfg.slopeBars || DailyContext == null || !DailyContext.Valid) return;
			int side = prevRun >= Cfg.sideBars ? prevSide : 0;                                      // side established BEFORE this bar
			if (side == 0 || !(Bars.Low[0] <= m_ema && Bars.High[0] >= m_ema)) return;                // touch
			double vw = m_vwap[m_vwap.Count - 1], slope = vw - m_vwap[m_vwap.Count - 1 - Cfg.slopeBars];
			double atr = averageTrueRange(Bars, Cfg.atrLength);
			if (!(atr > 0) || Math.Sign(slope) != side || Math.Abs(slope) < Cfg.slopeAtr * atr || Math.Sign(c - vw) != side) return;
			double d = Math.Max(Cfg.bracketAtr20d * DailyContext.Atr20d, Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick);
			if (!(d > 0) || overRiskBudget(d)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"DV-15 {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} skip: bracket {d / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget"); return; }
			double entry = Math.Round(m_ema / tick) * tick;
			m_stop = entry - side * d; m_target = entry + side * d;      // 1:1 bracket
			if (side > 0) m_leLmt.Send(entry, Cfg.contracts); else m_seLmt.Send(entry, Cfg.contracts);
			if (journalOn()) logSignal("DV-15", side, entry, m_stop, new Dictionary<string, string> { { "vwap", vw.ToString("F2") }, { "slope", slope.ToString("F2") } }, true);
		}

		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }
	}
}
```

### 3.10 Example 17 (`_Ex17OpenFadeHvGate`) — A2-64 intraday mean reversion to the RTH open, gated by A2-70 (HV20 off-switch with hysteresis) (easy–medium)

**Features (direct).** F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13), M-DAILY (§5.19).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> A2-64 intraday mean reversion to the RTH open, gated by A2-70 (HV20 off-switch with hysteresis).
> Entry: limit fades at R +/- m x ATR20d (R = 09:30 RTH open), 09:45-15:00 ET. Target R. Flat 15:55. Disaster stop 2m x ATR20d, floored.
> Gate (A2-70): HV20 (annualised close-to-close, daily) > thetaOff blocks entries; re-enabled when HV20 < 0.8 x thetaOff.
> thetaOff below is a PLACEHOLDER to be set by the A2-70 study; it is not a result.
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Daily context aggregated from Data1 (no daily stream).
> Features: F-SESS (Session.rthOpen), M-DAILY (NeedsDaily, DailyConfig.hvLengths, Atr20d, Hv(n)), F-TM (entryAllowed), F-JRNL.

**Pitfalls.**

- Contains PLACEHOLDER values (marked in the code); calibrate them before reading any result.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 17 - A2-64 intraday mean reversion to the RTH open, gated by A2-70 (HV20 off-switch with hysteresis).
// Entry: limit fades at R +/- m x ATR20d (R = 09:30 RTH open), 09:45-15:00 ET. Target R. Flat 15:55. Disaster stop 2m x ATR20d, floored.
// Gate (A2-70): HV20 (annualised close-to-close, daily) > thetaOff blocks entries; re-enabled when HV20 < 0.8 x thetaOff.
// thetaOff below is a PLACEHOLDER to be set by the A2-70 study; it is not a result.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Daily context aggregated from Data1 (no daily stream).
// Features: F-SESS (Session.rthOpen), M-DAILY (NeedsDaily, DailyConfig.hvLengths, Atr20d, Hv(n)), F-TM (entryAllowed), F-JRNL.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex17OpenFadeHvGate : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class A264Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1;
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), to = new TimeSpan(15, 0, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public TimeSpan halfDayExitBefore = new TimeSpan(0, 5, 0);
			public double m = 0.5;                 // band multiple of ATR20d (A2-64 sweep parameter)
			public int atrLength = 20, hvLength = 20;
			public double thetaOff = 0.30;         // PLACEHOLDER (annualised HV); A2-70 sets it
			public double reEnable = 0.8;
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected A264Config Cfg { get; } = new A264Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leLmt, m_seLmt, m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private IOrderMarket m_lx, m_sx;
		private DateTime m_lastDaily = DateTime.MinValue, m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private bool m_gateOff;
		private double m_atr = double.NaN, m_hv = double.NaN, m_stop = double.NaN;
		private bool m_traded;
		private double m_r = double.NaN;

		public _Ex17OpenFadeHvGate(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			MaxTradesPerDay = 1;
			m_leLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "A264LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_seLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "A264SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A264LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A264SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A264LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A264SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A264LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A264ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			NeedsDaily = true;                      // M-DAILY, dataStream 0 = aggregate Data1 per Globex day (roll-adjusted)
			DailyConfig.atrLength = Cfg.atrLength; DailyConfig.hvLengths = new[] { Cfg.hvLength };
			base.StartCalc();
			m_lastDaily = m_day = DateTime.MinValue; m_gateOff = false; m_atr = m_hv = m_stop = double.NaN; m_traded = false;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			stepDaily();
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) { m_day = day; m_traded = false; }
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay, flat = flatAt(day);
			SessionStats s = Session;
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				m_traded = true;
				if (double.IsNaN(m_stop)) m_stop = StrategyInfo.AvgEntryPrice - Math.Sign(pos) * m_r;   // first bar in the trade
				if (tod >= flat && tod < new TimeSpan(17, 0, 0)) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(s.rthOpen); } else { m_sxStp.Send(m_stop); m_sxLmt.Send(s.rthOpen); }
				return;
			}
			if (rollOn(1)) m_traded = true;
			if (m_traded || s == null || s.rthOpen <= 0 || !(m_atr > 0) || m_gateOff) return;
			if (tod < Cfg.from || tod >= Cfg.to || tod >= flat) return;
			string why;
			if (!entryAllowed(out why)) return;
			double tick = tickSize(), band = Cfg.m * m_atr;
			double r = Math.Max(2 * Cfg.m * m_atr, Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick);   // disaster stop 2m x ATR, floored
			if (overRiskBudget(r)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-64 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} skip: stop {r / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget {Cfg.riskBudgetFraction:P2}"); m_traded = true; return; }
			double buyAt = Math.Round((s.rthOpen - band) / tick) * tick, sellAt = Math.Round((s.rthOpen + band) / tick) * tick;
			// both limits stand; whichever fills first sets the side (MaxTradesPerDay = 1 blocks a second entry). The protective stop
			// is placed from the fill price on the first closed bar in the trade: a fill-bar spike is unprotected (pitfall).
			m_leLmt.Send(buyAt, Cfg.contracts); m_seLmt.Send(sellAt, Cfg.contracts);
			m_r = r; m_stop = double.NaN;
		}

		/// <summary>New completed day in M-DAILY: ATR20d and HV20 from the (roll-adjusted) daily context, and the A2-70 hysteresis gate.</summary>
		private void stepDaily()
		{
			_DailyContext d = DailyContext;
			if (d == null || !d.Valid || d.PrevDate == m_lastDaily) return;
			m_lastDaily = d.PrevDate;
			m_atr = d.Atr20d; m_hv = d.Hv(Cfg.hvLength);
			if (double.IsNaN(m_hv)) return;
			bool was = m_gateOff;
			if (!m_gateOff && m_hv > Cfg.thetaOff) m_gateOff = true;
			else if (m_gateOff && m_hv < Cfg.reEnable * Cfg.thetaOff) m_gateOff = false;
			if (was != m_gateOff && journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-70 {d.PrevDate:yyyy-MM-dd} HV20 {m_hv:P1} gate {(m_gateOff ? "OFF" : "ON")}");
		}

		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }

		private TimeSpan flatAt(DateTime day)
		{
			_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day);
			TimeSpan f = Cfg.flat;
			if (cme.RthCloseEt.HasValue && cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore < f) f = cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore;
			return f;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.11 Example 4 (`_Ex04OpeningRvGate`) — A2-50 same-slot relative-volume gate (medium)

**Features (direct).** F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), M-TOD (§5.18), M-EVENT (§5.20).


**Catalogue rules (A2-50).** RV30 = Σ volume(09:30–10:00) / SSB(N = 20) of the same 30-minute window. HIGH if RV30 ≥ q_hi, LOW if ≤ q_lo, else MID. q_hi/q_lo are the trailing 250-session 70th/30th percentiles, rolled daily and causal. Computed once at 10:00; 08:30-report days are a separate stratum. It is a module that routes hosts, so it places no orders.

**Chart.** Data1 = @ES **30m** (bars end 10:00, 10:30, …); the strategy throws on another bar size. dxFeed unadjusted, 24h Globex template.

**Walk-through.**
- M-TOD is enabled for Volume with `TodSessions = 20`, `TodMinSessions = 20` and bucket 0 (the host then uses the Data1 bar size, 30 minutes).
- `todHalfDay` is overridden so early-close days stay out of the baseline.
- At the 10:00 bar close, RV30 = that bar's volume / the mean of the last 20 values in `TodNormaliser.VolumePool(todMinuteOfSession(t))` (prior sessions only; `base.CalcBar()` runs first). Half-days and NoRth days get no state.
- RV30 is compared with the trailing 250 completed sessions' values (strictly earlier), then pushed. The state is HIGH, MID or LOW, exposed as `Rv30State` and journaled.
- The event engine is used only to **tag** the day as an 08:30-report day (or as unknown coverage), so the stratum can be split later.

**Pitfalls.**
- **Assumption (labelled):** nearest-rank percentiles.
- The first 250 sessions after the TOD warm-up have no state (warm-up).
- On a 1m chart the 09:30–10:00 sum would need 30 buckets; the 30m chart makes it one bucket. The 15-minute window arm needs a 15m chart.

**Full strategy** — `_Ex04OpeningRvGate.Strategy.CS`:

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 4 - A2-50 same-slot relative-volume opening gate (module). Computes RV30 once per session at the 10:00 ET
// bar close and journals HIGH / MID / LOW. It places NO orders: hosts read Rv30State (A2-50 routes hosts, it is not a strategy).
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 30m (bars end 10:00, 10:30, ...), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED, 24h CME Globex template. A 30m chart is used
// because a 30m TOD bucket on a 1m chart groups bar ENDS 09:31-10:00 into two buckets (09:31-09:59 and 10:00-10:29).
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex04OpeningRvGate : _CustomSignal
	{
		public enum ERvState { Unknown = 0, Low = 1, Mid = 2, High = 3 }

		protected sealed class A250Config
		{
			public TimeSpan slotEnd = new TimeSpan(10, 0, 0);   // ET close-stamp of the 09:30-10:00 bar
			public int ssbSessions = 20;                         // SSB(N = 20)
			public int pctSessions = 250;                        // trailing percentile window
			public double qHi = 0.70, qLo = 0.30;
		}
		protected A250Config Cfg { get; } = new A250Config();

		/// <summary>Today's gate state (Unknown before 10:00 ET, on warm-up, half-days and holidays).</summary>
		public ERvState Rv30State { get; private set; }
		public double Rv30 { get; private set; }

		private _CmeSessionClock m_clock;
		private readonly List<double> m_history = new List<double>();   // prior sessions' RV30, oldest first
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;

		public _Ex04OpeningRvGate(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		private _CmeSessionClock Clock { get { if (m_clock == null) m_clock = new _CmeSessionClock(resolveBarTimeZone(Bars.Info)); return m_clock; } }
		protected override DateTime tradingDayKey(DateTime t) { return Clock.GlobexDate(t); }
		protected override TimeSpan sessionClock(DateTime t) { return Clock.ToEastern(t).TimeOfDay; }
		protected override bool isRthBar(DateTime t) { return Clock.IsRthValueBarEt(Clock.ToEastern(t)); }
		/// <summary>Half-days are kept out of the same-slot pools (M-TOD) and out of the gate.</summary>
		protected override bool todHalfDay(DateTime sessionDate) { return _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(sessionDate).RthCloseEt.HasValue; }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			EventConfig.enabled = true;              // only to tag 08:30-report days (reported as a separate stratum)
			EventConfig.classes = EEventClassMask.Macro;
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			TodMetrics = ETodMetric.Volume;
			TodSessions = Cfg.ssbSessions;
			TodMinSessions = Cfg.ssbSessions;        // no SSB until 20 completed sessions
			TodBucketMinutes = 0;                    // 0 = Data1 bar size (30)
			m_clock = null;
			base.StartCalc();
			m_history.Clear(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; Rv30State = ERvState.Unknown; Rv30 = double.NaN;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();                          // steps M-TOD first: the pool below never holds today
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime t = Bars.Time[0], day = tradingDayKey(t);
			if (day != m_day) { m_day = day; Rv30State = ERvState.Unknown; Rv30 = double.NaN; }
			if (Clock.ToEastern(t).TimeOfDay != Cfg.slotEnd) return;
			if (Bars.Info.Resolution.Size != 30) throw new ArgumentException("_Ex04OpeningRvGate: Data1 must be 30-minute bars");

			_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day);
			if (cme.NoRth || cme.RthCloseEt.HasValue) { journal(day, "skip half-day/no-RTH", double.NaN, double.NaN); return; }

			IList<double> pool = TodNormaliser.VolumePool(todMinuteOfSession(t));
			if (pool == null || pool.Count < Cfg.ssbSessions) { journal(day, "SSB warm-up", double.NaN, double.NaN); return; }
			double ssb = pool.Skip(pool.Count - Cfg.ssbSessions).Average();   // mean of the last 20 completed sessions' 09:30-10:00 volume
			if (!(ssb > 0)) return;
			double rv = Bars.Volume[0] / ssb;
			Rv30 = rv;

			if (m_history.Count >= Cfg.pctSessions)
			{
				var w = m_history.Skip(m_history.Count - Cfg.pctSessions).OrderBy(x => x).ToList();   // prior 250 only (causal)
				double hi = nearestRank(w, Cfg.qHi), lo = nearestRank(w, Cfg.qLo);
				Rv30State = rv >= hi ? ERvState.High : rv <= lo ? ERvState.Low : ERvState.Mid;
				journal(day, Rv30State.ToString(), hi, lo);
			}
			else journal(day, "percentile warm-up " + m_history.Count + "/" + Cfg.pctSessions, double.NaN, double.NaN);
			m_history.Add(rv);
			if (m_history.Count > Cfg.pctSessions + 5) m_history.RemoveAt(0);
		}

		/// <summary>Nearest-rank quantile of a sorted list (the catalogue does not fix the quantile method: an assumption).</summary>
		private static double nearestRank(List<double> sorted, double q)
		{
			int k = (int)Math.Ceiling(q * sorted.Count) - 1;
			return sorted[Math.Max(0, Math.Min(sorted.Count - 1, k))];
		}

		private void journal(DateTime day, string state, double hi, double lo)
		{
			if (!journalOn()) return;
			string rep = "n/a";
			_EventCalendar ev = EventCalendar;
			if (ev != null && !ev.Disabled)
			{
				EEventKind hole;
				if (ev.CoverageBlocks(day, EEventClassMask.Macro, EEventTier.None, out hole)) rep = "unknown(" + hole + ")";
				else rep = ev.EventsOn(day, EEventClassMask.Macro).Any(r => r.etTime.TimeOfDay == EventRules.T0830) ? "yes" : "no";
			}
			trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-50 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} RV30({Rv30:F3}) qHi({hi:F3}) qLo({lo:F3}) state({state}) report0830({rep})");
		}
	}
}
```

**Compile status:** compiled — type-checked with Roslyn against the repo sources (`origin/main` a07257a) plus MC API stubs (stubs not authoritative). Not run in MultiCharts; no backtest results.

### 3.12 Example 5 (`_Ex05TwoTimeframeTurn`) — A2-57 60m turn + first 12m oversold (medium)

**Features (direct).** F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-JRNL (§5.13), F-EVT (§5.14), M-MTF (§5.17).


**Catalogue rules (A2-57, base configuration).** Trend: EMA(13) of 60m closes (closed bars, 24h). Up-turn = the EMA slope changes from ≤ 0 to > 0. Oscillator: raw %K(3) on 12m. Entry: the **first** 12m bar after the up-turn with %K(3) ≤ 20, then a buy stop at that bar's high + 1 tick, valid for the next two 12m bars; RTH 09:45–15:00 only; one trade per up-turn. Stop: the lower of the signal-bar low and the prior 12m low − 1 tick; break-even at +1R; then trail to protect 50% of the peak open profit. Exit: the 60m slope turns ≤ 0, the trail, or 15:55.

**Chart.** Data1 = @ES 1m, Data2 = @ES 12m, Data3 = @ES 60m (`HtfStreams = {2, 3}`), dxFeed unadjusted, 24h Globex template.

**Walk-through.**
- Each new closed 60m bar updates the EMA and its slope. An up-turn arms the "first oversold" search.
- Each new closed 12m bar computes %K(3). The first one at ≤ 20 inside 09:45–15:00 becomes the signal bar. An oversold bar outside that window **forfeits** the up-turn.
- The buy stop is sent on every 1m bar until two 12m bars have closed after the signal bar.
- The structural stop is min(signal-bar low, prior 12m low) − 1 tick. If trigger − stop is under the 14-tick cost floor (`costFloorTicks`), the stop is widened to trigger − 14 ticks. If the widened stop breaches the per-trade risk budget (`riskBudgetFraction` = 0.01, 1% of equity, decided by Ettienne 2026-10-23; PF-04's 0.5% cap is not the default for the floor rule), the signal is skipped and journaled, and that up-turn is used up. Size is never shrunk.
- In the trade, the stop moves to break-even at +1R, then to entry + 50% of the peak open profit. The position exits on a 60m slope ≤ 0 or at 15:55 (`RthCloseEt` − 5 min on an early-close day, `flatAt`).
- RollDetection is Series. `rollOn(1)` clears a pending trigger, so a buy stop never spans the splice. It also **re-seeds the 60m EMA**: `m_ema`, `m_slope`, `m_ema60Count` and `m_up` are reset and a journal line is written. No slope turn counts until 3 × 13 new closed 60m bars, so the roll gap can't make a false turn.

**Pitfalls.**
- **Assumptions (labelled):** the EMA warm-up (3 × 13 closed 60m bars before the first turn counts), and the reading that an oversold bar outside 09:45–15:00 uses up "the first OS".
- The protective stop is sent from the bar after the fill. On a 1m chart the fill bar itself is unprotected for that minute.
- **Rolls.** Without the re-seed, the first EMA updates on the new contract include the roll gap, and an up-turn in the slope can come from the splice alone. **Coding choice (labelled):** re-seed rather than ignore turns for 3 × 13 bars. The two differ only in whether the warm-up uses old-contract closes. During the warm-up the slope exit is off too, which doesn't matter while the position is flat by 15:55 before a session-start splice.
- Run MC Probe P2 (HTF bar-end stamping) first, as the catalogue says (§4.12 VERIFY).

**Full strategy** — `_Ex05TwoTimeframeTurn.Strategy.CS`:

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 5 - A2-57 60m EMA(13) slope up-turn + FIRST 12m %K(3) <= 20, buy stop above the signal bar (long arm, base config).
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m, Data2 = @ES 12m, Data3 = @ES 60m (same symbol and 24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex05TwoTimeframeTurn : _CustomSignal
	{
		protected sealed class A257Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1;
			public int emaLength = 13, stochLength = 3, validBars = 2;
			public double osLevel = 20;
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), to = new TimeSpan(15, 0, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public TimeSpan halfDayExitBefore = new TimeSpan(0, 5, 0);   // early-close days: flat at RthCloseEt - 5 min
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;  // catalogue 2.1: ES cost floor; stop distance = max(structural, floor), size never shrunk
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;  // floor-rule risk budget: Ettienne, 2026-10-23: 1% of equity (PF-04's 0.5% is not the default here)
			public double trailKeep = 0.5;   // after +1R: protect 50% of the peak open profit
		}
		protected A257Config Cfg { get; } = new A257Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leStp, m_lxStp;
		private IOrderMarket m_lx;
		private _CmeSessionClock m_clock;
		private DateTime m_last12 = DateTime.MinValue, m_last60 = DateTime.MinValue;
		private double m_ema = double.NaN, m_slope = double.NaN;
		private int m_ema60Count;
		private bool m_up, m_osTaken;          // 60m state; the first OS after this up-turn was already seen
		private double m_trigger = double.NaN, m_planStop = double.NaN; private int m_validLeft;
		private double m_stop = double.NaN, m_entry = double.NaN, m_risk = double.NaN, m_peak = double.NaN;
		private bool m_slopeDown;

		/// <summary>Catalogue 2.1 / App F #9 (resolved): true when Cfg.contracts at this stop distance risk more than the per-trade budget.</summary>
		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }
		public _Ex05TwoTimeframeTurn(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		private _CmeSessionClock Clock { get { if (m_clock == null) m_clock = new _CmeSessionClock(resolveBarTimeZone(Bars.Info)); return m_clock; } }
		protected override DateTime tradingDayKey(DateTime t) { return Clock.GlobexDate(t); }
		protected override TimeSpan sessionClock(DateTime t) { return Clock.ToEastern(t).TimeOfDay; }
		protected override bool isRthBar(DateTime t) { return Clock.IsRthValueBarEt(Clock.ToEastern(t)); }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;   // unadjusted series: rollOn(1) below needs detection on (default is Off)
			m_leStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "A257LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A257LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A257LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			HtfStreams = new[] { 2, 3 };
			m_clock = null;
			base.StartCalc();
			m_last12 = m_last60 = DateTime.MinValue; m_ema = m_slope = double.NaN; m_ema60Count = 0;
			m_up = m_osTaken = m_slopeDown = false; m_trigger = m_planStop = double.NaN; m_validLeft = 0;
			m_stop = m_entry = m_risk = m_peak = double.NaN;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			TimeSpan tod = Clock.ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
			TimeSpan flat = flatAt(tradingDayKey(Bars.Time[0]));
			bool rthWindow = tod >= Cfg.from && tod < Cfg.to && tod < flat;

			if (rollOn(1))
			{
				m_trigger = double.NaN; m_validLeft = 0;   // never carry a level across the splice
				// re-seed the 60m EMA from the new contract: no slope turn counts until 3 x emaLength new closed 60m bars
				m_ema = m_slope = double.NaN; m_ema60Count = 0; m_up = false;
				if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-57 roll {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm}: 60m EMA re-seeded");
			}
			step60();
			bool new12 = step12(rthWindow);

			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos > 0) { manage(tod, flat); return; }
			m_stop = m_entry = m_risk = m_peak = double.NaN;
			if (new12 && m_validLeft > 0) m_validLeft--;                    // "valid for the next 2 12m bars"
			if (m_validLeft > 0 && rthWindow && m_up && !double.IsNaN(m_trigger)) m_leStp.Send(m_trigger, Cfg.contracts);
		}

		/// <summary>New CLOSED 60m bar: update EMA(13) and the slope state.</summary>
		private void step60()
		{
			DateTime t; double o, h, l, c, v;
			HtfClosedBar(3, 0, out t, out o, out h, out l, out c, out v);
			if (t == m_last60) return;
			m_last60 = t;
			double prevEma = m_ema, prevSlope = m_slope, a = 2.0 / (Cfg.emaLength + 1);
			m_ema = double.IsNaN(m_ema) ? c : m_ema + a * (c - m_ema);
			m_ema60Count++;
			if (double.IsNaN(prevEma) || m_ema60Count < 3 * Cfg.emaLength) return;   // EMA warm-up (3 x length: an assumption)
			m_slope = m_ema - prevEma;
			bool turnUp = !double.IsNaN(prevSlope) && prevSlope <= 0 && m_slope > 0;
			if (turnUp) { m_up = true; m_osTaken = false; if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-57 60m up-turn {t:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} ema({m_ema:F2})"); }
			if (m_slope <= 0) { m_up = false; m_slopeDown = true; m_trigger = double.NaN; m_validLeft = 0; } else m_slopeDown = false;
		}

		/// <summary>New CLOSED 12m bar: the first %K(3) &lt;= 20 after the up-turn arms the buy stop. Returns true on a new 12m bar.</summary>
		private bool step12(bool rthWindow)
		{
			DateTime t; double o, h, l, c, v;
			HtfClosedBar(2, 0, out t, out o, out h, out l, out c, out v);
			if (t == m_last12) return false;
			m_last12 = t;
			if (!m_up || m_osTaken || BarsOfData(2).CurrentBar <= Cfg.stochLength + 1) return true;
			double hh = double.MinValue, ll = double.MaxValue, prevLow = double.NaN;
			for (int k = 0; k < Cfg.stochLength; k++)
			{
				DateTime tk; double ok, hk, lk, ck, vk;
				HtfClosedBar(2, k, out tk, out ok, out hk, out lk, out ck, out vk);
				hh = Math.Max(hh, hk); ll = Math.Min(ll, lk);
				if (k == 1) prevLow = lk;
			}
			double pctK = hh > ll ? 100.0 * (c - ll) / (hh - ll) : 50.0;
			if (pctK > Cfg.osLevel) return true;
			m_osTaken = true;                       // FIRST oversold only: later ones are ignored (the key rule)
			if (!rthWindow) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-57 first OS {t:HH:mm} outside 09:45-15:00: turn forfeited"); return true; }
			double tick = tickSize();
			double trigger = h + tick, planStop = Math.Min(l, double.IsNaN(prevLow) ? l : prevLow) - tick;
			if (trigger - planStop < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) planStop = trigger - Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick;   // catalogue 2.1: widen to the cost floor
			if (overRiskBudget(trigger - planStop))   // widened stop breaches the per-trade risk budget: skipped and logged, not resized (this up-turn is used up)
			{
				if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-57 {t:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} skip: stop {(trigger - planStop) / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget {Cfg.riskBudgetFraction:P2}");
				return true;
			}
			m_trigger = trigger; m_planStop = planStop;
			m_validLeft = Cfg.validBars + 1;        // decremented on this bar's new12 pass, leaving 2 full 12m bars
			if (journalOn())
			{
				var ctx = new Dictionary<string, string> { { "pctK", pctK.ToString("F1") }, { "ema60", m_ema.ToString("F2") } };
				journalArm("A257LE", m_trigger, m_planStop, double.NaN, ctx);
				logSignal("A2-57", +1, m_trigger, m_planStop, ctx, true);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void manage(TimeSpan tod, TimeSpan flat)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(m_entry))
			{
				m_entry = StrategyInfo.AvgEntryPrice; m_stop = m_planStop; m_risk = m_entry - m_stop; m_peak = m_entry;
				m_trigger = double.NaN; m_validLeft = 0;   // one trade per up-turn
			}
			if ((tod >= flat && tod < new TimeSpan(17, 0, 0)) || m_slopeDown) { m_lx.Send(); return; }
			m_peak = Math.Max(m_peak, Bars.High[0]);
			if (m_risk > 0 && m_peak - m_entry >= m_risk)
			{
				double trail = m_entry + Cfg.trailKeep * (m_peak - m_entry);   // >= break-even once +1R is reached
				m_stop = Math.Max(m_stop, Math.Max(m_entry, trail));
			}
			m_lxStp.Send(m_stop);
		}

		/// <summary>Flat time for a Globex day: Cfg.flat, or on a CME early-close day RthCloseEt - halfDayExitBefore if earlier
		/// (_CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day).RthCloseEt; same rule as A263Config.halfDayExitBefore).</summary>
		private TimeSpan flatAt(DateTime day)
		{
			_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day);
			TimeSpan f = Cfg.flat;
			if (cme.RthCloseEt.HasValue && cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore < f) f = cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore;
			return f;
		}
	}
}
```

**Compile status:** compiled — type-checked with Roslyn against the repo sources (`origin/main` a07257a) plus MC API stubs (stubs not authoritative). Not run in MultiCharts; no backtest results.

### 3.13 Example 9 (`_Ex09OpenAtrBreakout`) — A2-63 open + k × ATR20d breakout with RegimeGate (medium)

**Features (direct).** F-JRNL (§5.13), M-DAILY (§5.19), _RegimeEngine (§5.30).


**Catalogue rules (A2-63, core).** Buy stop at O + k × ATR20d, O = the 09:30 ET RTH open; active 09:30–15:30; one entry per day; exit 15:55. Core: k 0.5, 30m bars, ATR 20. The G-R consumer `_RegimeGate` (C1) adds arms U (ungated), G and A, pre-registered in `specs/regime-consumer-prereg.md`; its test has **not** been run.

**Chart.** Data1 = @ES 30m (the book's size) or 1m, dxFeed unadjusted, 24h Globex template. `[Input] GateArm` 0 = U, 1 = G, 2 = A.

**Walk-through.** This is `_Test6` cut down to show the API; `_Test6` stays the pre-registered harness.
- `StartCalc` validates the arm, then calls `RegimeGate.ApplyArm(GateCfg, RegimeConfig)` **before** `base.StartCalc()`, because the engines are built there. It sets `NeedsDaily` and `DailyConfig.atrLength`.
- `AtrTercileTracker` gets one value per completed day (`daily.PrevDate`, `daily.Atr20d`).
- On the latch bar (`A263Core.IsLatchBar`, the first bar closing after 09:30 ET) it reads ATR and as-of date, asks `RegimeGate.Decide(arm, Regime, day, tercile)` and latches the open, threshold and gate decision in `A263Core.Latch`.
- With TraceLevel Journal it writes `RegimeGate.Line(...)` plus the core's suffix, and `journalArm` with `RegimeGate.TradeFields` when an allowed signal exists.
- `A263Core.Step` returns the stop price to send or the exit.

**Pitfalls.**
- Like `_Test6`, this example is a plain `_CustomSignal` that converts time with `ToZone(..., "Eastern Standard Time")`. Unlike `_Test6` (Globex key since PR #46) it does not override `tradingDayKey`, so M-DAILY days are calendar days on a Globex chart (**VERIFY**, §1.6 and Appendix F).
- RollDetection is Off here: the roll gap enters the daily ATR. With it on and the default `atrRestartOnRoll = true`, `Atr20d` is NaN for 20 sessions after each roll (§4.3). `_Test6` itself switched in PR #46 to `Table` detection with `atrRestartOnRoll = false` (ATR carried on, splice-day TR skipped), which is the closest match to the catalogue's "Atr20d, roll-gap removed"; copy those two `StartCalc` lines (§4.3) to match it. See Appendix F.
- Change the gate config only before `base.StartCalc()`; the hash (`RegimeGate.Hash`) records it.
- **Not coded:** the host H2 "disaster-stop-at-the-open" arm named in A2-63's gate line (a disaster stop at O). `A263Config` has no disaster stop, so the core has no stop and the cost floor doesn't apply.

**Full strategy** — `_Ex09OpenAtrBreakout.Strategy.CS`:

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 9 - A2-63 long-only open + k x ATR20d breakout, exit 15:55 ET, with the _RegimeGate arm switch (G-R consumer C1).
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 30m (book) or 1m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED, 24h CME Globex template.
// The pre-registered harness is _Test6; this example is the same wiring cut down to show the API (A263Core does the rules).
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex09OpenAtrBreakout : _CustomSignal
	{
		[Input] public int GateArm { get; set; }      // 0 = U (ungated), 1 = G, 2 = A (see RegimeGate pre-registration)

		protected RegimeGateConfig GateCfg { get; } = new RegimeGateConfig();
		protected A263Config HostCfg { get; } = new A263Config();   // k 0.5, ATR 20, 09:30-15:30 active, exit 15:55

		private IOrderPriced m_le; private IOrderMarket m_lx;
		private A263Core m_core; private AtrTercileTracker m_terc;
		private string m_hash;

		public _Ex09OpenAtrBreakout(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { GateArm = 0; }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			m_le = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "A263LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A263LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			if (GateArm < 0 || GateArm > 2) throw new ArgumentException("GateArm must be 0, 1 or 2");
			GateCfg.arm = (ERegimeGateArm)GateArm;
			string err = GateCfg.Validate(); if (err != null) throw new ArgumentException(err);
			RegimeGate.ApplyArm(GateCfg, RegimeConfig);   // BEFORE base.StartCalc: the engines are built there
			NeedsDaily = true;                            // M-DAILY, lazily created by the base
			DailyConfig.atrLength = HostCfg.atrLength;
			base.StartCalc();
			m_core = new A263Core(HostCfg);
			m_terc = new AtrTercileTracker(RegimeConfig.atrPctLookback, RegimeConfig.K);
			m_hash = RegimeGate.Hash(RegimeConfig, GateCfg, HostCfg);
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime et = ToZone(Bars.Time[0], "Eastern Standard Time");
			_DailyContext daily = DailyContext;
			if (daily != null) m_terc.AddDay(daily.PrevDate, daily.Atr20d);
			bool isLong = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition > 0;

			if (m_core.IsLatchBar(et))   // first bar closing after 09:30 ET: O = its open
			{
				double atr = daily != null ? daily.Atr20d : double.NaN;      // RollDetection is Off here (_Test6 uses Table + atrRestartOnRoll = false since PR #46): roll gaps enter the ATR
				DateTime asOf = daily != null ? daily.PrevDate : DateTime.MinValue;
				_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(et.Date);
				RegimeGateDecision d = RegimeGate.Decide(GateCfg.arm, Regime, et.Date, m_terc.LastDate == asOf ? m_terc.Tercile : -1);
				m_core.Latch(et, Bars.Open[0], atr, asOf, d.allow, cme.RthCloseEt, cme.NoRth);
				if (journalOn())
				{
					trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, RegimeGate.Line(d, m_core.HasSignal, m_hash, GateCfg.placeboSeed) + m_core.Suffix());
					if (d.allow && m_core.HasSignal) journalArm("A263LE", m_core.Threshold, double.NaN, double.NaN, RegimeGate.TradeFields(d, m_hash, GateCfg.placeboSeed));
				}
			}

			A263Action a = m_core.Step(et, isLong);
			if (a.exit) m_lx.Send();
			else if (a.sendStop) m_le.Send(a.stop, HostCfg.contracts);
		}
	}
}
```
