- **v4 note.** This v3 example still exposes `[Input]`s (house rule: protected config); kept unchanged for continuity.

### 3.14 Example 11 (`_Ex11OvernightBreak`) — PS-24 overnight high/low break-and-go at RTH (core arm: 2 closed 5m closes beyond the ON extreme, market entry) (medium)

**Features (direct).** F-PROF (§5.2), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-JRNL (§5.13), F-EVT (§5.14), M-MTF (§5.17).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> PS-24 overnight high/low break-and-go at RTH (core arm: 2 closed 5m closes beyond the ON extreme, market entry).
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m, Data2 = @ES 5m (same symbol, 24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. IBKR = broker only.
> Features: F-SESS (Session.ethHigh/ethLow, rthOpen), F-PROF V-score (ValueMigrationScore), F-TM, F-JRNL, roll guard, floor rule.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 11 - PS-24 overnight high/low break-and-go at RTH (core arm: 2 closed 5m closes beyond the ON extreme, market entry).
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m, Data2 = @ES 5m (same symbol, 24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. IBKR = broker only.
// Features: F-SESS (Session.ethHigh/ethLow, rthOpen), F-PROF V-score (ValueMigrationScore), F-TM, F-JRNL, roll guard, floor rule.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex11OvernightBreak : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Ps24Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1;
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), to = new TimeSpan(11, 30, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public TimeSpan halfDayExitBefore = new TimeSpan(0, 5, 0);
			public double minOutsideAtr = 0.5;     // the ON extreme must be >= 0.5 x ATR5 outside the RTH open
			public int deltaTicks = 1;             // delta: closes must be beyond the extreme by this many ticks
			public int closesNeeded = 2;
			public double stopAtr = 0.5;           // stop 0.5 x ATR5 back inside the ON extreme
			public double targetR = 1.0;           // b (catalogue sweep {0.75, 1.5})
			public int atrLength = 14;
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;        // catalogue 2.1 cost floor; stop = max(structural, floor), size never shrunk
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Ps24Config Cfg { get; } = new Ps24Config();

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_se, m_lx, m_sx;
		private IOrderPriced m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private DateTime m_last5 = DateTime.MinValue, m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private int m_runUp, m_runDn; private bool m_tradedToday;
		private double m_stop = double.NaN, m_target = double.NaN;

		public _Ex11OvernightBreak(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS24LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_se = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS24SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS24LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS24SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS24LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS24SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS24LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS24ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			HtfStreams = new[] { 2 };
			base.StartCalc();
			m_last5 = m_day = DateTime.MinValue; m_runUp = m_runDn = 0; m_tradedToday = false;
			m_stop = m_target = double.NaN;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) { m_day = day; m_runUp = m_runDn = 0; m_tradedToday = false; }
			if (rollOn(1)) { m_runUp = m_runDn = 0; m_tradedToday = true; }   // no ON level on the splice day
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay, flat = flatAt(day);
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				if (tod >= flat && tod < new TimeSpan(17, 0, 0)) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); } else { m_sxStp.Send(m_stop); m_sxLmt.Send(m_target); }
				return;
			}

			DateTime t5; double o5, h5, l5, c5, v5;
			HtfClosedBar(2, 0, out t5, out o5, out h5, out l5, out c5, out v5);
			if (t5 == m_last5) return;
			m_last5 = t5;
			SessionStats s = Session;
			if (s == null || s.ethHigh <= 0 || s.ethLow <= 0 || s.rthOpen <= 0 || m_tradedToday) return;
			if (tod < Cfg.from || tod >= Cfg.to || tod >= flat) { m_runUp = m_runDn = 0; return; }
			double tick = tickSize(), atr = atr5();
			if (double.IsNaN(atr)) return;
			m_runUp = c5 > s.ethHigh + Cfg.deltaTicks * tick ? m_runUp + 1 : 0;
			m_runDn = c5 < s.ethLow - Cfg.deltaTicks * tick ? m_runDn + 1 : 0;
			int side = m_runUp >= Cfg.closesNeeded ? +1 : m_runDn >= Cfg.closesNeeded ? -1 : 0;
			if (side == 0) return;
			double level = side > 0 ? s.ethHigh : s.ethLow;
			if (side * (level - s.rthOpen) < Cfg.minOutsideAtr * atr) { m_runUp = m_runDn = 0; return; }   // extreme too close to the open
			int? vs = ValueMigrationScore(1);
			if (!vs.HasValue || side * vs.Value < 0)                                                          // V-score opposed or unknown
			{
				if (journalOn()) logSignal("PS-24", side, level, double.NaN, new Dictionary<string, string> { { "vscore", vs.HasValue ? vs.Value.ToString() : "na" } }, false);
				m_runUp = m_runDn = 0; return;
			}
			double entry = Bars.Close[0], stop = level - side * Cfg.stopAtr * atr;
			if (side * (entry - stop) < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) stop = entry - side * Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick;   // catalogue 2.1 floor
			double r = Math.Abs(entry - stop);
			if (overRiskBudget(r)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-24 {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} skip: stop {r / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget {Cfg.riskBudgetFraction:P2}"); m_tradedToday = true; return; }
			m_stop = stop; m_target = entry + side * Cfg.targetR * r; m_tradedToday = true;
			if (side > 0) m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts); else m_se.Send(Cfg.contracts);
			if (journalOn())
			{
				var ctx = new Dictionary<string, string> { { "on", level.ToString("F2") }, { "atr5", atr.ToString("F2") }, { "vscore", vs.Value.ToString() } };
				journalArm(side > 0 ? "PS24LE" : "PS24SE", entry, m_stop, m_target, ctx);
				logSignal("PS-24", side, entry, m_stop, ctx, true);
			}
		}

		/// <summary>Mean true range of the last atrLength CLOSED 5m bars (simple mean, not Wilder), NaN in warm-up.</summary>
		private double atr5()
		{
			int n = Cfg.atrLength;
			if (BarsOfData(2).CurrentBar < n + 2) return double.NaN;
			double sum = 0;
			for (int k = 0; k < n; k++)
			{
				DateTime t; double o, h, l, c, v, o1, h1, l1, pc, v1;
				HtfClosedBar(2, k, out t, out o, out h, out l, out c, out v);
				HtfClosedBar(2, k + 1, out t, out o1, out h1, out l1, out pc, out v1);
				sum += Math.Max(h, pc) - Math.Min(l, pc);
			}
			return sum / n;
		}

		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }

		private TimeSpan flatAt(DateTime day)
		{
			_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day);
			TimeSpan f = Cfg.flat;
			if (cme.RthCloseEt.HasValue && cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore < f) f = cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore;
			return f;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.15 Example 15 (`_Ex15NarrowIbBreak`) — PS-08 narrow initial balance breakout (core arm). IB width <= 33rd percentile of the last 20 sessions; (medium)

**Features (direct).** F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-JRNL (§5.13), F-EVT (§5.14).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> PS-08 narrow initial balance breakout (core arm). IB width <= 33rd percentile of the last 20 sessions;
> after 10:30 ET, 2 consecutive closed 5m bars beyond IB +/- delta -> market; stop back inside by 0.25 x IB (floored); target 1 x IB beyond.
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. IbMinutes = 60 (default).
> Features: F-SESS (Session.ibHigh/ibLow/ibComplete, IbRange), F-CAL half-days, F-JRNL, roll guard, floor rule.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 15 - PS-08 narrow initial balance breakout (core arm). IB width <= 33rd percentile of the last 20 sessions;
// after 10:30 ET, 2 consecutive closed 5m bars beyond IB +/- delta -> market; stop back inside by 0.25 x IB (floored); target 1 x IB beyond.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. IbMinutes = 60 (default).
// Features: F-SESS (Session.ibHigh/ibLow/ibComplete, IbRange), F-CAL half-days, F-JRNL, roll guard, floor rule.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex15NarrowIbBreak : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Ps08Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1;
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(10, 30, 0), to = new TimeSpan(15, 0, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public TimeSpan halfDayExitBefore = new TimeSpan(0, 5, 0);
			public int lookback = 20;
			public double maxPercentile = 0.33;
			public int deltaTicks = 1, closesNeeded = 2;
			public double stopInsideIb = 0.25, targetIb = 1.0;
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Ps08Config Cfg { get; } = new Ps08Config();

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_se, m_lx, m_sx;
		private IOrderPriced m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private readonly List<double> m_ibHist = new List<double>();   // completed IB widths, oldest first
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private bool m_ibLogged, m_narrow, m_done;
		private int m_up, m_dn;
		private double m_stop = double.NaN, m_target = double.NaN;

		public _Ex15NarrowIbBreak(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS08LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_se = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS08SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS08LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS08SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS08LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS08SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS08LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS08ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			base.StartCalc();
			m_ibHist.Clear(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; m_ibLogged = m_narrow = m_done = false; m_up = m_dn = 0;
			m_stop = m_target = double.NaN;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) { m_day = day; m_ibLogged = m_narrow = m_done = false; m_up = m_dn = 0; }
			if (rollOn(1)) m_done = true;
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay, flat = flatAt(day);
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				if (tod >= flat && tod < new TimeSpan(17, 0, 0)) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); } else { m_sxStp.Send(m_stop); m_sxLmt.Send(m_target); }
				return;
			}
			SessionStats s = Session;
			if (s == null || !s.ibComplete) return;
			double ib = s.IbRange;
			if (!m_ibLogged)                                      // once per session, the first bar after the IB completes
			{
				m_ibLogged = true;
				m_narrow = m_ibHist.Count >= Cfg.lookback && percentileRank(ib) <= Cfg.maxPercentile;
				m_ibHist.Add(ib); if (m_ibHist.Count > Cfg.lookback) m_ibHist.RemoveAt(0);
				if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-08 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} IB {s.ibLow:F2}-{s.ibHigh:F2} width {ib:F2} narrow({m_narrow})");
			}
			if (!m_narrow || m_done || tod < Cfg.from || tod >= Cfg.to || tod >= flat) return;
			double tick = tickSize();
			m_up = Bars.Close[0] > s.ibHigh + Cfg.deltaTicks * tick ? m_up + 1 : 0;
			m_dn = Bars.Close[0] < s.ibLow - Cfg.deltaTicks * tick ? m_dn + 1 : 0;
			int side = m_up >= Cfg.closesNeeded ? +1 : m_dn >= Cfg.closesNeeded ? -1 : 0;
			if (side == 0) return;
			m_done = true;                                        // one attempt per session
			string why;
			if (!hostGate(side, out why)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-08 {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} gate blocked side {side}: {why}"); return; }
			double edge = side > 0 ? s.ibHigh : s.ibLow, entry = Bars.Close[0];
			double stop = edge - side * Cfg.stopInsideIb * ib;
			if (side * (entry - stop) < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) stop = entry - side * Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick;
			double r = Math.Abs(entry - stop);
			if (overRiskBudget(r)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-08 {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} skip: stop {r / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget {Cfg.riskBudgetFraction:P2}"); return; }
			m_stop = stop; m_target = edge + side * Cfg.targetIb * ib;
			if (side > 0) m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts); else m_se.Send(Cfg.contracts);
			if (journalOn())
			{
				var ctx = new Dictionary<string, string> { { "ib", ib.ToString("F2") }, { "edge", edge.ToString("F2") } };
				journalArm(side > 0 ? "PS08LE" : "PS08SE", entry, m_stop, m_target, ctx);
				logSignal("PS-08", side, entry, m_stop, ctx, true);
			}
		}

		/// <summary>Fraction of the stored IB widths that are &lt;= w.</summary>
		/// <summary>Extra entry gate for derived strategies (example 21 / MT-03). true = allowed.</summary>
		protected virtual bool hostGate(int side, out string why) { why = null; return true; }

		private double percentileRank(double w)
		{
			int le = 0; foreach (double x in m_ibHist) if (x <= w) le++;
			return m_ibHist.Count == 0 ? 1.0 : (double)le / m_ibHist.Count;
		}

		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }

		private TimeSpan flatAt(DateTime day)
		{
			_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day);
			TimeSpan f = Cfg.flat;
			if (cme.RthCloseEt.HasValue && cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore < f) f = cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore;
			return f;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.16 Example 19 (`_Ex19OpenDrive`) — PS-07 open drive, continuation on the first higher low (core arm) (medium)

**Features (direct).** F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-JRNL (§5.13), M-SWING (§5.16), M-TOD (§5.18).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> PS-07 open drive, continuation on the first higher low (core arm).
> Drive test (operationalised): the 09:30-09:45 range >= driveMult x the sum of the three same-slot TOD Range medians, and the
> 09:45 close in the outer third of that range in the drive direction. Entry: after the first PA pivot low labelled HL (long) / pivot
> high labelled LH (short) that confirms before 11:30, a stop order 1 tick beyond the confirming bar. Stop: the pivot -/+ 1 tick, floored.
> Exit: 11:30 time stop. One trade per day.
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
> Features: M-TOD (TodMetrics = Range, Median, Valid), M-PA (PriceActionConfig, NewPivotLow/High, LastLow/LastHigh, EPivotLabel), F-SESS.

**Pitfalls.**

- Contains PLACEHOLDER values (marked in the code); calibrate them before reading any result.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 19 - PS-07 open drive, continuation on the first higher low (core arm).
// Drive test (operationalised): the 09:30-09:45 range >= driveMult x the sum of the three same-slot TOD Range medians, and the
// 09:45 close in the outer third of that range in the drive direction. Entry: after the first PA pivot low labelled HL (long) / pivot
// high labelled LH (short) that confirms before 11:30, a stop order 1 tick beyond the confirming bar. Stop: the pivot -/+ 1 tick, floored.
// Exit: 11:30 time stop. One trade per day.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
// Features: M-TOD (TodMetrics = Range, Median, Valid), M-PA (PriceActionConfig, NewPivotLow/High, LastLow/LastHigh, EPivotLabel), F-SESS.
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex19OpenDrive : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Ps07Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1;
			public double driveMult = 0.6;                  // PLACEHOLDER threshold, not a tested value
			public TimeSpan driveEnd = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), timeStop = new TimeSpan(11, 30, 0);
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Ps07Config Cfg { get; } = new Ps07Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leStp, m_seStp, m_lxStp, m_sxStp;
		private IOrderMarket m_lx, m_sx;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private double m_hi, m_lo, m_medSum, m_trigger = double.NaN, m_stop = double.NaN;
		private int m_slots, m_side;
		private bool m_done;

		public _Ex19OpenDrive(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			TodMetrics = ETodMetric.Range; TodSessions = 20; TodMinSessions = 10; TodBucketMinutes = 5;
			PriceActionConfig.enabled = true;               // Futures preset applied by the base
			PriceActionConfig.dataStream = 1;
			m_leStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS07LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_seStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS07SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS07LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS07SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS07LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS07SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			base.StartCalc();
			m_day = DateTime.MinValue; m_side = 0; m_done = false; m_trigger = m_stop = double.NaN;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) { m_day = day; m_hi = double.MinValue; m_lo = double.MaxValue; m_medSum = 0; m_slots = 0; m_side = 0; m_done = false; m_trigger = double.NaN; }
			if (rollOn(1)) m_done = true;
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			double tick = tickSize();
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				m_done = true;                                        // one trade per day: never re-arm after this position
				if (tod >= Cfg.timeStop) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); else m_sxStp.Send(m_stop);
				return;
			}
			if (m_done || !IsRth()) return;
			if (tod > new TimeSpan(9, 30, 0) && tod <= Cfg.driveEnd)
			{
				m_hi = Math.Max(m_hi, Bars.High[0]); m_lo = Math.Min(m_lo, Bars.Low[0]);
				if (TodNormaliser != null && TodNormaliser.Valid(ETodMetric.Range)) { m_medSum += TodNormaliser.Median(ETodMetric.Range); m_slots++; }
				if (tod == Cfg.driveEnd) classifyDrive();
				return;
			}
			if (m_side == 0 || tod >= Cfg.timeStop) return;
			_PriceActionEngine pa = PriceActionEngine;
			if (pa == null || !pa.IsWarm) return;
			if (double.IsNaN(m_trigger))
			{
				_Pivot p = m_side > 0 ? (pa.NewPivotLow ? pa.LastLow(0) : null) : (pa.NewPivotHigh ? pa.LastHigh(0) : null);
				if (p == null || p.label != (m_side > 0 ? EPivotLabel.HL : EPivotLabel.LH)) return;
				m_trigger = m_side > 0 ? Bars.High[0] + tick : Bars.Low[0] - tick;
				m_stop = m_side > 0 ? p.price - tick : p.price + tick;
				if (m_side * (m_trigger - m_stop) < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) m_stop = m_trigger - m_side * Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick;
				double r = Math.Abs(m_trigger - m_stop);
				if (overRiskBudget(r)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-07 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} skip: stop {r / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget"); m_done = true; return; }
				if (journalOn()) journalArm(m_side > 0 ? "PS07LE" : "PS07SE", m_trigger, m_stop, double.NaN, null);
			}
			if (m_side > 0) m_leStp.Send(m_trigger, Cfg.contracts); else m_seStp.Send(m_trigger, Cfg.contracts);
		}


		private void classifyDrive()
		{
			if (m_slots < 3) return;                                  // TOD history not warm for all three slots
			double range = m_hi - m_lo, c = Bars.Close[0];
			if (!(range > 0) || range < Cfg.driveMult * m_medSum) return;
			if (c >= m_hi - range / 3) m_side = +1; else if (c <= m_lo + range / 3) m_side = -1;
			if (m_side != 0 && journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-07 {m_day:yyyy-MM-dd} drive {(m_side > 0 ? "up" : "down")} range {range:F2} vs sum-median {m_medSum:F2}");
		}

		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }
	}
}
```

### 3.17 Example 21 (`_Ex21CompositeValueGate`) — MT-03 composite-value gate on the example 15 host (PS-08 narrow-IB breakout) (medium)

**Features (direct).** F-DEV (§5.1), F-PROF (§5.2), F-COMP (§5.3). **Inherited from example 15:** F-ROLL, F-CAL, F-JRNL, F-EVT.

**What it does** (from the file header):

> MT-03 composite-value gate on the example 15 host (PS-08 narrow-IB breakout).
> Rule: long only if price >= the 20-session composite POC and the 20-session V-score >= 0; short mirrors (price <= POC, V-score <= 0).
> Chart: as example 15 (@ES 5m). The completed-session profiles come from the base's Own profiles (OwnProfileWindow = Rth), so the gate
> does not depend on the MC Volume Profile study settings.
> Features: F-VP (Composite, ValueMigrationScore, OwnProfileWindow, DevelopingSource), M-VSCORE.

**Pitfalls.**

- Module on example 15: compile both files; the host's rules, stops and settings apply unless overridden.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 21 - MT-03 composite-value gate on the example 15 host (PS-08 narrow-IB breakout).
// Rule: long only if price >= the 20-session composite POC and the 20-session V-score >= 0; short mirrors (price <= POC, V-score <= 0).
// Chart: as example 15 (@ES 5m). The completed-session profiles come from the base's Own profiles (OwnProfileWindow = Rth), so the gate
// does not depend on the MC Volume Profile study settings.
// Features: F-VP (Composite, ValueMigrationScore, OwnProfileWindow, DevelopingSource), M-VSCORE.
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex21CompositeValueGate : _Ex15NarrowIbBreak
	{
		private const int N = 20;
		public _Ex21CompositeValueGate(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			DevelopingSource = EDevelopingSource.Own;   // [Input] on the base: set in code, not in the Format dialog
			OwnProfileWindow = EOwnProfileWindow.Rth;
		}

		protected override bool hostGate(int side, out string why)
		{
			why = null;
			VpExtra comp = Composite(1, N);
			int? v = ValueMigrationScore(1, N);
			if (comp == null || !v.HasValue) { why = "composite/V-score not ready"; return false; }
			double px = Bars.Close[0];
			bool ok = side > 0 ? (px >= comp.poc && v.Value >= 0) : (px <= comp.poc && v.Value <= 0);
			if (!ok) why = $"px {px:F2} vs POC{N} {comp.poc:F2}, V{N} {v.Value}";
			return ok;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.18 Example 23 (`_Ex23FastInSlowFade`) — A2-58 fade the fast trend inside the slow trend (Kaufman), intraday 5m (medium)

**Features (direct).** F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> A2-58 fade the fast trend inside the slow trend (Kaufman), intraday 5m.
> Slow direction = sign(SMA_S[t] - SMA_S[t-1]); fast direction likewise with SMA_F. Long: slow up and fast turns from up to down on this
> closed bar, 09:45-15:00 ET; market next bar. Mirror short. Exit: slow direction turns, or 15:55. Disaster stop 2.0 x ATR(14, 5m), floored.
> S = 30, F = 3 (one point of the catalogue's 4 x 3 grid, not a tuned value).
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Indicators run on all bars (ETH included).
> Features: CME clock adapter on a plain _CustomSignal, F-TM (NoEntryBefore/After, FlatBy, entryAllowed, mustFlatten), averageTrueRange.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 23 - A2-58 fade the fast trend inside the slow trend (Kaufman), intraday 5m.
// Slow direction = sign(SMA_S[t] - SMA_S[t-1]); fast direction likewise with SMA_F. Long: slow up and fast turns from up to down on this
// closed bar, 09:45-15:00 ET; market next bar. Mirror short. Exit: slow direction turns, or 15:55. Disaster stop 2.0 x ATR(14, 5m), floored.
// S = 30, F = 3 (one point of the catalogue's 4 x 3 grid, not a tuned value).
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Indicators run on all bars (ETH included).
// Features: CME clock adapter on a plain _CustomSignal, F-TM (NoEntryBefore/After, FlatBy, entryAllowed, mustFlatten), averageTrueRange.
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex23FastInSlowFade : _CustomSignal
	{
		protected sealed class A258Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, slow = 30, fast = 3, atrLength = 14;
			public double stopAtr = 2.0;
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected A258Config Cfg { get; } = new A258Config();

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_se, m_lx, m_sx;
		private IOrderPriced m_lxStp, m_sxStp;
		private _CmeSessionClock m_clock;
		private double m_slowPrev = double.NaN, m_fastPrev = double.NaN, m_stop;
		private int m_slowDir, m_fastDir;

		public _Ex23FastInSlowFade(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		private _CmeSessionClock Clock { get { if (m_clock == null) m_clock = new _CmeSessionClock(resolveBarTimeZone(Bars.Info)); return m_clock; } }
		protected override DateTime tradingDayKey(DateTime t) { return Clock.GlobexDate(t); }
		protected override TimeSpan sessionClock(DateTime t) { return Clock.ToEastern(t).TimeOfDay; }
		protected override bool isRthBar(DateTime t) { return Clock.IsRthValueBarEt(Clock.ToEastern(t)); }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			NoEntryBefore = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0); NoEntryAfter = new TimeSpan(15, 0, 0); FlatBy = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "A258LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_se = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "A258SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A258LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A258SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A258LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A258SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			m_clock = null;
			base.StartCalc();
			m_slowPrev = m_fastPrev = double.NaN; m_slowDir = m_fastDir = 0;
		}

		private double sma(int n) { double s = 0; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) s += Bars.Close[i]; return s / n; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close || Bars.CurrentBar <= Cfg.slow) return;
			if (rollOn(1)) { m_slowPrev = m_fastPrev = double.NaN; m_slowDir = m_fastDir = 0; }   // directions restart; the SMAs still span the roll gap for S bars (pitfall: use a back-adjusted series if that matters)
			double sl = sma(Cfg.slow), fs = sma(Cfg.fast);
			int slowDir = double.IsNaN(m_slowPrev) ? 0 : Math.Sign(sl - m_slowPrev);
			int fastDir = double.IsNaN(m_fastPrev) ? 0 : Math.Sign(fs - m_fastPrev);
			int prevSlow = m_slowDir, prevFast = m_fastDir;
			m_slowPrev = sl; m_fastPrev = fs;
			if (slowDir != 0) m_slowDir = slowDir;
			if (fastDir != 0) m_fastDir = fastDir;

			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				if (mustFlatten() || m_slowDir != Math.Sign(pos)) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); else m_sxStp.Send(m_stop);
				return;
			}
			string why;
			if (!entryAllowed(out why) || !isRthBar(Bars.Time[0])) return;
			int side = 0;
			if (m_slowDir > 0 && prevFast > 0 && m_fastDir < 0 && prevSlow > 0) side = +1;
			else if (m_slowDir < 0 && prevFast < 0 && m_fastDir > 0 && prevSlow < 0) side = -1;
			if (side == 0) return;
			double atr = averageTrueRange(Bars, Cfg.atrLength), tick = tickSize(), c = Bars.Close[0];
			if (!(atr > 0)) return;
			double r = Math.Max(Cfg.stopAtr * atr, Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick);
			if (overRiskBudget(r)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-58 {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} skip: stop {r / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget"); return; }
			m_stop = c - side * r;                                    // approximate: from the signal close, not the fill
			if (side > 0) m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts); else m_se.Send(Cfg.contracts);
			if (journalOn()) journalArm(side > 0 ? "A258LE" : "A258SE", c, m_stop, double.NaN, null);
		}

		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }
	}
}
```

### 3.19 Example 24 (`_Ex24ExclusionGate`) — RG-07 event, roll and thin-session exclusions, applied as a gate on the example 18 host (A2-56) (medium)

**Features (direct).** F-CAL (§5.8), F-EVT (§5.14), M-EVENT (§5.20), VP helpers (§5.27). **Inherited from example 18:** F-ROLL, F-TM, F-JRNL.

**What it does** (from the file header):

> RG-07 event, roll and thin-session exclusions, applied as a gate on the example 18 host (A2-56).
> Rules: tier-1 news -2/+10 min blackout (and flatten); FOMC day after 14:00 ET -> no new entries; roll week -> no new entries;
> CME half-days and holiday-adjacent sessions -> off. Unscheduled news is NOT covered. GDP/PCE coverage holes 2016-24 (EV-4.6b):
> allowPartialCoverage = false makes an Unknown-coverage day block (conservative).
> Chart: as example 18. M-EVENT uses its embedded table unless EventConfig.csvPath is set.
> Features: M-EVENT (EventConfig, EventBlackout, EventFlattenNow, EventCalendar.Classify, IsRollWeek, CoverageBlocks), F-CAL, F-ROLL.

**Pitfalls.**

- Module on example 18: compile both files; the host's rules, stops and settings apply unless overridden.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 24 - RG-07 event, roll and thin-session exclusions, applied as a gate on the example 18 host (A2-56).
// Rules: tier-1 news -2/+10 min blackout (and flatten); FOMC day after 14:00 ET -> no new entries; roll week -> no new entries;
// CME half-days and holiday-adjacent sessions -> off. Unscheduled news is NOT covered. GDP/PCE coverage holes 2016-24 (EV-4.6b):
// allowPartialCoverage = false makes an Unknown-coverage day block (conservative).
// Chart: as example 18. M-EVENT uses its embedded table unless EventConfig.csvPath is set.
// Features: M-EVENT (EventConfig, EventBlackout, EventFlattenNow, EventCalendar.Classify, IsRollWeek, CoverageBlocks), F-CAL, F-ROLL.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex24ExclusionGate : _Ex18FirstHourBreak
	{
		private IOrderMarket m_flatL, m_flatS;
		public _Ex24ExclusionGate(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			EventConfig.enabled = true;                         // lazy: the calendar is built only when enabled
			EventConfig.blackoutTier = EEventTier.T1;
			EventConfig.blackoutPreMin = 2; EventConfig.blackoutPostMin = 10;
			EventConfig.blackoutFlatten = true;                 // the base only RAISES EventFlattenNow; this strategy acts on it
			EventConfig.allowPartialCoverage = false;
			m_flatL = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "RG07LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_flatS = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "RG07SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();                                     // runs the host (which calls hostGate) and the event engine
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			if (EventFlattenNow)
			{
				int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
				if (pos > 0) m_flatL.Send(); else if (pos < 0) m_flatS.Send();
				if (pos != 0 && journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"RG-07 {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} tier-1 blackout: flatten");
			}
		}

		protected override bool hostGate(DateTime day, out string why)
		{
			why = null;
			if (EventBlackout) { why = "T1 blackout"; return false; }
			_EventCalendar ev = EventCalendar;
			if (ev == null) { why = "no calendar"; return false; }
			EventDay d = ev.Classify(day);
			TimeSpan tod = sessionClock(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (d.hasFomc && tod >= new TimeSpan(14, 0, 0)) why = "FOMC day after 14:00";
			else if (d.isRollWeek || ev.IsRollWeek()) why = "roll week";
			else if (d.isHalfDay || _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day).RthCloseEt.HasValue) why = "half-day";
			else if (holidayAdjacent(day)) why = "holiday-adjacent";
			else if (ev.CoverageBlocks(day, EEventClassMask.Macro)) why = "coverage hole";
			if (why != null && journalOn()) logSignal("RG-07", 0, Bars.Close[0], double.NaN, new Dictionary<string, string> { { "blocked", why } }, false);
			return why == null;
		}

		/// <summary>Day before or after a weekday CME holiday (no RTH session) or early close.</summary>
		private static bool holidayAdjacent(DateTime day)
		{
			foreach (int k in new[] { -1, 1 })
			{
				DateTime d = day.AddDays(k);
				while (d.DayOfWeek == DayOfWeek.Saturday || d.DayOfWeek == DayOfWeek.Sunday) d = d.AddDays(k);
				_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day r = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(d);
				if (r.Closed || r.NoRth || r.RthCloseEt.HasValue) return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.20 Example 25 (`_Ex25ShockBarFade`) — A2-51 intraday shock-bar fade with the delayed stop (Kaufman Ch 16), exit arm E1 (volume decay) (medium)

**Features (direct).** F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13), M-TOD (§5.18).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> A2-51 intraday shock-bar fade with the delayed stop (Kaufman Ch 16), exit arm E1 (volume decay).
> Signal (closed 5m bar b, 10:00-15:00 ET): TR_b >= k x same-slot TOD median TR (N = 100 sessions; the catalogue's SSB average is
> approximated by the normaliser's MEDIAN), close in the top 20% of the bar (-> short) or bottom 20% (-> long). Bars whose window contains
> 08:30 / 10:00 / 14:00 ET +/- 2 min are skipped. Entry: market next bar. Stop: disaster entry -/+ 1.5 x TR_b throughout; from the close of
> b+2 a stop beyond the b..b+2 extreme + 1 tick (both floored at 14 ticks). Exit E1: the first closed bar with volume <= the same-slot TOD
> median volume; E0 15:55 as backstop. k = 2.5.
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED, tick-built volume.
> Features: M-TOD (TodMetrics = TrueRange | Volume, TodSessions = 100, Valid, Median), F-SESS, F-TM (FlatBy, mustFlatten).

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 25 - A2-51 intraday shock-bar fade with the delayed stop (Kaufman Ch 16), exit arm E1 (volume decay).
// Signal (closed 5m bar b, 10:00-15:00 ET): TR_b >= k x same-slot TOD median TR (N = 100 sessions; the catalogue's SSB average is
// approximated by the normaliser's MEDIAN), close in the top 20% of the bar (-> short) or bottom 20% (-> long). Bars whose window contains
// 08:30 / 10:00 / 14:00 ET +/- 2 min are skipped. Entry: market next bar. Stop: disaster entry -/+ 1.5 x TR_b throughout; from the close of
// b+2 a stop beyond the b..b+2 extreme + 1 tick (both floored at 14 ticks). Exit E1: the first closed bar with volume <= the same-slot TOD
// median volume; E0 15:55 as backstop. k = 2.5.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED, tick-built volume.
// Features: M-TOD (TodMetrics = TrueRange | Volume, TodSessions = 100, Valid, Median), F-SESS, F-TM (FlatBy, mustFlatten).
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex25ShockBarFade : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class A251Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1;
			public double k = 2.5, closeZone = 0.2, disasterTr = 1.5;
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(10, 0, 0), to = new TimeSpan(15, 0, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected A251Config Cfg { get; } = new A251Config();
		private static readonly TimeSpan[] Releases = { new TimeSpan(8, 30, 0), new TimeSpan(10, 0, 0), new TimeSpan(14, 0, 0) };

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_se, m_lx, m_sx;
		private IOrderPriced m_lxStp, m_sxStp;
		private int m_shockBar = -1, m_side;
		private double m_shockTr, m_stop;

		public _Ex25ShockBarFade(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			TodMetrics = ETodMetric.TrueRange | ETodMetric.Volume; TodSessions = 100; TodMinSessions = 20; TodBucketMinutes = 5;
			FlatBy = Cfg.flat;
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "A251LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_se = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "A251SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A251LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A251SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A251LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A251SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { base.StartCalc(); m_shockBar = -1; m_side = 0; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close || Bars.CurrentBar < 2) return;
			double tick = tickSize(), floor = Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick;
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			_TodNormaliser tod = TodNormaliser;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				if (mustFlatten()) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				int since = Bars.CurrentBar - m_shockBar;                     // 1 = entry bar b+1, 2 = b+2
				if (since == 1)
				{
					double e = StrategyInfo.AvgEntryPrice, d = Math.Max(Cfg.disasterTr * m_shockTr, floor);
					m_stop = e - Math.Sign(pos) * d;
				}
				else if (since >= 2)
				{
					double ext = pos > 0 ? Math.Min(Bars.Low[0], Math.Min(Bars.Low[1], Bars.Low[2])) - tick
					                     : Math.Max(Bars.High[0], Math.Max(Bars.High[1], Bars.High[2])) + tick;
					if (since == 2) { double e = StrategyInfo.AvgEntryPrice; if (Math.Abs(e - ext) < floor) ext = e - Math.Sign(pos) * floor; m_stop = pos > 0 ? Math.Max(m_stop, ext) : Math.Min(m_stop, ext); }
				}
				if (since >= 2 && tod != null && tod.Valid(ETodMetric.Volume) && Bars.Volume[0] <= tod.Median(ETodMetric.Volume))
				{ if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }      // E1 volume decay
				if (pos > 0) m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); else m_sxStp.Send(m_stop);
				return;
			}
			if (rollOn(1) || tod == null || !tod.Valid(ETodMetric.TrueRange)) return;
			DateTime et = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]);
			TimeSpan t = et.TimeOfDay;
			if (!IsRth() || t < Cfg.from || t > Cfg.to || nearRelease(t)) return;
			double h = Bars.High[0], l = Bars.Low[0], c = Bars.Close[0];
			double tr = Math.Max(h, Bars.Close[1]) - Math.Min(l, Bars.Close[1]);
			double med = tod.Median(ETodMetric.TrueRange);
			if (!(med > 0) || tr < Cfg.k * med || !(h > l)) return;
			double loc = (c - l) / (h - l);
			int side = loc >= 1 - Cfg.closeZone ? -1 : loc <= Cfg.closeZone ? +1 : 0;
			if (side == 0) return;
			double r = Math.Max(Cfg.disasterTr * tr, floor);
			if (overRiskBudget(r)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-51 {et:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} skip: disaster stop {r / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget"); return; }
			m_shockBar = Bars.CurrentBar; m_shockTr = tr; m_stop = c - side * r;
			if (side > 0) m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts); else m_se.Send(Cfg.contracts);
			if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-51 {et:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} shock TR {tr:F2} = {tr / med:F2} x median; fade {(side > 0 ? "long" : "short")}");
		}

		private bool nearRelease(TimeSpan barEndEt)
		{
			TimeSpan start = barEndEt - TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5);
			foreach (TimeSpan r in Releases) if (r >= start - TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2) && r <= barEndEt + TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2)) return true;
			return false;
		}

		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }
	}
}
```

### 3.21 Example 37 (`_Ex37BurstExtremeBreak`) — OF-07 bar-level burst at a fresh extreme, standalone form (both sides) (medium)

**Features (direct).** F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13), M-TICK (§5.21), M-PORT (§5.23).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> OF-07 bar-level burst at a fresh extreme, standalone form (both sides).
> Event: a closed 1m RTH bar takes out the session's RTH high (low) so far, M-TICK flags a print-rate burst on that bar
> (TickStats.valid && TickStats.burst, z >= TickConfig.burstZ), and its range >= 1.5 x the mean range of the prior 20 bars.
> Deviations (stated): the catalogue's volume z >= 2 and "clock-minute norm" are operationalised as the M-TICK print-rate burst and a
> rolling 20-bar range mean; ON extremes are not used. Entry: market next bar in the break direction, 09:35-15:30 ET, max 3 per day.
> Stop: the break bar's midpoint, floored at 14 ticks. Target b = 0.75R (catalogue grid {0.5, 0.75, 1}). Risk budget 0.01 x equity.
> Chart: Data1 @ES 1m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED (M-TICK loads the same symbol's 1-tick Trade stream via DataLoader).
> Features: M-TICK (TickConfig, TickStats), CS-7 (MaxTradesPerDay, FlatBy, entryAllowed), M-PORT (portfolioArm, portfolioStop).

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 37 - OF-07 bar-level burst at a fresh extreme, standalone form (both sides).
// Event: a closed 1m RTH bar takes out the session's RTH high (low) so far, M-TICK flags a print-rate burst on that bar
// (TickStats.valid && TickStats.burst, z >= TickConfig.burstZ), and its range >= 1.5 x the mean range of the prior 20 bars.
// Deviations (stated): the catalogue's volume z >= 2 and "clock-minute norm" are operationalised as the M-TICK print-rate burst and a
// rolling 20-bar range mean; ON extremes are not used. Entry: market next bar in the break direction, 09:35-15:30 ET, max 3 per day.
// Stop: the break bar's midpoint, floored at 14 ticks. Target b = 0.75R (catalogue grid {0.5, 0.75, 1}). Risk budget 0.01 x equity.
// Chart: Data1 @ES 1m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED (M-TICK loads the same symbol's 1-tick Trade stream via DataLoader).
// Features: M-TICK (TickConfig, TickStats), CS-7 (MaxTradesPerDay, FlatBy, entryAllowed), M-PORT (portfolioArm, portfolioStop).
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex37BurstExtremeBreak : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Of07Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, rangeLen = 20, costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double minRangeMult = 1.5, targetR = 0.75, riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 35, 0), to = new TimeSpan(15, 30, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
		}
		protected Of07Config Cfg { get; } = new Of07Config();

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_se, m_lx, m_sx;
		private IOrderPriced m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private double m_hi, m_lo, m_stop, m_target;

		public _Ex37BurstExtremeBreak(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			TickConfig.enabled = true;                         // lazy: no DataLoader tick request unless enabled
			TickConfig.burstZ = 3.0;
			MaxTradesPerDay = 3; FlatBy = Cfg.flat;
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "OF07LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_se = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "OF07SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "OF07LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "OF07SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "OF07LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "OF07SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "OF07LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "OF07ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { base.StartCalc(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) { m_day = day; m_hi = double.NaN; m_lo = double.NaN; }
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				if (mustFlatten()) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); } else { m_sxStp.Send(m_stop); m_sxLmt.Send(m_target); }
				portfolioStop(m_stop);                        // M-PORT: no-op outside Portfolio Trader
			}
			if (!IsRth()) return;
			double h = Bars.High[0], l = Bars.Low[0], c = Bars.Close[0], tick = tickSize();
			bool first = double.IsNaN(m_hi);
			int side = first ? 0 : h > m_hi ? +1 : l < m_lo ? -1 : 0;
			if (h > m_hi || first) m_hi = h;
			if (l < m_lo || first) m_lo = l;
			if (side == 0 || pos != 0 || rollOn(1)) return;
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
			if (tod < Cfg.from || tod > Cfg.to) return;
			string reason;
			if (!entryAllowed(out reason)) return;
			TickBarStats ts = TickStats;
			if (ts == null || !ts.valid || !ts.burst) return;
			if (Bars.CurrentBar <= Cfg.rangeLen) return;
			double mean = 0;
			for (int i = 1; i <= Cfg.rangeLen; i++) mean += Bars.High[i] - Bars.Low[i];
			mean /= Cfg.rangeLen;
			if (h - l < Cfg.minRangeMult * mean) return;
			double stop = (h + l) / 2;
			if (side * (c - stop) < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) stop = c - side * Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick;
			double r = Math.Abs(c - stop);
			if (Cfg.contracts * r * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction))
			{ if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"OF-07 {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} skip: risk budget"); return; }
			m_stop = stop; m_target = c + side * Cfg.targetR * r;
			if (journalOn()) journalArm(side > 0 ? "OF07LE" : "OF07SE", c, m_stop, m_target, null);
			portfolioArm(side, c, m_stop, Cfg.contracts);    // M-PORT: no-op outside Portfolio Trader
			if (side > 0) m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts); else m_se.Send(Cfg.contracts);
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.22 Example 40 (`_Ex40DailyKillSwitch`) — A2-78 daily kill switch as a strategy-level layer on the OF-07 host (Ex37) (medium)

**Features (direct).** F-TM (§5.12), M-PORT (§5.23). **Inherited from example 37:** F-SESS, F-ROLL, F-CAL, F-JRNL, M-TICK.

**What it does** (from the file header):

> A2-78 daily kill switch as a strategy-level layer on the OF-07 host (Ex37).
> A2-78 rules: when the day's P&L <= -D x R, cancel and flatten and take no new entries until the next session; two-strike rule:
> stop after K losers. Grid: D in {2, 3, 4}, K in {off, 3}; this file shows D = 2, K = 3 (grid points, not tuned values).
> What the framework provides per strategy (CS-7): DailyLossStopR (closed net loss in R of RiskUnitDollars) and DailyLossStopCount
> (losing closed trades in the trading day) block new entries through entryAllowed(). Deviations (stated): CS-7 counts ALL losers of
> the day, not CONSECUTIVE losers, and uses CLOSED P&L only; the open position keeps its bracket. The BOOK-level rule (open + closed,
> cancel-and-flatten) is _PortfolioRiskManager / PortMmConfig (dailyLossStop, dailyLossFlatten) in Portfolio Trader.
> RiskUnitDollars = 14 ticks x $12.50 = $175, i.e. the cost-floor stop of one ES contract (a definition of R, not a result).
> M-PORT: PortfolioHalted reports the strategy's own kill to the portfolio MM; PortfolioFamily = "OF".

**Pitfalls.**

- Module on example 37: compile both files; the host's rules, stops and settings apply unless overridden.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 40 - A2-78 daily kill switch as a strategy-level layer on the OF-07 host (Ex37).
// A2-78 rules: when the day's P&L <= -D x R, cancel and flatten and take no new entries until the next session; two-strike rule:
// stop after K losers. Grid: D in {2, 3, 4}, K in {off, 3}; this file shows D = 2, K = 3 (grid points, not tuned values).
// What the framework provides per strategy (CS-7): DailyLossStopR (closed net loss in R of RiskUnitDollars) and DailyLossStopCount
// (losing closed trades in the trading day) block new entries through entryAllowed(). Deviations (stated): CS-7 counts ALL losers of
// the day, not CONSECUTIVE losers, and uses CLOSED P&L only; the open position keeps its bracket. The BOOK-level rule (open + closed,
// cancel-and-flatten) is _PortfolioRiskManager / PortMmConfig (dailyLossStop, dailyLossFlatten) in Portfolio Trader.
// RiskUnitDollars = 14 ticks x $12.50 = $175, i.e. the cost-floor stop of one ES contract (a definition of R, not a result).
// M-PORT: PortfolioHalted reports the strategy's own kill to the portfolio MM; PortfolioFamily = "OF".
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex40DailyKillSwitch : _Ex37BurstExtremeBreak
	{
		private bool m_halted;

		public _Ex40DailyKillSwitch(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override string PortfolioFamily { get { return "OF"; } }
		protected override bool PortfolioHalted { get { return m_halted; } }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			DailyLossStopR = 2;            // D
			DailyLossStopCount = 3;        // K (all losers of the day; see header)
			RiskUnitDollars = 14 * 12.50;  // 1 R = cost-floor stop of one full-size ES
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { base.StartCalc(); m_halted = false; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			string reason;
			bool ok = entryAllowed(out reason);              // CS-7 resets per trading day
			bool halted = !ok && reason != null && reason.StartsWith("DailyLossStop");
			if (halted && !m_halted && journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-78 {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} kill: {reason}");
			m_halted = halted;
			base.CalcBar();
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.23 Example 6 (`_Ex06NakedLevelReaction`) — PS-21 naked POC/VAH/VAL first touch (medium–hard)

**Features (direct).** F-PROF (§5.2), F-NPOC (§5.4), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-JRNL (§5.13), F-EVT (§5.14), M-MTF (§5.17).


**Catalogue rules (PS-21).** Market and timeframe: ES/NQ, GC, CL; 5m. Level: the nearest naked POC/VAH/VAL ≤ 20 sessions old. Entry: first touch ± δ, then a 5m rejection close. Stop: level + floor. Target: 1R. Sweep: age {≤ 5, ≤ 20} × level {POC, VAH/VAL}. Multi-day levels reset at rolls. The catalogue expects a kill and keeps it as a control for EX-02. **G0-d-gated** (families 1 and 2).

**Chart.** Data1 = @ES 1m with the MC Volume Profile **on** (session profiles), Data2 = @ES 5m, dxFeed unadjusted, 24h. Derives from `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`, `TrackNakedPoc = true` (StartCalc throws otherwise).

**Walk-through.**
- The level search uses `RecentCompletedProfiles(1, maxAge, barNo - 1)`: profiles completed **as of the previous bar**. `barNo` is `Bars.FullSymbolData.Current`, the 1-based Status Bar number that `asOfBar` expects (not `Bars.CurrentBar`).
- Profiles that cross a roll or start at or before the last roll bar are skipped.
- Naked tests: `VpExtra.IsNakedPoc(asOfBar)` for POCs, `IsNakedVah`/`IsNakedVal` for value edges. A price already traded once is kept in a set and never used again (first touch only).
- Side: price above the level means support (long), below means resistance (short). The nearest candidate to the prior close wins.
- A touch is the 1m low (or high) within `touchTicks` of the level. The decision waits for the first **closed** 5m bar at or after the touch: a close back on the entry side is a rejection, and the trade is armed at market; anything else is discarded and logged as untaken.
- Stop = level − side × 4 ticks (`stopBufferTicks`); target = 1R from the 1m close of the decision bar. If that R is under the 14-tick cost floor (`costFloorTicks`), the stop is widened to 14 ticks from the 1m close (and the 1R target moves with it). If the widened stop breaches the per-trade risk budget (`riskBudgetFraction` = 0.01, 1% of equity, decided by Ettienne 2026-10-23; PF-04's 0.5% cap is not the default for the floor rule), the trade is skipped and journaled. Size is never shrunk. Short entries mirror longs. Entries until 15:30; flat at 15:55, or at `RthCloseEt` − 5 min on an early-close day (`flatAt`).

**Pitfalls.**
- **Assumption (labelled):** the 4-tick buffer beyond the level. When the 1m close is under 10 ticks from the level, the 4-tick buffer gives a stop under the 14-tick floor, so the stop is widened to 14 ticks from the close.
- **First touch uses up the level.** The level goes into `m_used` at the touch, so a touch that is discarded (no rejection) or skipped (risk-budget breach) uses up that level too.
- Stop and target are sent from the bar after the fill (§1.7), so the fill bar has no working exit.
- `asOfBar` is the Status Bar number. Passing `Bars.CurrentBar` silently shifts the as-of point once MaxBarsBack is not 0.
- If the touch bar also closes the 5m bar that holds it, the rejection is checked on the same bar.
- PS-21's K-FL control with distance matching is mandatory (catalogue); it is not coded here.

**Full strategy** — `_Ex06NakedLevelReaction.Strategy.CS`:

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 6 - PS-21 first touch of the nearest naked POC / VAH / VAL (<= 20 sessions) + 5m rejection close, target 1R.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m with the MultiCharts Volume Profile ON (session profiles), Data2 = @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED, 24h.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex06NakedLevelReaction : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		public enum ELevelSet { Poc = 1, ValueEdges = 2 }

		protected sealed class Ps21Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1;
			public int maxAgeSessions = 20;          // sweep {5, 20}
			public ELevelSet levels = ELevelSet.Poc; // sweep {POC, VAH/VAL}
			public int touchTicks = 1;               // delta of "first touch +/- delta"
			public int stopBufferTicks = 4;          // structural stop: this far beyond the level (an assumption of this example)
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;          // catalogue 2.1: ES cost floor; stop distance = max(structural, floor), size never shrunk
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;  // floor-rule risk budget: Ettienne, 2026-10-23: 1% of equity (PF-04's 0.5% is not the default here)
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 30, 0), to = new TimeSpan(15, 30, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public TimeSpan halfDayExitBefore = new TimeSpan(0, 5, 0);   // early-close days: flat at RthCloseEt - 5 min
		}
		protected Ps21Config Cfg { get; } = new Ps21Config();

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_se, m_lx, m_sx;
		private IOrderPriced m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private int m_lastRollBar;                       // 1-based Status Bar # of the last roll bar
		private double m_level = double.NaN; private int m_side; private DateTime m_touch5 = DateTime.MinValue, m_last5 = DateTime.MinValue;
		private readonly HashSet<double> m_used = new HashSet<double>();   // first touch only: a level is never traded twice
		private double m_stop = double.NaN, m_target = double.NaN;

		/// <summary>Catalogue 2.1 / App F #9 (resolved): true when Cfg.contracts at this stop distance risk more than the per-trade budget.</summary>
		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }
		public _Ex06NakedLevelReaction(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			TrackNakedPoc = true;
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS21LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_se = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS21SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS21LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS21SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS21LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS21SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS21LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS21ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			HtfStreams = new[] { 2 };
			base.StartCalc();
			if (!TrackNakedPoc) throw new ArgumentException("_Ex06NakedLevelReaction: TrackNakedPoc must be true");
			m_lastRollBar = 0; m_level = double.NaN; m_side = 0; m_touch5 = m_last5 = DateTime.MinValue; m_used.Clear();
			m_stop = m_target = double.NaN;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			int barNo = Bars.FullSymbolData.Current;           // 1-based Status Bar #: the unit of asOfBar (NOT Bars.CurrentBar)
			if (rollOn(1)) { m_lastRollBar = barNo; m_level = double.NaN; m_side = 0; }
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
			TimeSpan flat = flatAt(GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]));
			TimeSpan entryEnd = Cfg.to < flat ? Cfg.to : flat;
			double tick = tickSize();
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;

			if (pos != 0)
			{
				if (tod >= flat && tod < new TimeSpan(17, 0, 0)) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); } else { m_sxStp.Send(m_stop); m_sxLmt.Send(m_target); }
				return;
			}

			// a. pending touch: wait for the first 5m bar that CLOSES after the touch (closed 5m bars only)
			DateTime t5; double o5, h5, l5, c5, v5;
			HtfClosedBar(2, 0, out t5, out o5, out h5, out l5, out c5, out v5);
			bool new5 = t5 != m_last5; m_last5 = t5;
			if (m_side != 0 && new5 && t5 >= m_touch5) { checkReject(c5, tod, entryEnd, tick); return; }
			if (m_side != 0 || tod < Cfg.from || tod >= entryEnd) return;

			// b. levels naked as of the PREVIOUS bar, from the last maxAgeSessions completed profiles, none older than the last roll
			double best = double.NaN; int side = 0; double c1 = Bars.Close[1];
			foreach (VpExtra e in RecentCompletedProfiles(1, Cfg.maxAgeSessions, barNo - 1))
			{
				if (e.crossesRoll || e.startBar <= m_lastRollBar) continue;   // multi-day levels reset at rolls
				var cand = new List<double>();
				if (Cfg.levels == ELevelSet.Poc) { if (e.IsNakedPoc(barNo - 1)) cand.Add(e.poc); }
				else { if (IsNakedVah(e, barNo - 1)) cand.Add(e.vah); if (IsNakedVal(e, barNo - 1)) cand.Add(e.val); }
				foreach (double lv in cand)
				{
					if (m_used.Contains(lv)) continue;
					int s = c1 > lv ? +1 : c1 < lv ? -1 : 0;     // approached from above = support (long); from below = resistance (short)
					if (s == 0) continue;
					if (double.IsNaN(best) || Math.Abs(c1 - lv) < Math.Abs(c1 - best)) { best = lv; side = s; }
				}
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(best)) return;
			bool touched = side > 0 ? Bars.Low[0] <= best + Cfg.touchTicks * tick : Bars.High[0] >= best - Cfg.touchTicks * tick;
			if (!touched) return;
			m_used.Add(best); m_level = best; m_side = side; m_touch5 = Bars.Time[0];
			if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-21 touch {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} level({best}) side({side})");
			if (new5 && t5 >= m_touch5) checkReject(c5, tod, entryEnd, tick);   // the touch bar also closed the 5m bar that holds it
		}

		/// <summary>The first 5m close at or after the touch decides: back on the entry side of the level = rejection, else discard.
		/// Either way the level stays in m_used: a discarded touch uses the level up for good.</summary>
		private void checkReject(double c5, TimeSpan tod, TimeSpan entryEnd, double tick)
		{
			bool reject = m_side > 0 ? c5 > m_level : c5 < m_level;
			if (reject && tod < entryEnd) arm(m_side, Bars.Close[0], tick);
			else if (journalOn()) logSignal("PS-21", m_side, m_level, double.NaN, null, false);
			m_side = 0; m_level = double.NaN;
		}

		private void arm(int side, double planned, double tick)
		{
			double stop = m_level - side * Cfg.stopBufferTicks * tick;
			double r = Math.Abs(planned - stop);
			if (r < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) { r = Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick; stop = planned - side * r; }   // catalogue 2.1: widen to the cost floor
			if (overRiskBudget(r))   // widened stop breaches the per-trade risk budget: skipped and logged, not resized
			{
				if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-21 {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} skip: stop {r / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget {Cfg.riskBudgetFraction:P2}");
				return;
			}
			m_stop = stop;
			m_target = planned + side * r;                     // 1R from the planned (signal-close) price
			var ctx = new Dictionary<string, string> { { "level", m_level.ToString() } };
			if (journalOn()) { journalArm(side > 0 ? "PS21LE" : "PS21SE", planned, m_stop, m_target, ctx); logSignal("PS-21", side, planned, m_stop, ctx, true); }
			if (side > 0) m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts); else m_se.Send(Cfg.contracts);
		}

		/// <summary>Flat time for a Globex day: Cfg.flat, or on a CME early-close day RthCloseEt - halfDayExitBefore if earlier
		/// (_CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day).RthCloseEt; same rule as A263Config.halfDayExitBefore).</summary>
		private TimeSpan flatAt(DateTime day)
		{
			_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day);
			TimeSpan f = Cfg.flat;
			if (cme.RthCloseEt.HasValue && cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore < f) f = cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore;
			return f;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.24 Example 8 (`_Ex08EntropyDayType`) — A2-29 entropy day type at 11:00 (medium–hard)

**Features (direct).** F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), M-GMM (§5.25), VP helpers (§5.27).


**Catalogue rules (A2-29).** At 11:00 ET, take the developing RTH profile 09:30–11:00 and its H_norm and n_eff as percentiles versus the same-time values of the trailing 250 sessions. H_norm pct ≥ 80 → trend/dispersed; H_norm pct ≤ 30 **and** n_eff pct ≤ 30 → balance; otherwise no change. It is a classifier (module), so no orders.

**Chart.** Data1 = @ES 1m, dxFeed unadjusted, 24h Globex template. `RangeProfiles.enabled = true`.

**Walk-through.**
- At the bar closing at 11:00 ET (skipping NoRth days), `RangeProfileAfter(1, t0, t)` builds the profile of 1m bars ending in `(09:30, 11:00]`: an exclusive start, end-stamped bars.
- `RangeStatsOf(e)` gives the bar count; `ProfileEntropy(e, st.bars)` gives the Miller–Madow `HnormMm` and `Neff`, with N = contributing bars (the VP-4 default).
- Two `_EntropyTracker`s (lookback `ProfileStats.pctLookbackA229` = 250, `pctMinFill` = 0.8) return today's percentile against **earlier** sessions first, and only then push today's value.
- The state (TREND, BALANCE, NEUTRAL, or warm-up/no profile) is exposed as `DayType` and journaled with the raw values.

**Pitfalls.**
- Percentile first, push second. Doing it the other way includes today in its own baseline.
- `t0 = t − 90 minutes` in chart time; the range is in chart time, not ET.
- Until 80% of 250 sessions are filled, the percentiles are NaN and the line says warm-up.
- **Half-days are in the pool.** Only NoRth days are skipped, so early-close sessions add their 11:00 values to the 250-session baselines (on a half-day, 09:30–11:00 is still complete). M-TOD excludes half-days elsewhere in this manual (`todHalfDay`, §1.7); this example does not.

**Full strategy** — `_Ex08EntropyDayType.Strategy.CS`:

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 8 - A2-29 developing-profile entropy day type at 11:00 ET (module: journals TREND / BALANCE / NEUTRAL, no orders).
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED, 24h CME Globex template. Range profiles ON.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex08EntropyDayType : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		public enum EDayType { Unknown = 0, Neutral = 1, Trend = 2, Balance = 3 }

		protected sealed class A229Config
		{
			public TimeSpan rthOpen = new TimeSpan(9, 30, 0), decision = new TimeSpan(11, 0, 0);
			public double trendPct = 80, balancePct = 30;
		}
		protected A229Config Cfg { get; } = new A229Config();

		public EDayType DayType { get; private set; }
		private _EntropyTracker m_hnorm, m_neff;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private int m_slot;

		public _Ex08EntropyDayType(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			RangeProfiles.enabled = true;
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			base.StartCalc();
			m_slot = Cfg.decision.Hours * 60 + Cfg.decision.Minutes;   // = _EntropyTracker.Slot(11:00) = 660
			m_hnorm = new _EntropyTracker(ProfileStats.pctLookbackA229, ProfileStats.pctMinFill, new[] { m_slot });
			m_neff = new _EntropyTracker(ProfileStats.pctLookbackA229, ProfileStats.pctMinFill, new[] { m_slot });
			m_day = DateTime.MinValue; DayType = EDayType.Unknown;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime t = Bars.Time[0], et = ToEastern(t), day = GlobexDate(t);
			if (day != m_day) { m_day = day; DayType = EDayType.Unknown; }
			if (et.TimeOfDay != Cfg.decision) return;
			_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day);
			if (cme.NoRth) return;

			// developing RTH profile 09:30-11:00: 1m bars ENDING in (09:30, 11:00] -> exclusive t0, in chart time (t is the 11:00 bar end)
			DateTime t0 = t - (Cfg.decision - Cfg.rthOpen);
			VpExtra e = RangeProfileAfter(1, t0, t);
			RangeStats st = RangeStatsOf(e);
			if (e == null || st == null || st.bars < 1) { journal(day, "no profile", st); return; }
			EntropyResult r = ProfileEntropy(e, st.bars);   // Miller-Madow N = contributing bars (VP-4 default basis)

			// percentiles vs the prior 250 sessions FIRST, then push today (strictly earlier sessions only)
			double pH = m_hnorm.Percentile(m_slot, r.HnormMm), pN = m_neff.Percentile(m_slot, r.Neff);
			m_hnorm.Push(m_slot, day, r.HnormMm); m_neff.Push(m_slot, day, r.Neff);
			if (double.IsNaN(pH) || double.IsNaN(pN)) { journal(day, "warm-up", st, r, pH, pN); return; }
			DayType = pH >= Cfg.trendPct ? EDayType.Trend : (pH <= Cfg.balancePct && pN <= Cfg.balancePct) ? EDayType.Balance : EDayType.Neutral;
			journal(day, DayType.ToString(), st, r, pH, pN);
		}

		private void journal(DateTime day, string state, RangeStats st, EntropyResult r = default(EntropyResult), double pH = double.NaN, double pN = double.NaN)
		{
			if (!journalOn()) return;
			trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-29 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} 11:00 HnormMm({r.HnormMm:F4}) Neff({r.Neff:F1}) pctH({pH:F1}) pctNeff({pN:F1}) state({state}) stats({st})");
		}
	}
}
```
