### 3.25 Example 22 (`_Ex22PoorHighRun`) — PS-19 prior-session poor high / poor low run-through (medium–hard)

**Features (direct).** F-DEV (§5.1), F-PROF (§5.2), F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> PS-19 prior-session poor high / poor low run-through.
> Level: the prior RTH profile's high when IsPoorHigh (low when IsPoorLow). Signal: the first 5m close >= delta beyond it, 09:45-14:30 ET.
> Entry: market next bar. Stop: 0.5 x ATR(14, 5m) back inside the level, floored. Target: b x R (b = 1; the catalogue sweeps 0.75/1/1.5).
> Flat 15:55. One trade per day.
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
> Features: F-VP (DevelopingSource = Own, OwnProfileWindow = Rth, PriorRthProfile, IsPoorHigh/IsPoorLow), F-SESS, F-TM (FlatBy).

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 22 - PS-19 prior-session poor high / poor low run-through.
// Level: the prior RTH profile's high when IsPoorHigh (low when IsPoorLow). Signal: the first 5m close >= delta beyond it, 09:45-14:30 ET.
// Entry: market next bar. Stop: 0.5 x ATR(14, 5m) back inside the level, floored. Target: b x R (b = 1; the catalogue sweeps 0.75/1/1.5).
// Flat 15:55. One trade per day.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
// Features: F-VP (DevelopingSource = Own, OwnProfileWindow = Rth, PriorRthProfile, IsPoorHigh/IsPoorLow), F-SESS, F-TM (FlatBy).
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex22PoorHighRun : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Ps19Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, deltaTicks = 2, atrLength = 14;
			public double stopAtr = 0.5, targetR = 1.0;
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), to = new TimeSpan(14, 30, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Ps19Config Cfg { get; } = new Ps19Config();

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_se, m_lx, m_sx;
		private IOrderPriced m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private double m_hiLvl, m_loLvl, m_stop, m_target;
		private bool m_done;

		public _Ex22PoorHighRun(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			DevelopingSource = EDevelopingSource.Own;      // PriorRthProfile() is built from Own profiles
			OwnProfileWindow = EOwnProfileWindow.Rth;
			FlatBy = Cfg.flat;
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS19LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_se = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS19SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS19LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS19SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS19LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS19SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS19LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS19ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { base.StartCalc(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; m_done = false; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day)
			{
				m_day = day; m_done = false; m_hiLvl = m_loLvl = double.NaN;
				VpExtra prior = PriorRthProfile();
				if (prior != null && !prior.crossesRoll)
				{
					if (IsPoorHigh(prior)) m_hiLvl = prior.high;
					if (IsPoorLow(prior)) m_loLvl = prior.low;
				}
			}
			if (rollOn(1)) m_done = true;
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				m_done = true;
				if (mustFlatten()) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); } else { m_sxStp.Send(m_stop); m_sxLmt.Send(m_target); }
				return;
			}
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
			if (m_done || !IsRth() || tod < Cfg.from || tod > Cfg.to) return;
			double tick = tickSize(), c = Bars.Close[0], d = Cfg.deltaTicks * tick;
			int side = !double.IsNaN(m_hiLvl) && c >= m_hiLvl + d ? +1 : !double.IsNaN(m_loLvl) && c <= m_loLvl - d ? -1 : 0;
			if (side == 0) return;
			m_done = true;                                                  // the FIRST qualifying close only
			double atr = averageTrueRange(Bars, Cfg.atrLength);
			if (!(atr > 0)) return;
			double lvl = side > 0 ? m_hiLvl : m_loLvl, stop = lvl - side * Cfg.stopAtr * atr;
			if (side * (c - stop) < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) stop = c - side * Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick;
			double r = Math.Abs(c - stop);
			if (overRiskBudget(r)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-19 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} skip: stop {r / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget"); return; }
			m_stop = stop; m_target = c + side * Cfg.targetR * r;
			if (side > 0) m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts); else m_se.Send(Cfg.contracts);
			if (journalOn()) journalArm(side > 0 ? "PS19LE" : "PS19SE", c, stop, m_target, null);
		}

		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }
	}
}
```

### 3.26 Example 26 (`_Ex26CompositeBreakPullback`) — PS-01 balance break, acceptance, then the first higher-low pullback (long side shown; short mirrors) (medium–hard)

**Features (direct).** F-DEV (§5.1), F-PROF (§5.2), F-COMP (§5.3), F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13), M-SWING (§5.16).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> PS-01 balance break, acceptance, then the first higher-low pullback (long side shown; short mirrors).
> Filter: IsBalanced(Own RTH profiles, n = 3, 0.6) at the prior close; V-score >= +1 for longs (<= -1 shorts); break 09:45-14:30 ET.
> Acceptance: 2 closed 5m bars > composite VAH + delta (W = composite VAH - VAL). Entry: the first pullback that holds above
> VAH - 0.25 W and prints a PA pivot low labelled HL; buy stop 1 tick above the confirming bar. Stop: max(1 tick below the HL, floor);
> hard invalidation on a 5m close < VAH - delta. Target: VAH + 1.0 W; stop to break-even at +1 R.
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
> Features: F-COMP (Composite), F-VP (IsBalanced, ValueMigrationScore, OwnProfileWindow), M-PA (HL pivots), F-TM (FlatBy).

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 26 - PS-01 balance break, acceptance, then the first higher-low pullback (long side shown; short mirrors).
// Filter: IsBalanced(Own RTH profiles, n = 3, 0.6) at the prior close; V-score >= +1 for longs (<= -1 shorts); break 09:45-14:30 ET.
// Acceptance: 2 closed 5m bars > composite VAH + delta (W = composite VAH - VAL). Entry: the first pullback that holds above
// VAH - 0.25 W and prints a PA pivot low labelled HL; buy stop 1 tick above the confirming bar. Stop: max(1 tick below the HL, floor);
// hard invalidation on a 5m close < VAH - delta. Target: VAH + 1.0 W; stop to break-even at +1 R.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m (24h Globex template), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
// Features: F-COMP (Composite), F-VP (IsBalanced, ValueMigrationScore, OwnProfileWindow), M-PA (HL pivots), F-TM (FlatBy).
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex26CompositeBreakPullback : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Ps01Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, n = 3, deltaTicks = 2, acceptCloses = 2;
			public double holdW = 0.25, targetW = 1.0, balanceOverlap = 0.6;
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), to = new TimeSpan(14, 30, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Ps01Config Cfg { get; } = new Ps01Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leStp, m_seStp, m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private IOrderMarket m_lx, m_sx;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private bool m_balanced, m_done;
		private int m_vscore, m_up, m_dn, m_side;
		private double m_vah, m_val, m_w, m_trigger = double.NaN, m_stop, m_target, m_entryRisk;

		public _Ex26CompositeBreakPullback(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			DevelopingSource = EDevelopingSource.Own; OwnProfileWindow = EOwnProfileWindow.Rth;
			PriceActionConfig.enabled = true; PriceActionConfig.dataStream = 1;
			FlatBy = Cfg.flat;
			m_leStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS01LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_seStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS01SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS01LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS01SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS01LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS01SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS01LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS01ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { base.StartCalc(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) newDay(day);
			if (rollOn(1)) m_done = true;
			double tick = tickSize(), c = Bars.Close[0], d = Cfg.deltaTicks * tick;
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				m_done = true;
				if (mustFlatten() || (pos > 0 ? c < m_vah - d : c > m_val + d)) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }  // hard invalidation
				double e = StrategyInfo.AvgEntryPrice;
				if (pos > 0 ? Bars.High[0] >= e + m_entryRisk : Bars.Low[0] <= e - m_entryRisk) m_stop = pos > 0 ? Math.Max(m_stop, e) : Math.Min(m_stop, e);   // BE at +1R
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); } else { m_sxStp.Send(m_stop); m_sxLmt.Send(m_target); }
				return;
			}
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
			if (m_done || !m_balanced || !(m_w > 0) || !IsRth() || tod > Cfg.to) return;
			if (m_side == 0)
			{
				if (tod < Cfg.from) return;
				m_up = c > m_vah + d ? m_up + 1 : 0; m_dn = c < m_val - d ? m_dn + 1 : 0;
				if (m_up >= Cfg.acceptCloses && m_vscore >= 1) m_side = +1;
				else if (m_dn >= Cfg.acceptCloses && m_vscore <= -1) m_side = -1;
				if (m_side != 0 && journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-01 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} accepted {(m_side > 0 ? "above VAH" : "below VAL")} W={m_w:F2} V={m_vscore}");
				return;
			}
			double hold = m_side > 0 ? m_vah - Cfg.holdW * m_w : m_val + Cfg.holdW * m_w;
			if (m_side > 0 ? Bars.Low[0] < hold : Bars.High[0] > hold) { m_done = true; return; }   // pullback failed to hold
			_PriceActionEngine pa = PriceActionEngine;
			if (pa != null && pa.IsWarm)
			{
				_Pivot p = m_side > 0 ? (pa.NewPivotLow ? pa.LastLow(0) : null) : (pa.NewPivotHigh ? pa.LastHigh(0) : null);
				if (p != null && p.label == (m_side > 0 ? EPivotLabel.HL : EPivotLabel.LH))
				{
					m_trigger = m_side > 0 ? Bars.High[0] + tick : Bars.Low[0] - tick;
					double stop = p.price - m_side * tick;
					if (m_side * (m_trigger - stop) < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) stop = m_trigger - m_side * Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick;
					m_entryRisk = Math.Abs(m_trigger - stop);
					if (overRiskBudget(m_entryRisk)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-01 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} skip: stop {m_entryRisk / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget"); m_done = true; return; }
					m_stop = stop; m_target = m_side > 0 ? m_vah + Cfg.targetW * m_w : m_val - Cfg.targetW * m_w;
					if (journalOn()) journalArm(m_side > 0 ? "PS01LE" : "PS01SE", m_trigger, m_stop, m_target, null);
				}
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(m_trigger)) return;
			if (m_side > 0) m_leStp.Send(m_trigger, Cfg.contracts); else m_seStp.Send(m_trigger, Cfg.contracts);
		}

		private void newDay(DateTime day)
		{
			m_day = day; m_done = false; m_up = m_dn = m_side = 0; m_trigger = double.NaN;
			VpExtra comp = Composite(1, Cfg.n);                       // the n completed RTH profiles before today
			m_balanced = comp != null && IsBalanced(1, Cfg.n, Cfg.balanceOverlap);
			int? v = ValueMigrationScore(1, Cfg.n, 0.10, 20, false);  // completed sessions only (as of the prior close)
			m_vscore = v ?? 0;
			if (comp != null) { m_vah = comp.vah; m_val = comp.val; m_w = comp.vah - comp.val; } else m_w = double.NaN;
		}

		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }
	}
}
```

### 3.27 Example 27 (`_Ex27CompositeBreakMarket`) — PS-02 balance break, market entry after acceptance (long side shown; short mirrors) (medium–hard)

**Features (direct).** F-DEV (§5.1), F-PROF (§5.2), F-COMP (§5.3), F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13), M-DAILY (§5.19), M-GMM (§5.25).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> PS-02 balance break, market entry after acceptance (long side shown; short mirrors).
> Filter: as PS-01 (IsBalanced(RTH, n = 3, 0.6)) + V-score aligned (>= +1 long, <= -1 short) + composite entropy Hnorm <= 0.93.
> Entry: at market on the next bar after the 2nd consecutive closed 5m bar beyond composite VAH/VAL + delta, 09:45-14:30 ET.
> Stop: composite VAH - 0.30 W (long) / VAL + 0.30 W (short), floored at 14 ticks from the signal close. Skip the setup (never resize)
> if 0.30 W < ATR14(5m) + 2 ticks (noise) or 0.30 W > 1.5 x daily ATR (catalogue "daily ATR"; operationalised as M-DAILY Atr20d).
> Target: 1.0 W beyond the broken edge. Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
> Features: F-COMP, F-VP (IsBalanced, ValueMigrationScore), F-PSTAT (ProfileStats.enabled, ProfileEntropy), M-DAILY (Atr20d), F-TM.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 27 - PS-02 balance break, market entry after acceptance (long side shown; short mirrors).
// Filter: as PS-01 (IsBalanced(RTH, n = 3, 0.6)) + V-score aligned (>= +1 long, <= -1 short) + composite entropy Hnorm <= 0.93.
// Entry: at market on the next bar after the 2nd consecutive closed 5m bar beyond composite VAH/VAL + delta, 09:45-14:30 ET.
// Stop: composite VAH - 0.30 W (long) / VAL + 0.30 W (short), floored at 14 ticks from the signal close. Skip the setup (never resize)
// if 0.30 W < ATR14(5m) + 2 ticks (noise) or 0.30 W > 1.5 x daily ATR (catalogue "daily ATR"; operationalised as M-DAILY Atr20d).
// Target: 1.0 W beyond the broken edge. Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
// Features: F-COMP, F-VP (IsBalanced, ValueMigrationScore), F-PSTAT (ProfileStats.enabled, ProfileEntropy), M-DAILY (Atr20d), F-TM.
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex27CompositeBreakMarket : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Ps02Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, n = 3, deltaTicks = 2, acceptCloses = 2, atrLength = 14, noiseTicks = 2;
			public double stopW = 0.30, targetW = 1.0, maxEntropy = 0.93, maxDailyAtr = 1.5, balanceOverlap = 0.6;
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), to = new TimeSpan(14, 30, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Ps02Config Cfg { get; } = new Ps02Config();

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_se, m_lx, m_sx;
		private IOrderPriced m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private bool m_ok, m_done;
		private int m_vscore, m_up, m_dn;
		private double m_vah, m_val, m_w, m_stop, m_target;

		public _Ex27CompositeBreakMarket(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			DevelopingSource = EDevelopingSource.Own; OwnProfileWindow = EOwnProfileWindow.Rth;
			ProfileStats.enabled = true;              // lazy: F-PSTAT helpers (entropy) only exist when enabled
			FlatBy = Cfg.flat;
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS02LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_se = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS02SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS02LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS02SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS02LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS02SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS02LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS02ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { NeedsDaily = true; base.StartCalc(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) newDay(day);
			if (rollOn(1)) m_done = true;
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				m_done = true;
				if (mustFlatten()) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); } else { m_sxStp.Send(m_stop); m_sxLmt.Send(m_target); }
				return;
			}
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
			if (m_done || !m_ok || !IsRth() || tod < Cfg.from || tod > Cfg.to) return;
			double tick = tickSize(), c = Bars.Close[0], d = Cfg.deltaTicks * tick;
			m_up = c > m_vah + d ? m_up + 1 : 0; m_dn = c < m_val - d ? m_dn + 1 : 0;
			int side = m_up >= Cfg.acceptCloses && m_vscore >= 1 ? +1 : m_dn >= Cfg.acceptCloses && m_vscore <= -1 ? -1 : 0;
			if (side == 0) return;
			m_done = true;                                          // one attempt per day, whatever the skip outcome
			double sw = Cfg.stopW * m_w, atr = averageTrueRange(Bars, Cfg.atrLength);
			double atrD = DailyContext != null ? DailyContext.Atr20d : double.NaN;
			string why = !(atrD > 0) ? "Atr20d NaN" : sw < atr + Cfg.noiseTicks * tick ? "0.30W inside 5m noise" : sw > Cfg.maxDailyAtr * atrD ? "0.30W > 1.5 daily ATR" : null;
			double stop = side > 0 ? m_vah - sw : m_val + sw;
			if (side * (c - stop) < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) stop = c - side * Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick;
			if (why == null && Cfg.contracts * Math.Abs(c - stop) * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction)) why = "risk budget";
			if (why != null) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-02 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} skip: {why}"); return; }
			m_stop = stop; m_target = side > 0 ? m_vah + Cfg.targetW * m_w : m_val - Cfg.targetW * m_w;
			if (journalOn()) journalArm(side > 0 ? "PS02LE" : "PS02SE", c, m_stop, m_target, null);
			if (side > 0) m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts); else m_se.Send(Cfg.contracts);
		}

		private void newDay(DateTime day)
		{
			m_day = day; m_done = false; m_up = m_dn = 0;
			VpExtra comp = Composite(1, Cfg.n);
			m_ok = comp != null && IsBalanced(1, Cfg.n, Cfg.balanceOverlap);
			if (m_ok)
			{
				EntropyResult h = ProfileEntropy(comp, comp.endBar - comp.startBar + 1);   // mmN = bars in the window (EMmN.Bars convention)
				m_ok = h.Hnorm <= Cfg.maxEntropy;
				m_vah = comp.vah; m_val = comp.val; m_w = comp.vah - comp.val;
				m_ok &= m_w > 0;
			}
			m_vscore = ValueMigrationScore(1, Cfg.n, 0.10, 20, false) ?? 0;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.28 Example 29 (`_Ex29LvnEdgeRejection`) — PS-22 composite LVN-edge rejection, with the HVN-ceiling veto as a separate (switchable) layer (medium–hard)

**Features (direct).** F-DEV (§5.1), F-COMP (§5.3), F-HVN (§5.5), F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13), F-EVT (§5.14).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> PS-22 composite LVN-edge rejection, with the HVN-ceiling veto as a separate (switchable) layer.
> Level: LVN edges of the 20-session RTH composite (Composite(1, 20).lvns). Long: a 5m bar trades down into an LVN (low <= lvn.high)
> from above and closes back above lvn.high + delta in its upper half (rejection close, PS-16 without the slowness filter).
> Short mirrors at lvn.low. Entry: market next bar. Stop: max(bar extreme -/+ 1 tick, 14-tick floor). Target 1 R.
> Veto (Cfg.hvnVeto, tested separately): skip a long whose 1 R target lies beyond the low of the nearest composite HVN above.
> Window 09:45-15:00 ET, one trade per day. Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: F-COMP (lvns/hvns), F-TM.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 29 - PS-22 composite LVN-edge rejection, with the HVN-ceiling veto as a separate (switchable) layer.
// Level: LVN edges of the 20-session RTH composite (Composite(1, 20).lvns). Long: a 5m bar trades down into an LVN (low <= lvn.high)
// from above and closes back above lvn.high + delta in its upper half (rejection close, PS-16 without the slowness filter).
// Short mirrors at lvn.low. Entry: market next bar. Stop: max(bar extreme -/+ 1 tick, 14-tick floor). Target 1 R.
// Veto (Cfg.hvnVeto, tested separately): skip a long whose 1 R target lies beyond the low of the nearest composite HVN above.
// Window 09:45-15:00 ET, one trade per day. Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: F-COMP (lvns/hvns), F-TM.
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex29LvnEdgeRejection : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Ps22Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, n = 20, deltaTicks = 1;
			public bool hvnVeto = false;              // the veto layer is its own test arm
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), to = new TimeSpan(15, 0, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Ps22Config Cfg { get; } = new Ps22Config();

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_se, m_lx, m_sx;
		private IOrderPriced m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private VpExtra m_comp;
		private bool m_done;
		private double m_stop, m_target;

		public _Ex29LvnEdgeRejection(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			DevelopingSource = EDevelopingSource.Own; OwnProfileWindow = EOwnProfileWindow.Rth;
			FlatBy = Cfg.flat; MaxTradesPerDay = 1;
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS22LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_se = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS22SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS22LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS22SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS22LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS22SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS22LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS22ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { base.StartCalc(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) { m_day = day; m_done = false; m_comp = Composite(1, Cfg.n); }
			if (rollOn(1)) m_done = true;
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				m_done = true;
				if (mustFlatten()) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); } else { m_sxStp.Send(m_stop); m_sxLmt.Send(m_target); }
				return;
			}
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
			if (m_done || m_comp == null || m_comp.crossesRoll || !IsRth() || tod < Cfg.from || tod > Cfg.to || !entryAllowed(out string _)) return;
			double tick = tickSize(), d = Cfg.deltaTicks * tick, h = Bars.High[0], l = Bars.Low[0], c = Bars.Close[0], mid = (h + l) / 2;
			foreach (Node z in m_comp.lvns)
			{
				int side = 0;
				if (l <= z.high && Bars.Close[1] > z.high && c > z.high + d && c >= mid) side = +1;       // support edge held
				else if (h >= z.low && Bars.Close[1] < z.low && c < z.low - d && c <= mid) side = -1;    // resistance edge held
				if (side == 0) continue;
				double stop = side > 0 ? Math.Min(l - tick, c - Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) : Math.Max(h + tick, c + Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick);
				double r = Math.Abs(c - stop), target = c + side * r;
				string why = null;
				if (Cfg.contracts * r * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction)) why = "risk budget";
				else if (Cfg.hvnVeto && side > 0 && hvnCeilingBelow(c, target)) why = "1R target beyond HVN ceiling";
				m_done = true;
				if (why != null) { logSignal("PS22", side, c, stop, null, false); if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-22 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} skip: {why}"); return; }
				m_stop = stop; m_target = target;
				logSignal("PS22", side, c, stop, null, true);
				if (side > 0) m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts); else m_se.Send(Cfg.contracts);
				return;
			}
		}

		private bool hvnCeilingBelow(double entry, double target)
		{
			double best = double.MaxValue;
			foreach (Node n in m_comp.hvns) if (n.low > entry && n.low < best) best = n.low;
			return best < target;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.29 Example 30 (`_Ex30RejectedRepricingOpen`) — PS-05 rejected repricing open (Keller IX-3; gap-up side shown in words, both sides coded) (medium–hard)

**Features (direct).** F-DEV (§5.1), F-PROF (§5.2), F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13), M-DAILY (§5.19).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> PS-05 rejected repricing open (Keller IX-3; gap-up side shown in words, both sides coded).
> Filter: RTH open outside the prior RTH value area by >= 0.25 x ATR20d, with |open - prior RTH close| <= 1.5 x ATR20d.
> Signal: by 10:15 ET, two 5m closes back inside the prior VA (gap up: close <= prior VAH). Entry: sell stop 1 tick through the low
> of the second inside bar (buy stop through the high for a gap down). Stop: max(session extreme + 1 tick, 14-tick floor).
> Target: prior POC (the "then opposite VA edge" leg is not coded: one target). The prior profile is skipped if it crosses a roll.
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: F-VP (PriorRthProfile), F-SESS (Session), M-DAILY (Atr20d), F-TM.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 30 - PS-05 rejected repricing open (Keller IX-3; gap-up side shown in words, both sides coded).
// Filter: RTH open outside the prior RTH value area by >= 0.25 x ATR20d, with |open - prior RTH close| <= 1.5 x ATR20d.
// Signal: by 10:15 ET, two 5m closes back inside the prior VA (gap up: close <= prior VAH). Entry: sell stop 1 tick through the low
// of the second inside bar (buy stop through the high for a gap down). Stop: max(session extreme + 1 tick, 14-tick floor).
// Target: prior POC (the "then opposite VA edge" leg is not coded: one target). The prior profile is skipped if it crosses a roll.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: F-VP (PriorRthProfile), F-SESS (Session), M-DAILY (Atr20d), F-TM.
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex30RejectedRepricingOpen : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Ps05Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, insideCloses = 2;
			public double minOutsideAtr = 0.25, maxGapAtr = 1.5;
			public TimeSpan signalBy = new TimeSpan(10, 15, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Ps05Config Cfg { get; } = new Ps05Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leStp, m_seStp, m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private IOrderMarket m_lx, m_sx;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private bool m_opened, m_done;
		private int m_side, m_inside;
		private double m_vah, m_val, m_poc, m_trigger = double.NaN, m_stop;

		public _Ex30RejectedRepricingOpen(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			DevelopingSource = EDevelopingSource.Own; OwnProfileWindow = EOwnProfileWindow.Rth;   // PriorRthProfile needs Own
			FlatBy = Cfg.flat;
			m_leStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS05LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_seStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS05SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS05LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS05SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS05LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS05SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS05LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS05ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { NeedsDaily = true; base.StartCalc(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) { m_day = day; m_opened = m_done = false; m_side = m_inside = 0; m_trigger = double.NaN; }
			if (rollOn(1)) m_done = true;
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				m_done = true;
				if (mustFlatten()) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_poc); } else { m_sxStp.Send(m_stop); m_sxLmt.Send(m_poc); }
				return;
			}
			if (m_done || !IsRth()) return;
			SessionStats s = Session;
			double tick = tickSize(), c = Bars.Close[0];
			if (!m_opened)
			{
				m_opened = true;                                   // first closed RTH bar: classify the open once
				VpExtra prior = PriorRthProfile();
				double atr = DailyContext != null ? DailyContext.Atr20d : double.NaN;
				if (prior == null || prior.crossesRoll || !(atr > 0) || s == null || !(s.rthOpen > 0)) { m_done = true; return; }
				m_vah = prior.vah; m_val = prior.val; m_poc = prior.poc;
				double o = s.rthOpen, gap = Math.Abs(o - s.priorRthClose);
				if (gap > Cfg.maxGapAtr * atr) { m_done = true; return; }
				m_side = o >= m_vah + Cfg.minOutsideAtr * atr ? -1 : o <= m_val - Cfg.minOutsideAtr * atr ? +1 : 0;   // fade side
				if (m_side == 0) { m_done = true; return; }
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(m_trigger))
			{
				if (ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay > Cfg.signalBy) { m_done = true; return; }
				bool inside = m_side < 0 ? c <= m_vah : c >= m_val;
				m_inside = inside ? m_inside + 1 : 0;
				if (m_inside < Cfg.insideCloses) return;
				double trig = m_side < 0 ? Bars.Low[0] - tick : Bars.High[0] + tick;
				double stop = m_side < 0 ? Math.Max(s.rthHigh + tick, trig + Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) : Math.Min(s.rthLow - tick, trig - Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick);
				if (m_side * (m_poc - trig) <= 0 || Cfg.contracts * Math.Abs(trig - stop) * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction))
				{ if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-05 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} skip: target behind trigger or risk budget"); m_done = true; return; }
				m_trigger = trig; m_stop = stop;
				if (journalOn()) journalArm(m_side > 0 ? "PS05LE" : "PS05SE", m_trigger, m_stop, m_poc, null);
			}
			if (m_side > 0) m_leStp.Send(m_trigger, Cfg.contracts); else m_seStp.Send(m_trigger, Cfg.contracts);
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.30 Example 38 (`_Ex38StackedImbalanceRetest`) — OF-01 stacked-imbalance origin, first retest continuation (long side shown; short mirrors) (medium–hard)

**Features (direct).** F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-OF (§5.9), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> OF-01 stacked-imbalance origin, first retest continuation (long side shown; short mirrors).
> Zone: a closed 5m bar with >= 3 stacked BUY imbalances (ratio 3:1, VPF-5 StackedImbalances) that closes in its upper 30%;
> zone = the lowest buy run [lo, hi]. Flow quality must be Exact or High (the catalogue warns TickRule imbalances are artefacts).
> Arming: price then moves >= 1 x ATR(14, 5m) above the zone top. Entry: first retest only - buy limit at the zone top
> (catalogue entry arm "limit"; the 1m HL arm is not coded). Stop: zone low - 1 tick, floored at 14 ticks. Target: 1R.
> Deviation: the catalogue's volume floor P70 is not computed; minVol = 10 contracts is a PLACEHOLDER. Zone expires at session end.
> Chart: Data1 @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: VPF order flow (OrderFlowEnabled, StackedImbalances, FlowQuality).

**Pitfalls.**

- Contains PLACEHOLDER values (marked in the code); calibrate them before reading any result.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 38 - OF-01 stacked-imbalance origin, first retest continuation (long side shown; short mirrors).
// Zone: a closed 5m bar with >= 3 stacked BUY imbalances (ratio 3:1, VPF-5 StackedImbalances) that closes in its upper 30%;
// zone = the lowest buy run [lo, hi]. Flow quality must be Exact or High (the catalogue warns TickRule imbalances are artefacts).
// Arming: price then moves >= 1 x ATR(14, 5m) above the zone top. Entry: first retest only - buy limit at the zone top
// (catalogue entry arm "limit"; the 1m HL arm is not coded). Stop: zone low - 1 tick, floored at 14 ticks. Target: 1R.
// Deviation: the catalogue's volume floor P70 is not computed; minVol = 10 contracts is a PLACEHOLDER. Zone expires at session end.
// Chart: Data1 @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: VPF order flow (OrderFlowEnabled, StackedImbalances, FlowQuality).
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex38StackedImbalanceRetest : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Of01Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, minStack = 3, atrLength = 14, costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double ratio = 3.0, minVol = 10 /* PLACEHOLDER for P70 */, closeTopFrac = 0.30, awayAtr = 1.0, targetR = 1.0, riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
			public TimeSpan flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
		}
		protected Of01Config Cfg { get; } = new Of01Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leLmt, m_lxStp, m_lxLmt;
		private IOrderMarket m_lx;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private bool m_zone, m_armed, m_used;
		private double m_zLo, m_zHi, m_stop, m_target;

		public _Ex38StackedImbalanceRetest(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			OrderFlowEnabled = true;                          // lazy: footprints only exist when enabled
			FlatBy = Cfg.flat;
			m_leLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "OF01LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "OF01LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "OF01LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "OF01LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { base.StartCalc(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) { m_day = day; m_zone = m_armed = m_used = false; }
			if (rollOn(1)) m_zone = false;
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				m_used = true;
				if (mustFlatten()) { m_lx.Send(); return; }
				m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target);
				return;
			}
			if (!IsRth()) return;
			double tick = tickSize(), h = Bars.High[0], l = Bars.Low[0], c = Bars.Close[0];
			double atr = averageTrueRange(Bars, Cfg.atrLength);
			if (m_zone && !m_used)
			{
				if (!m_armed && h >= m_zHi + Cfg.awayAtr * atr) m_armed = true;
				else if (m_armed && l <= m_zHi) { m_used = true; m_zone = false; return; }   // the retest happened without a fill: first retest only
				if (m_armed && !mustFlatten()) { m_leLmt.Send(m_zHi, Cfg.contracts); return; }
			}
			if (m_zone || m_used || h - l <= 0 || (c - l) / (h - l) < 1 - Cfg.closeTopFrac) return;
			_OrderFlow.StackedResult s = StackedImbalances(0, Cfg.ratio, Cfg.minVol, Cfg.minStack);
			if (!s.Found || s.Quality > _OrderFlow.FlowQuality.High) return;
			foreach (double[] run in s.Runs)
			{
				if (run[3] <= 0) continue;                      // buy runs first, low -> high: take the lowest
				double stop = run[0] - tick, entry = run[1];
				if (entry - stop < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) stop = entry - Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick;
				double r = entry - stop;
				if (Cfg.contracts * r * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction))
				{ if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"OF-01 {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} skip: risk budget"); return; }
				m_zLo = run[0]; m_zHi = run[1]; m_stop = stop; m_target = entry + Cfg.targetR * r; m_zone = true; m_armed = false;
				if (journalOn()) journalArm("OF01LE", entry, m_stop, m_target, null);
				return;
			}
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.31 Example 31 (`_Ex31MultiTfStack`) — MT-01 multi-timeframe stack (long only, as catalogued) (medium–hard)

**Features (direct).** F-DEV (§5.1), F-COMP (§5.3), F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13), M-SWING (§5.16), M-DAILY (§5.19).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> MT-01 multi-timeframe stack (long only, as catalogued).
> Filter: M-DAILY Tsmom(20) > 0; last closed 60m structure Up (HH/HL) from a second PRICEACTION engine on Data3; the latest 15m
> pivot low (third engine, Data2) lies within delta15 of the 20-session composite VAH or the developing RTH POC
> (delta15 = 0.25 x the 15m engine ATR: PLACEHOLDER, not a catalogue value; swing-AVWAP level omitted).
> Entry: the next 5m pivot low labelled HL (Data1 engine), buy stop 1 tick above the confirming bar. Stop: max(HL - 1 tick, floor).
> Target: the last 15m pivot high. RTH 09:45-15:00 ET; one trade per day.
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m, Data2 = @ES 15m, Data3 = @ES 60m (same symbol, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED).
> Features: M-PA (PriceActionConfig + ExtraPriceAction + PriceActionFor, Structure), M-DAILY (Tsmom), F-COMP, F-VP (RthProfile).

**Pitfalls.**

- Contains PLACEHOLDER values (marked in the code); calibrate them before reading any result.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 31 - MT-01 multi-timeframe stack (long only, as catalogued).
// Filter: M-DAILY Tsmom(20) > 0; last closed 60m structure Up (HH/HL) from a second PRICEACTION engine on Data3; the latest 15m
// pivot low (third engine, Data2) lies within delta15 of the 20-session composite VAH or the developing RTH POC
// (delta15 = 0.25 x the 15m engine ATR: PLACEHOLDER, not a catalogue value; swing-AVWAP level omitted).
// Entry: the next 5m pivot low labelled HL (Data1 engine), buy stop 1 tick above the confirming bar. Stop: max(HL - 1 tick, floor).
// Target: the last 15m pivot high. RTH 09:45-15:00 ET; one trade per day.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m, Data2 = @ES 15m, Data3 = @ES 60m (same symbol, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED).
// Features: M-PA (PriceActionConfig + ExtraPriceAction + PriceActionFor, Structure), M-DAILY (Tsmom), F-COMP, F-VP (RthProfile).
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex31MultiTfStack : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Mt01Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, tsmomDays = 20, n = 20, ds15 = 2, ds60 = 3;
			public double nearAtr15 = 0.25;   // PLACEHOLDER
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), to = new TimeSpan(15, 0, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Mt01Config Cfg { get; } = new Mt01Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leStp, m_lxStp, m_lxLmt;
		private IOrderMarket m_lx;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private VpExtra m_comp;
		private bool m_done;
		private double m_trigger = double.NaN, m_stop, m_target;

		public _Ex31MultiTfStack(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			DevelopingSource = EDevelopingSource.Own; OwnProfileWindow = EOwnProfileWindow.Rth;
			PriceActionConfig.enabled = true; PriceActionConfig.dataStream = 1;                     // 5m trigger engine
			ExtraPriceAction.Add(new PriceActionConfig { enabled = true, dataStream = Cfg.ds15, role = "pb15" });
			ExtraPriceAction.Add(new PriceActionConfig { enabled = true, dataStream = Cfg.ds60, role = "trend60" });
			FlatBy = Cfg.flat; MaxTradesPerDay = 1;
			m_leStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "MT01LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "MT01LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "MT01LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "MT01LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { NeedsDaily = true; base.StartCalc(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) { m_day = day; m_done = false; m_trigger = double.NaN; m_comp = Composite(1, Cfg.n); }
			if (rollOn(1)) m_done = true;
			if (StrategyInfo.MarketPosition > 0)
			{
				m_done = true;
				if (mustFlatten()) { m_lx.Send(); return; }
				m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); return;
			}
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
			if (m_done || !IsRth() || tod < Cfg.from || tod > Cfg.to) return;
			if (double.IsNaN(m_trigger)) arm(day);
			if (!double.IsNaN(m_trigger)) m_leStp.Send(m_trigger, Cfg.contracts);
		}

		private void arm(DateTime day)
		{
			_PriceActionEngine pa5 = PriceActionEngine, pa15 = PriceActionFor(Cfg.ds15, "pb15"), pa60 = PriceActionFor(Cfg.ds60, "trend60");
			if (pa5 == null || pa15 == null || pa60 == null || !pa5.IsWarm || !pa15.IsWarm || !pa60.IsWarm || !pa5.NewPivotLow) return;
			double ts = DailyContext != null ? DailyContext.Tsmom(Cfg.tsmomDays) : double.NaN;
			if (!(ts > 0) || pa60.Structure(0) != EStructure.Up) return;
			_Pivot pb = pa15.LastLow(0), sh = pa15.LastHigh(0), hl = pa5.LastLow(0);
			if (pb == null || sh == null || hl == null || hl.label != EPivotLabel.HL) return;
			VpExtra dev = RthProfile();
			double near = Cfg.nearAtr15 * pa15.Atr;
			bool atLevel = (m_comp != null && Math.Abs(pb.price - m_comp.vah) <= near) || (dev != null && dev.poc > 0 && Math.Abs(pb.price - dev.poc) <= near);
			if (!atLevel) return;
			double tick = tickSize(), trig = Bars.High[0] + tick, stop = Math.Min(hl.price - tick, trig - Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick);
			string why = sh.price <= trig ? "15m swing high below trigger" : Cfg.contracts * (trig - stop) * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction) ? "risk budget" : null;
			if (why != null) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"MT-01 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} skip: {why}"); m_done = true; return; }
			m_trigger = trig; m_stop = stop; m_target = sh.price;
			if (journalOn()) journalArm("MT01LE", m_trigger, m_stop, m_target, null);
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.32 Example 35 (`_Ex35PocStabilityGate`) — A2-25 POC bootstrap-stability gate + A2-26 KDE bandwidth-sweep veto, as a module on the DV-02 host (Ex32) (medium–hard)

**Features (direct).** M-GMM (§5.25), VP helpers (§5.27). **Inherited from example 32:** F-DEV, F-PROF, F-SESS, F-ROLL, F-CAL, F-TM, F-JRNL, F-EVT, M-DAILY.

**What it does** (from the file header):

> A2-25 POC bootstrap-stability gate + A2-26 KDE bandwidth-sweep veto, as a module on the DV-02 host (Ex32).
> After the prior close, the prior RTH session's Data1 ring bars (SessionBars) feed _ProfileStats.BootstrapPoc (block 5, 500 draws,
> deterministic BootstrapSeed) and _ProfileStats.KdeSweep. Rules: KDE verdict FLAT or BIMODAL -> no trade (A2-26);
> stability >= 0.60 -> trade, stop distance = max(host bracket, ci90/2 + 1 tick) (A2-25); 0.25 <= stability < 0.60 -> the catalogue
> allows the trade only with the POC ZONE as the level; the DV-02 host is line-based, so this module skips that bucket (stated, not
> a catalogue rule); stability < 0.25 -> skip. Skip if the widened stop makes the target < 1R (the DV-02 bracket is symmetric, so the
> target is widened with it and stays 1R). Cut-offs 0.60/0.25 and block 5 are catalogue grid points.
> Features: F-PSTAT (ProfileStats.enabled -> ring, SessionBars, BootstrapPoc, BootstrapSeed, KdeSweep, EKdeVerdict), host hook.

**Pitfalls.**

- Module on example 32: compile both files; the host's rules, stops and settings apply unless overridden.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 35 - A2-25 POC bootstrap-stability gate + A2-26 KDE bandwidth-sweep veto, as a module on the DV-02 host (Ex32).
// After the prior close, the prior RTH session's Data1 ring bars (SessionBars) feed _ProfileStats.BootstrapPoc (block 5, 500 draws,
// deterministic BootstrapSeed) and _ProfileStats.KdeSweep. Rules: KDE verdict FLAT or BIMODAL -> no trade (A2-26);
// stability >= 0.60 -> trade, stop distance = max(host bracket, ci90/2 + 1 tick) (A2-25); 0.25 <= stability < 0.60 -> the catalogue
// allows the trade only with the POC ZONE as the level; the DV-02 host is line-based, so this module skips that bucket (stated, not
// a catalogue rule); stability < 0.25 -> skip. Skip if the widened stop makes the target < 1R (the DV-02 bracket is symmetric, so the
// target is widened with it and stays 1R). Cut-offs 0.60/0.25 and block 5 are catalogue grid points.
// Features: F-PSTAT (ProfileStats.enabled -> ring, SessionBars, BootstrapPoc, BootstrapSeed, KdeSweep, EKdeVerdict), host hook.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex35PocStabilityGate : _Ex32FailedLevelFlip
	{
		protected double StabilityHigh { get; set; } = 0.60;
		protected double StabilityLow { get; set; } = 0.25;

		public _Ex35PocStabilityGate(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			ProfileStats.enabled = true;                      // lazy: requires the Data1 ring (ProfileStats.ringBars) and range profiles
			ProfileStats.bootstrapBlock = 5; ProfileStats.bootstrapDraws = 500;
		}

		protected override bool pocGate(DateTime day, double poc, ref double bracket, out string why)
		{
			why = null;
			DateTime prev = priorTradingDay(day);
			List<RingBar> bars = prev == DateTime.MinValue ? null : SessionBars(prev, true);
			if (bars == null || bars.Count < 20) { why = "no prior RTH ring bars"; return false; }
			double tick = tickSize();
			KdeResult kde = _ProfileStats.KdeSweep(bars, tick, 2, poc, ProfileStats.kdeMedFloorTicks, ProfileStats.kdeFolds, ProfileStats.kdeFolding);
			if (kde.verdict == EKdeVerdict.Flat || kde.verdict == EKdeVerdict.Bimodal) { why = "KDE " + kde.verdict; return false; }
			int block = ProfileStats.bootstrapBlock, draws = ProfileStats.bootstrapDraws;
			BootstrapResult b = _ProfileStats.BootstrapPoc(bars, tick, 2, block, draws, _ProfileStats.BootstrapSeed(prev, block, draws));
			if (double.IsNaN(b.stability)) { why = "bootstrap n/a"; return false; }
			if (b.stability < StabilityHigh) { why = $"stability {b.stability:F2} < {StabilityHigh:F2} (zone bucket not handled by a line host)"; return false; }
			bracket = Math.Max(bracket, b.ci90Ticks / 2 * tick + tick);
			if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-25 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} stability {b.stability:F2} ci90 {b.ci90Ticks:F0}t KDE {kde.verdict} bracket {bracket:F2}");
			return true;
		}

		private DateTime priorTradingDay(DateTime day)
		{
			int maxAgo = Math.Min(ProfileStats.ringBars, Bars.CurrentBar - 1);   // ringBarOf reads chart history: stay inside MaxBarsBack
			for (int ago = 0; ago < maxAgo; ago++)
			{
				RingBar rb = ringBarOf(1, ago);
				if (rb.tradingDay < day) return rb.tradingDay;
			}
			return DateTime.MinValue;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.33 Example 7 (`_Ex07RotationVolumeLevel`) — PS-13 / DV-03 rotation POC first-touch retest (hard)

**Features (direct).** F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-JRNL (§5.13), F-EVT (§5.14), M-MTF (§5.17), VP helpers (§5.27).


**Catalogue rules (PS-13).** Filter: a rotation of ≥ 6 bars on 15m with range ≤ 1.5 × ATR15m, then a breakout bar closing outside with range ≥ 1.5 × ATR15m. Level: the highest-volume price in the rotation. Entry: first touch (limit) or first 5m HL after the touch; first touch only; level valid 5 sessions intraday. Stop: 10–20% of the 200-day ATR, floored. Target: before the next cluster. DV-03 (merged) adds a follow-through of ≥ 1 box width before any retest. **G0-d-gated** (family 10).

**Chart.** Data1 = @ES 1m, Data2 = @ES 15m, dxFeed unadjusted, 24h. `RangeProfiles.enabled = true`, RollDetection Series. This codes the **limit arm** of PS-13.

**Walk-through.**
- On each new closed 15m bar: ATR15 over the 14 bars before the breakout bar; the box = the high/low of the previous 6 closed 15m bars; reject if the box is taller than 1.5 × ATR15.
- The breakout bar must close outside the box with a range ≥ 1.5 × ATR15.
- `RangeProfileAfter(1, boxStart, boxEnd)` builds the 1m volume profile over the box only, and its POC is the level. `RangeStatsOf` must show coverage ≥ 0.95.
- Stop = level ∓ 0.10 × ATR200d; target = level ± 2R. Risk = max(0.10 × ATR200d, 14-tick cost floor `costFloorTicks`); stop and target use the widened risk. If it breaches the per-trade risk budget (`riskBudgetFraction` = 0.01, 1% of equity, decided by Ettienne 2026-10-23; PF-04's 0.5% cap is not the default for the floor rule), the level is skipped and journaled. Size is never shrunk.
- **Entry window (catalogue §2.1):** the limit is sent only on bar closes from 09:45 to before 15:30 ET (and before an early-close flat time), so it can fill only between 09:45 and 15:30. A position is closed at market at 15:55, or at `RthCloseEt` − 5 min on an early-close day (`flatAt`).
- A buy (sell) limit rests at the level. If a later bar reaches the level and the position is still flat, the level is spent. Once filled, the level is cleared, so it is never traded twice. It also expires after 5 sessions and is cleared at a roll. A touch outside the entry window, with no limit resting (overnight, for example), also spends the level.
- `justArmed` is true only on the bar that armed the level, so that bar's own range can't count as the first touch.
- **ATR200d is computed in the strategy**, not by M-DAILY: per-Globex-day true range, with the roll day's gap dropped, simple average of 200 days. M-DAILY with `atrLength = 200` and RollDetection on would reseed at every roll (about every 63 sessions), so it would never be valid.

**Pitfalls.**
- **Assumptions (labelled):** ATR15 length 14; coverage 0.95; a fixed 2R target as a stand-in for "before the next cluster".
- **Not coded:** DV-03's follow-through condition, the 5m HL entry arm and the near-HVN-edge level variant.
- The box start is the open time of the oldest box bar. The profile is "after" that time, assuming bars are stamped at their end (Probe P2, **VERIFY**).
- A bar that touches the level without a fill (for example with MC's "fill only when price trades beyond the limit") spends the level.
- **Coding choice (labelled):** a touch outside 09:45–15:30 spends the level (first touch only). The other reading would keep it for the next session's window.
- Stop and target are sent from the bar after the fill (§1.7), so the fill bar has no working exit.

**Full strategy** — `_Ex07RotationVolumeLevel.Strategy.CS`:

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 7 - PS-13 / DV-03 rotation -> breakout -> first touch of the rotation's highest-volume price (limit arm).
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m, Data2 = @ES 15m (same symbol, 24h Globex), dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Range profiles ON (RangeProfiles.enabled).
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex07RotationVolumeLevel : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Ps13Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1;
			public int rotationBars = 6, atr15Length = 14;   // ATR15m length is not in the catalogue: an assumption
			public double boxAtr = 1.5, breakoutAtr = 1.5;
			public int atrDailyLength = 200;
			public double stopFraction = 0.10;                 // sweep {0.10, 0.20} x ATR200d
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;                     // catalogue 2.1: ES cost floor; stop distance = max(structural, floor), size never shrunk
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;  // floor-rule risk budget: Ettienne, 2026-10-23: 1% of equity (PF-04's 0.5% is not the default here)
			public int validSessions = 5;
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), to = new TimeSpan(15, 30, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);   // catalogue 2.1 intraday rules
			public TimeSpan halfDayExitBefore = new TimeSpan(0, 5, 0);   // early-close days: flat at RthCloseEt - 5 min
			public double targetR = 2.0;                        // "before the next cluster" is not coded here: a fixed-R stand-in (coding choice)
		}
		protected Ps13Config Cfg { get; } = new Ps13Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leLmt, m_seLmt, m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private IOrderMarket m_lx, m_sx;
		private DateTime m_last15 = DateTime.MinValue;
		// own ATR200d: per-Globex-day TR with the roll day's TR dropped (M-DAILY reseeds its ATR at every roll, see section 4)
		private readonly List<double> m_tr = new List<double>();
		private DateTime m_aggDay = DateTime.MinValue; private double m_dH, m_dL, m_dC = double.NaN, m_prevC = double.NaN; private bool m_dRoll;
		private double m_level = double.NaN; private int m_side; private DateTime m_levelDay; private int m_levelSessions;
		private double m_stop = double.NaN, m_target = double.NaN;

		/// <summary>Catalogue 2.1 / App F #9 (resolved): true when Cfg.contracts at this stop distance risk more than the per-trade budget.</summary>
		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }
		public _Ex07RotationVolumeLevel(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			RangeProfiles.enabled = true;              // M-RANGE: needs the bar ring (ringBars 2880 = two days of 1m bars)
			m_leLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS13LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_seLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS13SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS13LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS13SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS13LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS13ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS13LX", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS13SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			HtfStreams = new[] { 2 };
			base.StartCalc();
			m_last15 = m_aggDay = DateTime.MinValue; m_tr.Clear(); m_dC = m_prevC = double.NaN; m_dRoll = false;
			m_level = m_stop = m_target = double.NaN; m_side = 0; m_levelSessions = 0;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime t = Bars.Time[0], day = GlobexDate(t);
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(t).TimeOfDay, flat = flatAt(day);
			bool roll = rollOn(1);
			stepDailyAtr(day, roll);
			if (roll) { m_level = double.NaN; m_side = 0; }       // multi-day rotations reset at rolls

			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				m_level = double.NaN; m_side = 0;                // filled: the level is spent (first touch only, no re-entry)
				if (tod >= flat && tod < new TimeSpan(17, 0, 0)) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); } else { m_sxStp.Send(m_stop); m_sxLmt.Send(m_target); }
				return;
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(m_level) && m_side != 0 && m_levelSessions > Cfg.validSessions) { m_level = double.NaN; m_side = 0; }

			step15();

			// first touch only: the resting limit IS the touch; once price reaches the level without a fill the level is spent.
			// A touch outside the entry window (no limit resting) also spends it.
			if (!double.IsNaN(m_level) && m_side != 0)
			{
				bool traded = m_side > 0 ? Bars.Low[0] <= m_level : Bars.High[0] >= m_level;
				if (traded && Bars.CurrentBar > 1 && !justArmed) { m_level = double.NaN; m_side = 0; return; }
				justArmed = false;
				// entries 09:45-15:30 ET only (and before an early-close flat time): the limit sent on this close works on the next bar
				bool window = tod >= Cfg.from && tod < Cfg.to && tod < flat;
				if (window) { if (m_side > 0) m_leLmt.Send(m_level, Cfg.contracts); else m_seLmt.Send(m_level, Cfg.contracts); }
			}
		}
		// true only on the bar that armed the level: that 1m bar belongs to the breakout, so its own range must not count
		// as the first touch and use up the level it just created
		private bool justArmed;

		/// <summary>New CLOSED 15m bar: rotation of the previous rotationBars bars, breakout on this bar, level = POC of the box.</summary>
		private void step15()
		{
			DateTime tb; double ob, hb, lb, cb, vb;
			HtfClosedBar(2, 0, out tb, out ob, out hb, out lb, out cb, out vb);
			if (tb == m_last15) return;
			m_last15 = tb;
			int n = Cfg.rotationBars;
			if (BarsOfData(2).CurrentBar <= n + Cfg.atr15Length + 2) return;
			double atr15 = atrClosed(2, Cfg.atr15Length, 1);   // ATR of the bars BEFORE the breakout bar
			double boxH = double.MinValue, boxL = double.MaxValue; DateTime boxStart = DateTime.MinValue, boxEnd = DateTime.MinValue;
			for (int k = 1; k <= n; k++)
			{
				DateTime tk; double ok, hk, lk, ck, vk;
				HtfClosedBar(2, k, out tk, out ok, out hk, out lk, out ck, out vk);
				boxH = Math.Max(boxH, hk); boxL = Math.Min(boxL, lk);
				if (k == 1) boxEnd = tk;
			}
			{ DateTime tk; double ok, hk, lk, ck, vk; HtfClosedBar(2, n + 1, out tk, out ok, out hk, out lk, out ck, out vk); boxStart = tk; }
			if (!(atr15 > 0) || boxH - boxL > Cfg.boxAtr * atr15) return;
			int side = cb > boxH ? +1 : cb < boxL ? -1 : 0;
			if (side == 0 || hb - lb < Cfg.breakoutAtr * atr15) return;
			// 1m volume-at-price over the box only: bars ending in (boxStart, boxEnd] (exclusive t0, end-stamped bars, Probe P2)
			VpExtra e = RangeProfileAfter(1, boxStart, boxEnd);
			if (e == null || !(e.poc > 0)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-13 {tb:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} no range profile"); return; }
			RangeStats st = RangeStatsOf(e);
			if (st == null || !(st.coverage >= 0.95)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-13 {tb:HH:mm} box coverage {(st == null ? double.NaN : st.coverage):F2} < 0.95: skip"); return; }
			double atrD = atrDaily();
			if (!(atrD > 0)) return;
			double tick = tickSize();
			double risk = Math.Max(Cfg.stopFraction * atrD, Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick);   // catalogue 2.1: stop = max(structural, cost floor)
			if (overRiskBudget(risk))   // widened stop breaches the per-trade risk budget: skipped and logged, not resized
			{
				if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-13 {tb:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} skip: stop {risk / tick:F0} ticks breaches risk budget {Cfg.riskBudgetFraction:P2}");
				return;
			}
			m_level = e.poc; m_side = side; m_levelDay = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]); m_levelSessions = 0; justArmed = true;
			m_stop = m_level - side * risk; m_target = m_level + side * Cfg.targetR * risk;
			var ctx = new Dictionary<string, string> { { "boxH", boxH.ToString() }, { "boxL", boxL.ToString() }, { "atr15", atr15.ToString("F2") }, { "atrD", atrD.ToString("F2") } };
			if (journalOn()) { journalArm(side > 0 ? "PS13LE" : "PS13SE", m_level, m_stop, m_target, ctx); logSignal("PS-13", side, m_level, m_stop, ctx, true); }
		}

		private void stepDailyAtr(DateTime day, bool roll)
		{
			if (day != m_aggDay)
			{
				if (m_aggDay != DateTime.MinValue)
				{
					if (!m_dRoll && !double.IsNaN(m_prevC)) m_tr.Add(Math.Max(m_dH - m_dL, Math.Max(Math.Abs(m_dH - m_prevC), Math.Abs(m_dL - m_prevC))));
					else m_tr.Add(m_dH - m_dL);                    // roll day: the gap to the old contract is not range
					if (m_tr.Count > Cfg.atrDailyLength) m_tr.RemoveAt(0);
					m_prevC = m_dC;
					if (!double.IsNaN(m_level)) m_levelSessions++;
				}
				m_aggDay = day; m_dH = Bars.High[0]; m_dL = Bars.Low[0]; m_dRoll = false;
			}
			m_dH = Math.Max(m_dH, Bars.High[0]); m_dL = Math.Min(m_dL, Bars.Low[0]); m_dC = Bars.Close[0];
			if (roll) { m_dRoll = true; m_prevC = double.NaN; }
		}
		private double atrDaily()
		{
			if (m_tr.Count < Cfg.atrDailyLength) return double.NaN;
			double s = 0; foreach (double x in m_tr) s += x; return s / m_tr.Count;
		}

		private double atrClosed(int ds, int len, int from)
		{
			double sum = 0;
			for (int k = from; k < from + len; k++)
			{
				DateTime t; double o, h, l, c, v, pc;
				HtfClosedBar(ds, k + 1, out t, out o, out h, out l, out pc, out v);
				HtfClosedBar(ds, k, out t, out o, out h, out l, out c, out v);
				sum += Math.Max(h - l, Math.Max(Math.Abs(h - pc), Math.Abs(l - pc)));
			}
			return sum / len;
		}

		/// <summary>Flat time for a Globex day: Cfg.flat, or on a CME early-close day RthCloseEt - halfDayExitBefore if earlier
		/// (_CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day).RthCloseEt; same rule as A263Config.halfDayExitBefore).</summary>
		private TimeSpan flatAt(DateTime day)
		{
			_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day);
			TimeSpan f = Cfg.flat;
			if (cme.RthCloseEt.HasValue && cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore < f) f = cme.RthCloseEt.Value - Cfg.halfDayExitBefore;
			return f;
		}
	}
}
```

**Compile status:** compiled — type-checked with Roslyn against the repo sources (`origin/main` a07257a) plus MC API stubs (stubs not authoritative). Not run in MultiCharts; no backtest results.

### 3.34 Example 10 (`_Ex10WeeklyValueAcceptance`) — MT-02 weekly accepted value + 60m trigger (hard)

**Features (direct).** F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-JRNL (§5.13), F-EVT (§5.14), M-MTF (§5.17), M-DAILY (§5.19), VP helpers (§5.27).


**Catalogue rules (MT-02).** Filter: by Tuesday's close, ≥ 2 daily closes above the prior-week VAH. Entry: the first pullback to prior-week VAH ± 0.25 × ATR20d with a 60m higher low, Wednesday–Friday. Stop: back inside the prior-week VA by 0.25 × weekly VA height, floored. Target: prior-week VAH + 1 × weekly VA height. Time stop 7 sessions. Sweep: acceptance days {1, 2} × target {1 × height, trail}. Multi-day levels reset at rolls. **G0-d-gated** (family 7).

**Chart.** Data1 = @ES 1m, Data2 = @ES 60m, dxFeed unadjusted, 24h. `WeeklyProfiles.enabled = true` (RTH sessions, roll policy Reset, minSessions 3), `NeedsDaily = true`, RollDetection Series. `[Input] AcceptanceDays` 1 or 2 (default 2).

**Walk-through.**
- The prior week is looked up by **key** (`_WeeklyProfiles.WeekKeyOf`, `WeekKey(i)`, `Week(i)`). A week counts as completed only when the next week's first session completes, so on Monday the prior week may still be `Week(0)`. Weeks that cross a roll, are partial, or have no valid VA are skipped.
- Acceptance: on Monday and Tuesday the RTH close (the 1m bar close-stamped 16:00 ET, or the early close) is compared with the prior-week VAH.
- From Wednesday to Friday during RTH: without enough acceptance closes, the week is done. Otherwise the zone is VAH ± 0.25 × `DailyContext.Atr20d`. The first bar that overlaps it marks the touch.
- Trigger: the first new closed 60m bar after the touch whose low is above the prior 60m low. The entry is a market order; stop = VAH − 0.25 × height; target = VAH + 1 × height. If the R from the signal close (close − stop) is under the 14-tick cost floor (`costFloorTicks`), the stop is widened to close − 14 ticks (the target is unchanged). If the widened stop breaches the per-trade risk budget (`riskBudgetFraction` = 0.01, 1% of equity, decided by Ettienne 2026-10-23; PF-04's 0.5% cap is not the default for the floor rule), the week is skipped and journaled. Size is never shrunk.
- **Zone break:** after the touch and before the 60m trigger, any RTH 1m bar that closes below the zone (VAH − 0.25 × ATR20d) cancels the setup for the rest of the week. That is very strict, since one 1m close is enough. It is a coding choice the catalogue doesn't make (**VERIFY** against intent; Appendix F).
- A roll during the week cancels the setup. If `Atr20d` is NaN the week is skipped. The time stop exits after 7 sessions held.

**Pitfalls.**
- **Assumptions (labelled):** a close below the zone kills the week's setup (**VERIFY** against intent); "60m HL" read as a 60m bar whose low is above the prior 60m bar's low. The v2 8-tick floor is gone: the stop is the structural 0.25 × height, and the catalogue's 14-tick cost floor widens it when it is shorter; the week is skipped only on a risk-budget breach.
- Stop and target are sent from the bar after the fill (§1.7), so the fill bar has no working exit.
- Acceptance uses the RTH close (16:00 ET or the early close); being a swing idea, the example does not apply the half-day flat.
- `Atr20d` is NaN for 20 sessions after each roll (§4.3), so weeks right after a roll are skipped and journaled.
- The trail target arm is not coded.

**Full strategy** — `_Ex10WeeklyValueAcceptance.Strategy.CS`:

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 10 - MT-02 weekly accepted value + 60m trigger (swing, long side as written in the catalogue).
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 1m, Data2 = @ES 60m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED, 24h CME Globex template. Weekly profiles ON (RTH sessions).
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex10WeeklyValueAcceptance : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		[Input] public int AcceptanceDays { get; set; }   // sweep {1, 2}; catalogue default 2

		protected sealed class Mt02Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1;
			public double zoneAtr = 0.25, stopHeight = 0.25, targetHeight = 1.0;
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;                    // catalogue 2.1: ES cost floor; stop distance = max(structural, floor), size never shrunk
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;  // floor-rule risk budget: Ettienne, 2026-10-23: 1% of equity (PF-04's 0.5% is not the default here)
			public int timeStopSessions = 7;
		}
		protected Mt02Config Cfg { get; } = new Mt02Config();

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_lxTime; private IOrderPriced m_lxStp, m_lxLmt;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue, m_week = DateTime.MinValue, m_last60 = DateTime.MinValue;
		private double m_vah = double.NaN, m_height = double.NaN;
		private int m_accept; private bool m_touched, m_doneWeek, m_rollThisWeek;
		private double m_stop, m_target; private int m_heldSessions;

		/// <summary>Catalogue 2.1 / App F #9 (resolved): true when Cfg.contracts at this stop distance risk more than the per-trade budget.</summary>
		private bool overRiskBudget(double stopDist) { return Cfg.contracts * stopDist * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction); }
		public _Ex10WeeklyValueAcceptance(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { AcceptanceDays = 2; }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			NeedsDaily = true;                               // Atr20d (NaN for ~20 sessions after each roll: those weeks are skipped)
			WeeklyProfiles.enabled = true;                   // source RthSessions, rollPolicy Reset, minSessions 3
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "MT02LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_lxTime = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "MT02LX", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "MT02LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "MT02LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc()
		{
			if (AcceptanceDays < 1 || AcceptanceDays > 2) throw new ArgumentException("AcceptanceDays must be 1 or 2");
			HtfStreams = new[] { 2 };
			base.StartCalc();
			m_day = m_week = m_last60 = DateTime.MinValue; m_vah = m_height = double.NaN; m_heldSessions = 0;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime t = Bars.Time[0], et = ToEastern(t), day = GlobexDate(t);
			bool roll = rollOn(1);
			bool newDay = day != m_day;
			if (newDay) { m_day = day; if (StrategyInfo.MarketPosition > 0) m_heldSessions++; }
			DateTime wk = _WeeklyProfiles.WeekKeyOf(day);
			if (wk != m_week) { m_week = wk; m_vah = double.NaN; m_accept = 0; m_touched = m_doneWeek = m_rollThisWeek = false; }
			if (roll) m_rollThisWeek = true;                 // multi-day levels reset at rolls: no new setup this week

			if (StrategyInfo.MarketPosition > 0)
			{
				if (m_heldSessions >= Cfg.timeStopSessions) { m_lxTime.Send(); return; }
				m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target);
				return;
			}
			m_heldSessions = 0;
			if (m_doneWeek || m_rollThisWeek) return;
			if (double.IsNaN(m_vah) && !loadPriorWeek(wk)) return;

			DayOfWeek dow = day.DayOfWeek;
			TimeSpan tod = et.TimeOfDay;
			bool rth = isRthBar(t);
			_CmeHolidays.Rules.Day cme = _CmeHolidays.Rules.Get(day);
			TimeSpan closeBar = cme.RthCloseEt ?? new TimeSpan(16, 0, 0);   // the RTH close bar (close-stamped 16:00 ET, or the early close)
			// acceptance: RTH closes (the 16:00 ET bar close on 1m) above the prior-week VAH, Monday and Tuesday
			if ((dow == DayOfWeek.Monday || dow == DayOfWeek.Tuesday) && rth && tod == closeBar && Bars.Close[0] > m_vah) m_accept++;
			if (dow < DayOfWeek.Wednesday || dow > DayOfWeek.Friday || cme.NoRth || !rth) return;
			if (m_accept < AcceptanceDays) { m_doneWeek = true; journal("no acceptance"); return; }

			double atr = DailyContext != null ? DailyContext.Atr20d : double.NaN;
			if (!(atr > 0)) { m_doneWeek = true; journal("Atr20d NaN (post-roll seeding)"); return; }
			double zLo = m_vah - Cfg.zoneAtr * atr, zHi = m_vah + Cfg.zoneAtr * atr;
			if (!m_touched && Bars.Low[0] <= zHi && Bars.High[0] >= zLo) m_touched = true;   // first pullback into the zone
			if (!m_touched) return;
			if (Bars.Close[0] < zLo) { m_doneWeek = true; journal("pullback broke the zone"); return; }   // coding choice (VERIFY)

			// trigger: the first new CLOSED 60m bar after the touch whose low is above the prior 60m low (a 60m higher low)
			DateTime t60; double o, h, l, c, v, o1, h1, l1, c1, v1;
			HtfClosedBar(2, 0, out t60, out o, out h, out l, out c, out v);
			if (t60 == m_last60) return;
			m_last60 = t60;
			DateTime tPrev; HtfClosedBar(2, 1, out tPrev, out o1, out h1, out l1, out c1, out v1);
			if (!(l > l1)) return;
			m_stop = m_vah - Cfg.stopHeight * m_height; m_target = m_vah + Cfg.targetHeight * m_height;
			if (Bars.Close[0] <= m_stop || Bars.Close[0] >= m_target) { m_doneWeek = true; return; }
			double r = Bars.Close[0] - m_stop;   // R from the planned (signal-close) entry
			if (r < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tickSize()) { r = Cfg.costFloorTicks * tickSize(); m_stop = Bars.Close[0] - r; }   // catalogue 2.1: widen to the cost floor
			if (overRiskBudget(r)) { m_doneWeek = true; journal($"skip: stop {r / tickSize():F0} ticks breaches risk budget {Cfg.riskBudgetFraction:P2}"); return; }   // not resized
			m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts); m_doneWeek = true;
			var ctx = new Dictionary<string, string> { { "vah", m_vah.ToString() }, { "height", m_height.ToString() }, { "atr20d", atr.ToString("F2") }, { "accept", m_accept.ToString() } };
			if (journalOn()) { journalArm("MT02LE", Bars.Close[0], m_stop, m_target, ctx); logSignal("MT-02", +1, Bars.Close[0], m_stop, ctx, true); }
		}

		/// <summary>Prior-week profile by KEY: a week is completed only when the next week's first session completes,
		/// so on Monday the prior week is still Week(0) and Week(1) is two weeks back.</summary>
		private bool loadPriorWeek(DateTime wk)
		{
			DateTime prior = wk.AddDays(-7);
			VpExtra e = WeekKey(0) == prior ? Week(0) : WeekKey(1) == prior ? Week(1) : null;
			if (e == null || e.crossesRoll || e.isPartial || !(e.vah > 0) || !(e.val > 0) || e.vah <= e.val) return false;
			m_vah = e.vah; m_height = e.vah - e.val;
			return true;
		}

		private void journal(string why) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"MT-02 week {m_week:yyyy-MM-dd} {why}"); }
	}
}
```

### 3.35 Example 28 (`_Ex28BreakAvwapPullback`) — AV-11 PS-01 break re-entry at the break-anchored AVWAP (long side shown; short mirrors) (hard)

**Features (direct).** F-DEV (§5.1), F-PROF (§5.2), F-COMP (§5.3), F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13), M-SWING (§5.16).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> AV-11 PS-01 break re-entry at the break-anchored AVWAP (long side shown; short mirrors).
> Context: the PS-01 filter (IsBalanced(RTH, 3, 0.6), V-score >= +1). Anchor: the FIRST 5m bar that closes > composite VAH + delta
> (09:45-14:30 ET). The AVWAP and its volume-weighted sigma are accumulated here from that bar (typical price), because the
> M-AVWAP Breakout anchor is a PA break-of-structure event, not a composite-edge break (see pitfall in section 5).
> Entry: the first pullback whose low trades into AVWAP +/- 0.25 sigma and that prints a PA HL; buy stop 1 tick above that bar.
> Stop: max(AVWAP - 1 sigma, 14-tick floor). Target: VAH + 1.0 W (PS-01 target). Invalidation: a 5m close < composite VAH - delta.
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: F-COMP, F-VP, M-PA, F-TM.

**Pitfalls.**

- Deviation: the catalogue's AVWAP is anchored at the break; `EAvwapAnchor.Breakout` anchors on a PRICEACTION break-of-structure instead, so the example computes its own break-anchored VWAP.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 28 - AV-11 PS-01 break re-entry at the break-anchored AVWAP (long side shown; short mirrors).
// Context: the PS-01 filter (IsBalanced(RTH, 3, 0.6), V-score >= +1). Anchor: the FIRST 5m bar that closes > composite VAH + delta
// (09:45-14:30 ET). The AVWAP and its volume-weighted sigma are accumulated here from that bar (typical price), because the
// M-AVWAP Breakout anchor is a PA break-of-structure event, not a composite-edge break (see pitfall in section 5).
// Entry: the first pullback whose low trades into AVWAP +/- 0.25 sigma and that prints a PA HL; buy stop 1 tick above that bar.
// Stop: max(AVWAP - 1 sigma, 14-tick floor). Target: VAH + 1.0 W (PS-01 target). Invalidation: a 5m close < composite VAH - delta.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: F-COMP, F-VP, M-PA, F-TM.
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex28BreakAvwapPullback : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Av11Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, n = 3, deltaTicks = 2;
			public double zoneSigma = 0.25, stopSigma = 1.0, targetW = 1.0, balanceOverlap = 0.6;
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), to = new TimeSpan(14, 30, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Av11Config Cfg { get; } = new Av11Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leStp, m_lxStp, m_lxLmt;
		private IOrderMarket m_lx;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private bool m_ok, m_done, m_anchored, m_touched;
		private double m_vah, m_w, m_spv, m_sp2v, m_sv, m_trigger = double.NaN, m_stop, m_target;

		public _Ex28BreakAvwapPullback(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			DevelopingSource = EDevelopingSource.Own; OwnProfileWindow = EOwnProfileWindow.Rth;
			PriceActionConfig.enabled = true; PriceActionConfig.dataStream = 1;
			FlatBy = Cfg.flat;
			m_leStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "AV11LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "AV11LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "AV11LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "AV11LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { base.StartCalc(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) newDay(day);
			if (rollOn(1)) m_done = true;
			double tick = tickSize(), c = Bars.Close[0], d = Cfg.deltaTicks * tick;
			if (StrategyInfo.MarketPosition > 0)
			{
				m_done = true;
				if (mustFlatten() || c < m_vah - d) { m_lx.Send(); return; }
				m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); return;
			}
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
			if (m_done || !m_ok || !IsRth()) return;
			if (!m_anchored)
			{
				if (tod < Cfg.from || tod > Cfg.to || !(c > m_vah + d)) return;
				m_anchored = true;
				if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"AV-11 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} anchor {Bars.Time[0]:HH:mm} close {c:F2} > VAH {m_vah:F2}");
			}
			double tp = (Bars.High[0] + Bars.Low[0] + c) / 3, v = Bars.Volume[0];
```
