```csharp
			m_spv += tp * v; m_sp2v += tp * tp * v; m_sv += v;
			if (!(m_sv > 0)) return;
			double av = m_spv / m_sv, sig = Math.Sqrt(Math.Max(0, m_sp2v / m_sv - av * av));
			if (c < m_vah - d) { m_done = true; return; }                       // break failed before the re-entry
			if (Bars.Low[0] <= av + Cfg.zoneSigma * sig && Bars.High[0] >= av - Cfg.zoneSigma * sig) m_touched = true;
			_PriceActionEngine pa = PriceActionEngine;
			if (m_touched && double.IsNaN(m_trigger) && tod <= Cfg.to && pa != null && pa.IsWarm && pa.NewPivotLow)
			{
				_Pivot p = pa.LastLow(0);
				if (p != null && p.label == EPivotLabel.HL)
				{
					double trig = Bars.High[0] + tick, stop = Math.Min(av - Cfg.stopSigma * sig, trig - Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick);
					if (Cfg.contracts * (trig - stop) * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction))
					{ if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"AV-11 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} skip: risk budget"); m_done = true; return; }
					m_trigger = trig; m_stop = stop; m_target = m_vah + Cfg.targetW * m_w;
					if (journalOn()) journalArm("AV11LE", m_trigger, m_stop, m_target, null);
				}
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(m_trigger)) m_leStp.Send(m_trigger, Cfg.contracts);
		}

		private void newDay(DateTime day)
		{
			m_day = day; m_done = m_anchored = m_touched = false; m_trigger = double.NaN; m_spv = m_sp2v = m_sv = 0;
			VpExtra comp = Composite(1, Cfg.n);
			m_ok = comp != null && IsBalanced(1, Cfg.n, Cfg.balanceOverlap) && (ValueMigrationScore(1, Cfg.n, 0.10, 20, false) ?? 0) >= 1;
			if (comp != null) { m_vah = comp.vah; m_w = comp.vah - comp.val; m_ok &= m_w > 0; }
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.36 Example 32 (`_Ex32FailedLevelFlip`) — DV-02 failed level -> reversal (flip) limit, with the CS-8 event log and control layer (hard)

**Features (direct).** F-DEV (§5.1), F-PROF (§5.2), F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13), F-EVT (§5.14), M-DAILY (§5.19).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> DV-02 failed level -> reversal (flip) limit, with the CS-8 event log and control layer.
> Level L: the prior completed RTH POC (a DV-01 level), passed through controlAdjustLevel() so a ShiftLevels control run moves it.
> Failure: after the first touch of L, a 5m close >= s x the bracket distance beyond L on the far side within M bars, with
> "no reaction" (max adverse excursion against the break after the touch < 0.25 x bracket). Entry: limit at L in the break
> direction, valid to the session end (X = 1 session), one flip per level. Bracket: +/- f x ATR20d (f = 0.10); the stop side is widened to
> the 14-tick floor if narrower (target unchanged), skipped only on a risk-budget breach, never resized. s = 1.0 and f = 0.10 are catalogue grid points; M = 12 bars is a PLACEHOLDER.
> Every armed flip is logged with logSignal(taken = true); skipped ones with taken = false (needs TraceLevel >= Journal).
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: F-EVT/CS-8 (logSignal, ControlMode, controlAdjustLevel,
> controlAdjustDelayBars), F-VP (PriorRthProfile), M-DAILY (Atr20d), F-TM.

**Pitfalls.**

- Contains PLACEHOLDER values (marked in the code); calibrate them before reading any result.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 32 - DV-02 failed level -> reversal (flip) limit, with the CS-8 event log and control layer.
// Level L: the prior completed RTH POC (a DV-01 level), passed through controlAdjustLevel() so a ShiftLevels control run moves it.
// Failure: after the first touch of L, a 5m close >= s x the bracket distance beyond L on the far side within M bars, with
// "no reaction" (max adverse excursion against the break after the touch < 0.25 x bracket). Entry: limit at L in the break
// direction, valid to the session end (X = 1 session), one flip per level. Bracket: +/- f x ATR20d (f = 0.10); the stop side is widened to
// the 14-tick floor if narrower (target unchanged), skipped only on a risk-budget breach, never resized. s = 1.0 and f = 0.10 are catalogue grid points; M = 12 bars is a PLACEHOLDER.
// Every armed flip is logged with logSignal(taken = true); skipped ones with taken = false (needs TraceLevel >= Journal).
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: F-EVT/CS-8 (logSignal, ControlMode, controlAdjustLevel,
// controlAdjustDelayBars), F-VP (PriorRthProfile), M-DAILY (Atr20d), F-TM.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex32FailedLevelFlip : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Dv02Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, breakWithinBars = 12;   // M: PLACEHOLDER
			public double s = 1.0, f = 0.10, noReaction = 0.25;
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
			public TimeSpan flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
		}
		protected Dv02Config Cfg { get; } = new Dv02Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leLmt, m_seLmt, m_lxStp, m_sxStp, m_lxLmt, m_sxLmt;
		private IOrderMarket m_lx, m_sx;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;
		private double m_level, m_br, m_sd, m_hiSince, m_loSince;
		private int m_touchBar, m_side, m_armBar;
		private bool m_done;

		public _Ex32FailedLevelFlip(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			DevelopingSource = EDevelopingSource.Own; OwnProfileWindow = EOwnProfileWindow.Rth;
			FlatBy = Cfg.flat;
			// Placebo run: ControlMode = EControlMode.ShiftLevels; ControlShiftTicks = 8;  (protected properties, never [Input])
			m_leLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "DV02LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_seLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "DV02SE", EOrderAction.SellShort));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV02LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_sx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV02SX", EOrderAction.BuyToCover));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV02LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV02SS", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV02LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_sxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV02ST", EOrderAction.BuyToCover, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { NeedsDaily = true; base.StartCalc(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) newDay(day);
			if (rollOn(1)) m_done = true;
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition, bar = Bars.FullSymbolData.Current;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				m_done = true;
				if (mustFlatten()) { if (pos > 0) m_lx.Send(); else m_sx.Send(); return; }
				double e = StrategyInfo.AvgEntryPrice;
				if (pos > 0) { m_lxStp.Send(e - m_sd); m_lxLmt.Send(e + m_br); } else { m_sxStp.Send(e + m_sd); m_sxLmt.Send(e - m_br); }
				return;
			}
			if (m_done || !(m_br > 0) || !IsRth()) return;
			double h = Bars.High[0], l = Bars.Low[0], c = Bars.Close[0];
			if (m_side != 0)                                           // armed: work the limit at L until the session ends
			{
				if (bar >= m_armBar + controlAdjustDelayBars())
				{ if (m_side > 0) m_leLmt.Send(m_level, Cfg.contracts); else m_seLmt.Send(m_level, Cfg.contracts); }
				return;
			}
			if (m_touchBar == 0)
			{
				if (l <= m_level && h >= m_level) { m_touchBar = bar; m_hiSince = h; m_loSince = l; }
				return;
			}
			m_hiSince = Math.Max(m_hiSince, h); m_loSince = Math.Min(m_loSince, l);
			if (bar - m_touchBar > Cfg.breakWithinBars) { m_done = true; return; }    // no failure within M bars: level held
			int side = c >= m_level + Cfg.s * m_br ? +1 : c <= m_level - Cfg.s * m_br ? -1 : 0;
			if (side == 0) return;
			double mae = side > 0 ? m_level - m_loSince : m_hiSince - m_level;
			bool reaction = mae >= Cfg.noReaction * m_br;
			bool budget = Cfg.contracts * m_sd * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction);
			bool taken = !reaction && !budget;
			logSignal("DV02", side, m_level, m_level - side * m_sd, new Dictionary<string, string> { { "mae", mae.ToString("F2") }, { "control", ControlMode.ToString() } }, taken);
			if (!taken) { m_done = true; return; }
			m_side = side; m_armBar = bar;
			if (journalOn()) journalArm(side > 0 ? "DV02LE" : "DV02SE", m_level, m_level - side * m_sd, m_level + side * m_br, null);
		}

		/// <summary>Hook for POC gate modules (Ex35: A2-25/A2-26). May widen the bracket; return false to skip the day.</summary>
		protected virtual bool pocGate(DateTime day, double poc, ref double bracket, out string why) { why = null; return true; }

		private void newDay(DateTime day)
		{
			m_day = day; m_done = false; m_touchBar = m_side = 0;
			VpExtra prior = PriorRthProfile();
			double atr = DailyContext != null ? DailyContext.Atr20d : double.NaN;
			m_br = atr > 0 ? Cfg.f * atr : double.NaN;
			if (prior == null || prior.crossesRoll || !(prior.poc > 0)) { m_done = true; return; }
			m_level = controlAdjustLevel(prior.poc);
			string gw;
			if (!pocGate(day, prior.poc, ref m_br, out gw)) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"DV-02 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} gate: {gw}"); m_done = true; return; }
			m_sd = Math.Max(m_br, Cfg.costFloorTicks * tickSize());   // stop = max(structural bracket, 14-tick floor); the target stays f x ATR20d
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.37 Example 33 (`_Ex33FvgHvnAlignment`) — DV-08 Fair Value Gap + heavy-volume alignment as a level layer (bullish side, as catalogued) (hard)

**Features (direct).** F-HVN (§5.5), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-JRNL (§5.13), M-DAILY (§5.19), VP helpers (§5.27).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> DV-08 Fair Value Gap + heavy-volume alignment as a level layer (bullish side, as catalogued).
> Level: a new bullish FVG on closed 30m bars (Fvgs, minAtrFrac = g x ATR30m; g = 0.25 is a PLACEHOLDER). The level is the FVG start
> edge (high of bar 1, fvgLevel(f, true)) and is kept only if it lies within 0.2 x ATR30m of an HVN edge of the range profile of the
> last 20 closed 30m bars (RangeProfileBars). Entry (DV-03/04 host style): buy limit at the level while the gap is live and untouched,
> valid until the gap fills or expires. Bracket: +/- f x ATR20d, f = 0.10 (catalogue grid point); the stop is widened to the 14-tick floor if narrower; skipped only on a risk-budget breach.
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 30m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: F-FVG (Fvgs, FvgDetector.NewThisBar/Live, fvgLevel -> LevelRef),
> F-RANGE (RangeProfiles.enabled, RangeProfileBars), M-DAILY (Atr20d), F-TM.

**Pitfalls.**

- Contains PLACEHOLDER values (marked in the code); calibrate them before reading any result.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 33 - DV-08 Fair Value Gap + heavy-volume alignment as a level layer (bullish side, as catalogued).
// Level: a new bullish FVG on closed 30m bars (Fvgs, minAtrFrac = g x ATR30m; g = 0.25 is a PLACEHOLDER). The level is the FVG start
// edge (high of bar 1, fvgLevel(f, true)) and is kept only if it lies within 0.2 x ATR30m of an HVN edge of the range profile of the
// last 20 closed 30m bars (RangeProfileBars). Entry (DV-03/04 host style): buy limit at the level while the gap is live and untouched,
// valid until the gap fills or expires. Bracket: +/- f x ATR20d, f = 0.10 (catalogue grid point); the stop is widened to the 14-tick floor if narrower; skipped only on a risk-budget breach.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 30m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: F-FVG (Fvgs, FvgDetector.NewThisBar/Live, fvgLevel -> LevelRef),
// F-RANGE (RangeProfiles.enabled, RangeProfileBars), M-DAILY (Atr20d), F-TM.
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex33FvgHvnAlignment : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Dv08Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, profileBars = 20;
			public double gapAtr = 0.25, alignAtr = 0.2, f = 0.10;   // gapAtr PLACEHOLDER; alignAtr and f from the catalogue
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Dv08Config Cfg { get; } = new Dv08Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leLmt, m_lxStp, m_lxLmt;
		private Fvg m_gap;
		private double m_level, m_br, m_sd;

		public _Ex33FvgHvnAlignment(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			Fvgs.enabled = true; Fvgs.dataStream = 1; Fvgs.minAtrFrac = Cfg.gapAtr; Fvgs.atrLen = 14; Fvgs.maxAgeSessions = 5;
			RangeProfiles.enabled = true; RangeProfiles.streams = new[] { 1 };
			m_leLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "DV08LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV08LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "DV08LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { NeedsDaily = true; base.StartCalc(); m_gap = null; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			if (rollOn(1)) m_gap = null;
			if (StrategyInfo.MarketPosition > 0)
			{
				m_gap = null;
				double e = StrategyInfo.AvgEntryPrice;
				m_lxStp.Send(e - m_sd); m_lxLmt.Send(e + m_br); return;
			}
			_FvgDetector det = FvgDetector;
			if (det == null) return;
			Fvg nf = det.NewThisBar;
			if (nf != null && nf.dir > 0) consider(nf, det.Atr);
			if (m_gap == null) return;
			if (!m_gap.IsLive || m_gap.touched) { m_gap = null; return; }       // first touch only
			m_leLmt.Send(m_level, Cfg.contracts);
		}

		private void consider(Fvg f, double atr30)
		{
			LevelRef lv = fvgLevel(f, true);
			VpExtra rp = RangeProfileBars(1, 0, Cfg.profileBars);
			double atrD = DailyContext != null ? DailyContext.Atr20d : double.NaN, tick = tickSize();
			if (lv == null || lv.crossesRoll || rp == null || !(atr30 > 0) || !(atrD > 0)) return;
			bool aligned = false;
			foreach (Node n in rp.hvns)
				if (Math.Abs(lv.price - n.high) <= Cfg.alignAtr * atr30 || Math.Abs(lv.price - n.low) <= Cfg.alignAtr * atr30) { aligned = true; break; }
			double br = Cfg.f * atrD, sd = Math.Max(br, Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick);   // stop = max(structural, 14-tick floor)
			string why = !aligned ? "no HVN edge within 0.2 ATR30m"
				: Cfg.contracts * sd * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction) ? "risk budget" : null;
			if (why != null) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"DV-08 {lv.tag} skip: {why}"); return; }
			m_gap = f; m_level = lv.price; m_br = br; m_sd = sd;
			if (journalOn()) journalArm("DV08LE", m_level, m_level - sd, m_level + br, null);
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.38 Example 34 (`_Ex34TrendClusterPullback`) — PS-12 trend-cluster pullback, Dale limit variant (long side, as catalogued) (hard)

**Features (direct).** F-DEV (§5.1), F-COMP (§5.3), F-HVN (§5.5), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-JRNL (§5.13), M-SWING (§5.16), VP helpers (§5.27).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> PS-12 trend-cluster pullback, Dale limit variant (long side, as catalogued).
> Filter: 60m PA structure Up (HH/HL; ExtraPriceAction engine on Data2). Cluster: a closed 30m bar whose largest single-price row
> (BarLadders, bid/ask tiers only) >= 2 x the bar's median row volume, inside a pause (bar range <= 0.7 x ATR30m).
> Validity: 2 later 30m closes above cluster + delta. Entry: buy limit at the cluster price (first touch). Stop: the next composite
> LVN low below the cluster, floored at 14 ticks. Target: the last 30m pivot high (skip if not above the entry). One cluster at a time.
> Chart: Data1 = @ES 30m, Data2 = @ES 60m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED; needs tick/bid-ask history for the flow tiers.
> Features: F-LADDER (BarLadders.enabled -> OrderFlowEnabled, Ladder(ago), LadderBar rows/total/HasBidAsk), M-PA (ExtraPriceAction),
> F-COMP (lvns), F-TM.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 34 - PS-12 trend-cluster pullback, Dale limit variant (long side, as catalogued).
// Filter: 60m PA structure Up (HH/HL; ExtraPriceAction engine on Data2). Cluster: a closed 30m bar whose largest single-price row
// (BarLadders, bid/ask tiers only) >= 2 x the bar's median row volume, inside a pause (bar range <= 0.7 x ATR30m).
// Validity: 2 later 30m closes above cluster + delta. Entry: buy limit at the cluster price (first touch). Stop: the next composite
// LVN low below the cluster, floored at 14 ticks. Target: the last 30m pivot high (skip if not above the entry). One cluster at a time.
// Chart: Data1 = @ES 30m, Data2 = @ES 60m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED; needs tick/bid-ask history for the flow tiers.
// Features: F-LADDER (BarLadders.enabled -> OrderFlowEnabled, Ladder(ago), LadderBar rows/total/HasBidAsk), M-PA (ExtraPriceAction),
// F-COMP (lvns), F-TM.
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex34TrendClusterPullback : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class Ps12Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, atrLength = 14, validCloses = 2, deltaTicks = 2, n = 20, ds60 = 2;
			public double clusterMult = 2.0, pauseAtr = 0.7;
			public int costFloorTicks = 14;
			public double riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
		}
		protected Ps12Config Cfg { get; } = new Ps12Config();

		private IOrderPriced m_leLmt, m_lxStp, m_lxLmt;
		private double m_cluster = double.NaN, m_stop, m_target;
		private int m_above;
		private bool m_valid;

		public _Ex34TrendClusterPullback(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			DevelopingSource = EDevelopingSource.Own; OwnProfileWindow = EOwnProfileWindow.Rth;
			BarLadders.enabled = true; BarLadders.keepBars = 500;                       // also switches OrderFlowEnabled on (VP-3)
			PriceActionConfig.enabled = true; PriceActionConfig.dataStream = 1;
			ExtraPriceAction.Add(new PriceActionConfig { enabled = true, dataStream = Cfg.ds60, role = "trend60" });
			m_leLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "PS12LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS12LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "PS12LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { base.StartCalc(); m_cluster = double.NaN; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			if (rollOn(1)) m_cluster = double.NaN;
			if (StrategyInfo.MarketPosition > 0) { m_cluster = double.NaN; m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target); return; }
			_PriceActionEngine pa60 = PriceActionFor(Cfg.ds60, "trend60");
			bool up = pa60 != null && pa60.IsWarm && pa60.Structure(0) == EStructure.Up;
			if (!up) { m_cluster = double.NaN; return; }
			double tick = tickSize();
			if (double.IsNaN(m_cluster)) { findCluster(); return; }
			if (!m_valid)
			{
				m_above = Bars.Close[0] > m_cluster + Cfg.deltaTicks * tick ? m_above + 1 : 0;
				if (Bars.Low[0] <= m_cluster) { m_cluster = double.NaN; return; }       // touched before it was validated
				if (m_above >= Cfg.validCloses) m_valid = arm();
				return;
			}
			m_leLmt.Send(m_cluster, Cfg.contracts);
			if (Bars.Low[0] <= m_cluster) m_cluster = double.NaN;                      // first touch consumed (filled or not)
		}

		private void findCluster()
		{
			LadderBar lb = Ladder(0);
			double atr = averageTrueRange(Bars, Cfg.atrLength);
			if (lb == null || !lb.HasBidAsk || !lb.HasRows || lb.crossesRoll || !(atr > 0) || Bars.High[0] - Bars.Low[0] > Cfg.pauseAtr * atr) return;
			double[] t = (double[])lb.total.Clone();
			Array.Sort(t);
			double med = t[t.Length / 2];
			int best = 0;
			for (int i = 1; i < lb.total.Length; i++) if (lb.total[i] > lb.total[best]) best = i;
			if (!(med > 0) || lb.total[best] < Cfg.clusterMult * med) return;
			m_cluster = lb.rows[best] * lb.tick; m_above = 0; m_valid = false;
		}

		private bool arm()
		{
			double tick = tickSize(), lvn = double.NaN;
			VpExtra comp = Composite(1, Cfg.n);
			if (comp != null) foreach (Node z in comp.lvns) if (z.high < m_cluster && (double.IsNaN(lvn) || z.low > lvn)) lvn = z.low;
			double stop = double.IsNaN(lvn) ? m_cluster - Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick : Math.Min(lvn - tick, m_cluster - Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick);
			_PriceActionEngine pa = PriceActionEngine;
			_Pivot sh = pa != null ? pa.LastHigh(0) : null;
			string why = sh == null || sh.price <= m_cluster ? "no swing high above" : Cfg.contracts * (m_cluster - stop) * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction) ? "risk budget" : null;
			if (why != null) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PS-12 cluster {m_cluster:F2} skip: {why}"); m_cluster = double.NaN; return false; }
			m_stop = stop; m_target = sh.price;
			if (journalOn()) journalArm("PS12LE", m_cluster, m_stop, m_target, null);
			return true;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.39 Example 36 (`_Ex36BimodalVoidTraverse`) — A2-27 bimodal-profile void traverse (long side shown; the short mirror uses E2 / mu1) (hard)

**Features (direct).** F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), F-TM (§5.12), F-JRNL (§5.13), M-MTF (§5.17), M-GMM (§5.25), VP helpers (§5.27).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> A2-27 bimodal-profile void traverse (long side shown; the short mirror uses E2 / mu1).
> Setup (at the first bar of the new Globex day, from the PRIOR RTH session): Data1 ring bars -> _ProfileStats.Histogram ->
> _ProfileStats.ProfileMixture (kMax 3, BIC, seed MixtureSeed(day), N_eff = bars). Require K == 2, both pi >= 0.30, void depth >= 0.5,
> mu2 - mu1 >= 1.0 x ATR(14) of Data2 (30m). E1 = mu1 + sd1, E2 = mu2 - sd2.
> Deviation: the catalogue fits the prior session's profile; here the histogram is built from the 5m Data1 ring (not 1m rows).
> Entry: price first closes at/below E1, then 2 consecutive closed 5m bars above E1 inside 09:45-14:30 ET -> buy at the next 5m open.
> Stop: mu1 - 1 tick, capped at 1.0 x ATR(14, 5m) from the signal close, then floored at 14 ticks (user rule). Skip if mu2 - close < 1.2R
> or if the risk budget (0.01 x equity) is breached. Target: mu2. Time stop: 90 minutes, flat by 15:55.
> Not coded: T1 50% at E2 (single-contract example). Thresholds are catalogue values, not results.
> Chart: Data1 @ES 5m, Data2 @ES 30m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: F-PSTAT (ProfileStats.enabled ring, SessionBars,
> Histogram, ProfileMixture, MixtureSeed), F-TM (FlatBy).

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 36 - A2-27 bimodal-profile void traverse (long side shown; the short mirror uses E2 / mu1).
// Setup (at the first bar of the new Globex day, from the PRIOR RTH session): Data1 ring bars -> _ProfileStats.Histogram ->
// _ProfileStats.ProfileMixture (kMax 3, BIC, seed MixtureSeed(day), N_eff = bars). Require K == 2, both pi >= 0.30, void depth >= 0.5,
// mu2 - mu1 >= 1.0 x ATR(14) of Data2 (30m). E1 = mu1 + sd1, E2 = mu2 - sd2.
// Deviation: the catalogue fits the prior session's profile; here the histogram is built from the 5m Data1 ring (not 1m rows).
// Entry: price first closes at/below E1, then 2 consecutive closed 5m bars above E1 inside 09:45-14:30 ET -> buy at the next 5m open.
// Stop: mu1 - 1 tick, capped at 1.0 x ATR(14, 5m) from the signal close, then floored at 14 ticks (user rule). Skip if mu2 - close < 1.2R
// or if the risk budget (0.01 x equity) is breached. Target: mu2. Time stop: 90 minutes, flat by 15:55.
// Not coded: T1 50% at E2 (single-contract example). Thresholds are catalogue values, not results.
// Chart: Data1 @ES 5m, Data2 @ES 30m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: F-PSTAT (ProfileStats.enabled ring, SessionBars,
// Histogram, ProfileMixture, MixtureSeed), F-TM (FlatBy).
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex36BimodalVoidTraverse : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected sealed class A227Config
		{
			public int contracts = 1, kMax = 3, acceptCloses = 2, atrLength = 14, costFloorTicks = 14, timeStopMin = 90;
			public double minPi = 0.30, minVoidDepth = 0.5, minSepAtr30 = 1.0, stopCapAtr5 = 1.0, minRR = 1.2, riskBudgetFraction = 0.01;
			public TimeSpan from = new TimeSpan(9, 45, 0), to = new TimeSpan(14, 30, 0), flat = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);
		}
		protected A227Config Cfg { get; } = new A227Config();

		private IOrderMarket m_le, m_lx;
		private IOrderPriced m_lxStp, m_lxLmt;
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue, m_entryTime;
		private bool m_ok, m_done, m_armed;
		private int m_up, m_lastPos;
		private double m_mu1, m_mu2, m_e1, m_stop, m_target;

		public _Ex36BimodalVoidTraverse(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			ProfileStats.enabled = true;                      // lazy: Data1 ring for SessionBars
			FlatBy = Cfg.flat;
			m_le = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "A227LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
			m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A227LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
			m_lxStp = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A227LS", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
			m_lxLmt = OrderCreator.Limit(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "A227LT", EOrderAction.Sell, OrderExit.FromAll));
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { base.StartCalc(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; m_lastPos = 0; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day != m_day) newDay(day);
			if (rollOn(1)) m_done = true;
			int pos = StrategyInfo.MarketPosition;
			if (pos != 0 && m_lastPos == 0) m_entryTime = Bars.Time[0];
			m_lastPos = pos;
			if (pos != 0)
			{
				m_done = true;
				if (mustFlatten() || (Bars.Time[0] - m_entryTime).TotalMinutes >= Cfg.timeStopMin) { m_lx.Send(); return; }
				m_lxStp.Send(m_stop); m_lxLmt.Send(m_target);
				return;
			}
			if (m_done || !m_ok || !IsRth()) return;
			double c = Bars.Close[0], tick = tickSize();
			if (c <= m_e1) { m_armed = true; m_up = 0; return; }   // in or below the lower component
			if (!m_armed) return;
			m_up++;
			TimeSpan tod = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
			if (m_up < Cfg.acceptCloses || tod < Cfg.from || tod > Cfg.to) return;
			m_done = true;                                          // one attempt per day
			double atr5 = averageTrueRange(Bars, Cfg.atrLength);
			double stop = Math.Max(m_mu1 - tick, c - Cfg.stopCapAtr5 * atr5);
			if (c - stop < Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick) stop = c - Cfg.costFloorTicks * tick;
			double r = c - stop;
			string why = m_mu2 - c < Cfg.minRR * r ? "target < 1.2R"
				: Cfg.contracts * r * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(Cfg.riskBudgetFraction) ? "risk budget" : null;
			if (why != null) { if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"A2-27 {day:yyyy-MM-dd} skip: {why}"); return; }
			m_stop = stop; m_target = m_mu2;
			if (journalOn()) journalArm("A227LE", c, m_stop, m_target, null);
			m_le.Send(Cfg.contracts);
		}

		private void newDay(DateTime day)
		{
			m_day = day; m_done = false; m_armed = false; m_up = 0; m_ok = false;
			DateTime prev = priorTradingDay(day);
			if (prev == DateTime.MinValue) return;
			List<RingBar> bars = SessionBars(prev, true);
			double tick = tickSize(), atr30 = averageTrueRange(BarsOfData(2), Cfg.atrLength);
			double[] px, vol;
			if (bars.Count < 20 || !_ProfileStats.Histogram(bars, null, tick, 2, out px, out vol)) return;
			ProfileMixtureResult m = _ProfileStats.ProfileMixture(px, vol, Cfg.kMax, _ProfileStats.MixtureSeed(prev), EGmmNeff.Bars, bars.Count, tick);
			if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, m.Journal(prev));
			if (m.K != 2 || m.pi[0] < Cfg.minPi || m.pi[1] < Cfg.minPi || !(m.voidDepth.Length > 0 && m.voidDepth[0] >= Cfg.minVoidDepth)) return;
			if (!(m.mu[1] - m.mu[0] >= Cfg.minSepAtr30 * atr30)) return;
			m_mu1 = m.mu[0]; m_mu2 = m.mu[1]; m_e1 = m.mu[0] + m.sd[0];
			m_ok = m_e1 < m.mu[1] - m.sd[1];                       // E1 below E2: a real void between the edges
		}

		private DateTime priorTradingDay(DateTime day)
		{
			int maxAgo = Math.Min(ProfileStats.ringBars, Bars.CurrentBar - 1);
			for (int ago = 0; ago < maxAgo; ago++) { RingBar rb = ringBarOf(1, ago); if (rb.tradingDay < day) return rb.tradingDay; }
			return DateTime.MinValue;
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.40 Example 39 (`_Ex39XmktBetaMonitor`) — PF-03 cross-market hidden-beta monitor, RESEARCH PROXY (no orders) (research)

**Features (direct).** F-SESS (§5.6), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), M-XMKT (§5.22).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> PF-03 cross-market hidden-beta monitor, RESEARCH PROXY (no orders).
> The catalogue rule correlates each sleeve's rolling 60-day DAILY P&L with SPX daily returns and flags rho > 0.5. Sleeve P&L
> series do not exist inside one MultiCharts strategy, so this example shows the M-XMKT building block instead: Data2 is a second
> market (here the leg role "SPX", e.g. an S&P index or another future), XmktLeg aligns it to Data1 on closed bars and keeps a rolling
> return Correlation / Beta / RsZ over corrLength bars. At the last RTH bar it journals the values and flags |rho| > 0.5.
> This is NOT the PF-03 statistic; the sleeve-P&L version must be computed offline from the trade journals.
> Chart: Data1 @ES 30m, Data2 = the reference market at 30m. Features: M-XMKT (XmktLegs, XmktLeg, Correlation, Beta, RsZ, Available).

**Pitfalls.**

- Research tool: it sends no orders and only journals.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 39 - PF-03 cross-market hidden-beta monitor, RESEARCH PROXY (no orders).
// The catalogue rule correlates each sleeve's rolling 60-day DAILY P&L with SPX daily returns and flags rho > 0.5. Sleeve P&L
// series do not exist inside one MultiCharts strategy, so this example shows the M-XMKT building block instead: Data2 is a second
// market (here the leg role "SPX", e.g. an S&P index or another future), XmktLeg aligns it to Data1 on closed bars and keeps a rolling
// return Correlation / Beta / RsZ over corrLength bars. At the last RTH bar it journals the values and flags |rho| > 0.5.
// This is NOT the PF-03 statistic; the sleeve-P&L version must be computed offline from the trade journals.
// Chart: Data1 @ES 30m, Data2 = the reference market at 30m. Features: M-XMKT (XmktLegs, XmktLeg, Correlation, Beta, RsZ, Available).
using System;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex39XmktBetaMonitor : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected double FlagRho { get; set; } = 0.5;
		private DateTime m_logged = DateTime.MinValue;

		public _Ex39XmktBetaMonitor(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			XmktLegs.Add(new XmktLegConfig { role = "SPX", dataStream = 2, returnBars = 1, corrLength = 60, rsLength = 60 });
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close || !IsRth()) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day == m_logged || ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay < new TimeSpan(15, 59, 0)) return;
			m_logged = day;
			_XmktLeg leg = XmktLeg("SPX");
			if (leg == null || !leg.Available || double.IsNaN(leg.Correlation)) return;
			bool flag = Math.Abs(leg.Correlation) > FlagRho;
			if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"PF-03proxy {day:yyyy-MM-dd} rho {leg.Correlation:F2} beta {leg.Beta:F2} rsZ {leg.RsZ:F2}{(flag ? " FLAG" : "")}");
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.41 Example 41 (`_Ex41LevelPersistenceStudy`) — A2-33 level-persistence vs matched-control study (RESEARCH, no orders) (research)

**Features (direct).** F-PROF (§5.2), F-NPOC (§5.4), F-ROLL (§5.7), F-CAL (§5.8), A2-33 (§5.28).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> A2-33 level-persistence vs matched-control study (RESEARCH, no orders).
> On the first closed Data1 bar of each trading day, registers the prior session's POC / VAH / VAL (age 1) and every still-naked POC
> up to LevelStudyConfig.maxAge sessions old with levelStudyAdd. The _LevelStudy harness (A2-33) then tracks touches, reactions vs
> matched random controls (distLo..distHi), and Kaplan-Meier level death, writing its results to the journal / csvPath at the end.
> Status on main: matched controls and Kaplan-Meier exist; the Cox model in the catalogue spec is NOT implemented (A2-33 partial).
> Ages assume RecentCompletedProfiles returns newest first (age = index + 1). Chart: Data1 @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
> Features: A2-33 (LevelStudyConfig, levelStudyAdd), F-PROF (RecentCompletedProfiles), F-NPOC (TrackNakedPoc, NakedPocs).

**Pitfalls.**

- Research tool: it sends no orders and only journals.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 41 - A2-33 level-persistence vs matched-control study (RESEARCH, no orders).
// On the first closed Data1 bar of each trading day, registers the prior session's POC / VAH / VAL (age 1) and every still-naked POC
// up to LevelStudyConfig.maxAge sessions old with levelStudyAdd. The _LevelStudy harness (A2-33) then tracks touches, reactions vs
// matched random controls (distLo..distHi), and Kaplan-Meier level death, writing its results to the journal / csvPath at the end.
// Status on main: matched controls and Kaplan-Meier exist; the Cox model in the catalogue spec is NOT implemented (A2-33 partial).
// Ages assume RecentCompletedProfiles returns newest first (age = index + 1). Chart: Data1 @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED.
// Features: A2-33 (LevelStudyConfig, levelStudyAdd), F-PROF (RecentCompletedProfiles), F-NPOC (TrackNakedPoc, NakedPocs).
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex41LevelPersistenceStudy : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		private DateTime m_day = DateTime.MinValue;

		public _Ex41LevelPersistenceStudy(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			RollDetection = ERollDetection.Series;
			TrackNakedPoc = true;                             // VP-base [Input]: set in code, never exposed
			LevelStudyConfig.enabled = true;                  // lazy: harness not created otherwise
			LevelStudyConfig.controls = 20; LevelStudyConfig.reactionBars = 60; LevelStudyConfig.maxAge = 15;
			LevelStudyConfig.csvPath = "";                    // empty = journal only
		}

		protected override void StartCalc() { base.StartCalc(); m_day = DateTime.MinValue; }

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();                                   // the harness steps inside base.CalcBar(); add levels after it
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
			DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);
			if (day == m_day) return;
			m_day = day;
			int maxAge = LevelStudyConfig.maxAge;
			List<VpExtra> recent = RecentCompletedProfiles(1, maxAge, Bars.CurrentBar);
			if (recent == null || recent.Count == 0) return;
			VpExtra p = recent[0];
			levelStudyAdd("POC", p.poc, 1); levelStudyAdd("VAH", p.vah, 1); levelStudyAdd("VAL", p.val, 1);
			foreach (VpExtra n in NakedPocs(1))
			{
				int age = recent.IndexOf(n) + 1;
				if (age >= 1 && age <= maxAge) levelStudyAdd("nPOC", n.poc, age, double.NaN, "nPOC@" + n.startBar);
			}
		}
	}
}
```

### 3.42 Example 42 (`_Ex42ForwardConeDiagnostic`) — KD-08 forward-cone diagnostic, RESEARCH SNIPPET (no orders; KD-08 itself is not buildable on main) (research)

**Features (direct).** F-ROLL (§5.7), M-DVP (§5.31).

**What it does** (from the file header):

> KD-08 forward-cone diagnostic, RESEARCH SNIPPET (no orders; KD-08 itself is not buildable on main).
> Status (origin/main 628542f): M-DVP is hosted on Data1 (PR-D3, #36) with the Fib lattice (PR-D4, #37). This file uses the host API
> (DvpConfig, dvpSnapshot, dvpZ, dvpRailPrice, dvpRails). The engine itself measures forward coverage: with journalFits = true it
> writes one DVP FIT line per fit and, on the last historical bar, a DVP RUN line with meanCovIn / meanCovFwd / meanCovFwdFlat
> (cov_fwd over projBars, roll-crossing horizons dropped and counted in fwdDropped). KD-08 still needs PR-D8 (Calibrate study) and
> PR-D9 (frozen d*): until then DevCoverageFrozen falls back to Calibrate with reported degree 1 (one "DVP DEGREE not frozen" warning).
> Every Step closed bars it journals the as-of t-1 snapshot, z of the close and the +-2 sigma rail prices. winLen 240 / projBars 60 /
> degMax 3 are DvpConfig defaults, not tuned values; Step 12 is a journal-thinning choice, not a parameter of the measurement.
> Chart: Data1 @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: M-DVP host API (+ F-ROLL via the snapshot's roll skip).

*Note (800bce7): the header above predates D5–D7; the confluence, acceptance, HTF and A2-33 hook APIs are now on main (§5.31). The example itself is unchanged and uses only the D3/D4 host API.*

**Pitfalls.**

- Research tool: it sends no orders and only journals.

**Code.**

```csharp
// Manual v4 example 42 - KD-08 forward-cone diagnostic, RESEARCH SNIPPET (no orders; KD-08 itself is not buildable on main).
// Status (origin/main 628542f): M-DVP is hosted on Data1 (PR-D3, #36) with the Fib lattice (PR-D4, #37). This file uses the host API
// (DvpConfig, dvpSnapshot, dvpZ, dvpRailPrice, dvpRails). The engine itself measures forward coverage: with journalFits = true it
// writes one DVP FIT line per fit and, on the last historical bar, a DVP RUN line with meanCovIn / meanCovFwd / meanCovFwdFlat
// (cov_fwd over projBars, roll-crossing horizons dropped and counted in fwdDropped). KD-08 still needs PR-D8 (Calibrate study) and
// PR-D9 (frozen d*): until then DevCoverageFrozen falls back to Calibrate with reported degree 1 (one "DVP DEGREE not frozen" warning).
// Every Step closed bars it journals the as-of t-1 snapshot, z of the close and the +-2 sigma rail prices. winLen 240 / projBars 60 /
// degMax 3 are DvpConfig defaults, not tuned values; Step 12 is a journal-thinning choice, not a parameter of the measurement.
// Chart: Data1 @ES 5m, dxFeed continuous UNADJUSTED. Features: M-DVP host API (+ F-ROLL via the snapshot's roll skip).
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using PowerLanguage.Function;

namespace PowerLanguage.Strategy
{
	public class _Ex42ForwardConeDiagnostic : _VolumeProfileFuturesBase
	{
		protected int Step { get; set; } = 12;

		public _Ex42ForwardConeDiagnostic(object _ctx) : base(_ctx) { }

		protected override void Create()
		{
			base.Create();
			DvpConfig.enabled = true;        // lazy engine, Data1 only (DvpConfig.streams default { 1 })
			DvpConfig.journalFits = true;    // DVP FIT per fit + DVP RUN (cov_fwd) at run end - the KD-08 measurement
			DvpConfig.coneMode = EDvpConeMode.Prediction;
		}

		protected override void CalcBar()
		{
			base.CalcBar();                  // stepDvp runs inside; the snapshot below is as of t-1
			if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close || Bars.CurrentBar % Step != 0 || rollOn(1)) return;
			DvpSnapshot s = dvpSnapshot(1);
			if (s == null || !s.valid) return;   // warm-up, or the window crosses a roll
			double z = dvpZ(1, Bars.Close[0]);
			double up2 = dvpRailPrice(1, ELevelKind.DvpSigma, 2), dn2 = dvpRailPrice(1, ELevelKind.DvpSigma, -2);
			IReadOnlyList<LevelRef> rails = dvpRails(1);   // reused by the next call; only counted here
			if (journalOn()) trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"KD-08sketch {Bars.Time[0]:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} deg {s.deg} calibrating {s.calibrating} R2 {s.r2:F2} sigmaAtr {s.sigmaAtr:F2} drift {s.endpointDrift:F2} cov2s(in) {s.inSampleCov2:F2} z {z:F2} band {dn2:F2}..{up2:F2} rails {rails.Count}");
		}
	}
}
```


---

## 4 — Engines and helpers

> Each chapter follows the same order: **why it exists · when to use it · enable · settings · API (with plain meaning) · timing · journal · tests · snippet**.
>
> **Snippets** in this part are the bodies of small compiled classes (`_S4_01` … `_S4_14`, each deriving from `_CustomSignal`; type-checked against `origin/main`). Every block starts with a comment that names where the code lives: `// class field(s)`, `// Create`, `// StartCalc`, `// CalcBar`. Two rules from §1 apply everywhere:
> - **Configure in `Create()`, or in `StartCalc()` before `base.StartCalc()`.** `base.StartCalc()` builds the engines from the settings it finds. Changing config after that is **unsupported**: engines hold the config objects by reference, so a mid-run change can take partial effect. **Exception:** call `requireRing(...)` *after* `base.StartCalc()`, because `startVpHelpers` (`_CustomSignal` L1440) clears earlier ring requests (§4.14).
> - **Read in `CalcBar()` after `base.CalcBar()`.** `base.CalcBar()` steps every engine on the closed bar; before that call you read the previous bar's values.
>
> Full public signatures, with file and class, are in **Appendix E**; complete strategies are in §3.

### 4.1 `_CustomSignal` core services

**Why it exists.** Every strategy needs the same plumbing: a log you can audit, a trade journal, position sizing from a stop, daily trading limits ("no entries before 09:35, flat by 15:55, at most two trades, stop after two losers"), time-zone conversion and a trading-day key. `_CustomSignal` provides these once, so a strategy only contains its own entry and exit logic.

**When to use it.** Always: every strategy in §3 derives from it (directly, or through `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`, §4.15). Trade-management rules (`entryAllowed`, `mustFlatten`) appear in examples 12, 13, 17, 18, 20, 22, 23, 25–32, 36, 37, 38 and 40; `logSignal` in examples 1–3, 5–7, 10–16, 20, 24, 29 and 32.

**Enable.** Always on. The only `[Input]` is `TraceLevel` (default `None`, set in the constructor since PR #49; `base.Create()` no longer resets it). The settings below are properties, set in `Create()`.

**API.**

| Member | Plain meaning |
|---|---|
| `traceOn(ETraceLevel)`, `trace(ETraceLevel, string)` | Is this log level on? Write one line to the trace file at that level. |
| `journalOn()` | True when the journal level is on; guard expensive journal text with it. |
| `engineSink(ETraceLevel)` | The callback the engines use to write into your trace; pass it to engines you build yourself. |
| `journalArm(string entryOrderName, double plannedEntry, double initialStop, double target, IDictionary<string,string> context = null)` | Record what you knew when you sent the entry (planned price, stop, target, context). The CS-1 trade row is written when the trade with that exact order name completes. Call it next to `Send()`. |
| `logSignal(string tag, int direction, double price, double stop, IDictionary<string,string> context, bool taken)` | Log a setup whether or not you traded it, so taken and skipped signals can be compared later. Forward returns are measured at `SignalHorizons` (default `{5,15,30,60}` bars). |
| `NoEntryBefore`, `NoEntryAfter`, `FlatBy` (`TimeSpan?`) | Daily entry window and the forced-flat time, compared with `sessionClock()`. |
| `MaxTradesPerDay`, `DailyLossStopCount`, `DailyLossStopR`, `RiskUnitDollars` | Daily limits: max entries, max losing trades, max loss in R (R = `RiskUnitDollars`). 0 = off. |
| `TimeStopBars` | Exit a position after this many bars. 0 = off. |
| `entryAllowed(out string reason)` | May I enter now? False with the rule that blocked (traced as `entry blocked: …` at Info). |
| `mustFlatten()` | Should the open position be closed now (FlatBy reached or TimeStopBars elapsed)? Always false when flat. |
| `TradesToday`, `LosersToday`, `NetProfitToday` | Today's counters, per `tradingDayKey`. |
| `sizeForRisk(IInstrument bars, double entry, double stop, double riskFraction, SizingLimits limits)` | Contracts so that the entry-to-stop loss ≈ `riskFraction` of equity, then capped by `SizingLimits` (`maxQty`, `maxNotionalFraction`, `unitNotional`, `minStopTicks`, `roundLots`). 0 = do not trade (reason traced). Also publishes the arm to M-PORT for Data1. |
| `equityAvailable(double)` | Equity available for one position: the whole chart equity, or the portfolio per-position share in Portfolio Trader, unless a custom percentage is given. |
| `costFilterPasses(IInstrument, entry, stop, out reason)` | Is the round-trip cost small enough relative to R? Cost = 2 × (Commission + Slippage) from Strategy Properties; fails when cost / R > `CostFilterMaxFractionOfR` (default 0 = filter off, always passes). |
| `ToZone(DateTime barTime, string tzId)`, `BarTimeZone`, `resolveBarTimeZone(IInstrumentSettings)` | Convert a bar time to another zone; the chart's zone. |
| `inTimeWindow(TimeSpan start, TimeSpan end, string tzId)` | Is the current bar time in `[start, end)` in that zone? Wraps past midnight. |
| `TradingDayRoll` (`Calendar` default, or `Auto`), `tradingDayKey(DateTime)` (virtual) | Which trading day a bar belongs to; all daily counters and engines use it. |
| `sessionClock(DateTime)` (virtual) | The time of day the CS-7 rules compare against: bar time on `_CustomSignal`, US Eastern on the futures base. |
| `Holidays` (`_NyseHolidays`) | NYSE holiday calendar. |
| `tickSize()` | Minimum price move of Data1 (resolved once per run). |

**Cost example.** With the manual's cost assumptions ($2.25 commission + 0.5 tick = $6.25 slippage per side, $17.00 round trip on ES), a 14-tick stop is R = $175 and the cost is 0.097 R, so `CostFilterMaxFractionOfR = 0.10` lets it through; a 13-tick stop ($162.50, 0.105 R) is rejected.

**Timing.** The CS-7 counters reset on a new `tradingDayKey`. `mustFlatten` is true at or after `FlatBy`, or once `TimeStopBars` have elapsed.

**Journal.**
- `RUN id(..) build(..) strategy(..) symbol(..) category(..) resolution(..) timeZone(..) firstBar(..) barNumber(..) app(..) inputs[..]`, plus ` event[canon] journalSchema(CS-1+evtDayTier,evtMinSince)` when Event is on.
- `RUN-END id(..) lastBar(..) barNumber(..) realTime(..)`.
- `entry blocked: <reason>` at Info.

**Snippet.** A long breakout entry that uses every core service: daily rules in `Create`, then on each closed bar: flatten if the rules say so, ask whether an entry is allowed, size from the stop, check costs, journal and send. Sizing follows the house rule (cost floor, §5.13): risk 1% of equity on the structural stop, widen the stop to at least 14 ticks, skip the setup if the widened risk exceeds 1%, and never shrink the size. The floor is strategy code; `sizeForRisk` has no floor of its own. `sizeForRisk` also arms M-PORT (§4.16) with the structural stop, so after widening the snippet calls `portfolioStop(stop)` (`protected void portfolioStop(double stop)`, `_CustomSignal` L1594; a no-op outside Portfolio Trader) so `pm.riskOpen` uses the widened stop once the order fills. `pm.R$` and `pm.riskNew` stay on the structural stop. The widened-risk key `pm.R$w` exists only in the draft M-PORT risk-profiles spec and is not on main, so for now the pending-entry risk the money manager sees is understated. Do not call `portfolioArm` with the widened stop: it would overwrite `pm.R$` and the money manager would cut the size. A result of `qty = 0`, a budget breach or a failed cost filter means "skip this setup"; the reason is in the trace.

```csharp
// class fields
private IOrderPriced m_le;                       // buy-stop entry
private IOrderMarket m_lx;                       // market exit

// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	m_le = OrderCreator.Stop(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.UserSpecified, "LE", EOrderAction.Buy));
	m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
	NoEntryBefore = new TimeSpan(9, 35, 0);      // compared with sessionClock(): bar time on _CustomSignal, ET on the futures base
	NoEntryAfter = new TimeSpan(15, 30, 0);
	FlatBy = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);            // mustFlatten() turns true at or after this time
	MaxTradesPerDay = 2;                         // counted per tradingDayKey()
	DailyLossStopCount = 2;                      // no new entries after 2 losing trades today
	CostFilterMaxFractionOfR = 0.10;             // skip trades whose round-trip cost exceeds 0.10 R
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();                              // engines and CS-7 counters update here
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	if (mustFlatten()) { m_lx.Send(); return; }  // FlatBy reached or TimeStopBars elapsed
	string why;
	if (StrategyInfo.MarketPosition != 0 || !entryAllowed(out why)) return;   // why names the blocking rule (traced at Info)
	double entry = Bars.High[0] + tickSize();
	double stop = Bars.Low[0] - tickSize();                     // structural stop
	int qty = sizeForRisk(Bars, entry, stop, 0.01,                    // house rule: 1% of equity at risk
		new SizingLimits { maxQty = 5 });                             // 0 = do not trade (reason traced)
	if (qty <= 0) return;
	stop = Math.Min(stop, entry - 14 * tickSize());             // house floor: stop distance = max(structural, 14 ticks)
	if (qty * (entry - stop) * Bars.Info.BigPointValue > equityAvailable(0.01)) return;   // widened risk > 1%: skip, never shrink qty
	portfolioStop(stop);                        // M-PORT: the stop the order carries (pm.riskOpen once filled); pm.R$/pm.riskNew stay on the structural stop
	if (!costFilterPasses(Bars, entry, stop, out why)) return;
	journalArm("LE", entry, stop, double.NaN);   // CS-1 row for the entry named "LE"
	m_le.Send(entry, qty);
}
```

### 4.2 `_CmeSessionClock` and the CME/NYSE calendars

**Why it exists.** CME futures trade nearly 24 hours, and the chart's time zone is rarely US Eastern. A strategy that says "trade the first 30 minutes of the cash session" must know, for any bar, the Eastern time, which session it falls in (Asia, Europe, New York, RTH), which Globex trading day it belongs to (an 18:00 ET bar is the next day's session), and whether today is a holiday, a half-day or a day with no cash session.

**When to use it.** Any rule tied to session times. Examples 1, 2, 4, 5, 12, 13, 18 and 23 build the clock themselves; strategies on the futures base use the built-in `SessionClock` and the wrappers listed below.

**Enable.** `new _CmeSessionClock(resolveBarTimeZone(Bars.Info))` (as in `_CmeSessionClockTest`). On the futures bases it is built in and exposed as `SessionClock`.

**Defaults (ET).**
- Globex open 18:00, close 17:00; halt 17:00–18:00.
- Asia 18:00–03:00; Europe 03:00–11:30; NewYork 08:00–17:00.
- `RthStart` 09:30, `RthEnd` 16:15 (session flag), `RthValueEnd` 16:00 (value window).
- `UseCmeHolidayTable = true`.

**API.**

| Member | Plain meaning |
|---|---|
| `ToEastern`, `EasternOf(barTime)` | The bar time in US Eastern (bar time is in the chart zone). |
| `State(barTime)` → `GlobexState {Closed, Halt, Open}` | Is Globex open, in the daily halt, or closed? |
| `Sessions(barTime)` → `[Flags] MarketSession`; `InSession`, `InAny` | Every session that contains the bar (they overlap: 10:05 ET is Europe, NewYork and Rth). |
| `GlobexDate(barTime)`, `GlobexDateEt` | The Globex trading date (Sunday 18:30 ET belongs to Monday). |
| `PrimarySessionEt` | The single main session of an Eastern time. |
| `TimeSinceOpenEt` / `MinutesSinceOpenEt(et, session)`, `TimeUntilCloseEt` / `MinutesUntilCloseEt`, `SessionLength` | How far into, or how far from the end of, a session. |
| `IsOpeningRangeEt(et, session, minutes)`, `IsRthAfterInitialBalanceEt(et, ibMinutes)` | Inside the first N minutes of a session? After the RTH initial balance? |
| `TrySessionBoundsEt(et, session, out open, out close)`, `TryWindow` | Open and close times of the session containing `et`; false for `None` or combined flags. |
| `RthValueEndEt(globexDate)`, `IsRthValueBarEt(et)` | End of the RTH value window (earlier on early-close days); is a close-stamped bar inside `(RthStart, RthValueEnd]`? |
| `IsGlobexClosedDay`, `IsNoRthDay` | Full Globex closure? NYSE holiday with Globex open (no cash session)? |
| `HolidayHaltEt`, `RthEarlyCloseEt` | Holiday halt time and early RTH close (null when none). |
| Hooks `GlobexClosedDayHook`, `NoRthDayHook`, `HolidayHaltHook`, `RthEarlyCloseHook` | Replace the built-in holiday rules. |
| Statics `DefaultGlobexClosedDay`, `InWindow`, `IsSingleFlag` | The default closure rule; a `[start, end)` window test that wraps midnight; is a flag a single session? |
| Static `GlobexTradingDayEt(DateTime et)`, `GlobexTradingDayEt(DateTime et, TimeSpan globexOpen)` (PR #46) | The Globex trading day of an ET time without a clock instance: at or after the open (18:00) it is the next date. `GlobexDateEt` and `_Test6.tradingDayKey` use it. |

**`_CmeHolidays.Rules`.** `Get(date)` returns a `Day` with `Closed`, `NoRth`, `HaltEt`, `RthCloseEt` and `Name`. Constants: `HolidayHaltEt` 13:00, `EarlyCloseEt` 13:15, `GoodFridayCloseEt` 09:15, `MourningCloseEt` 09:30. Helpers: `ClosedDates(year)`, `Observed`, `NthWeekday`, `LastWeekday`, `Easter`.

**Futures-base wrappers** (`_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`, no clock argument needed): `IsRth()`, `IsAsia()`, `IsEurope()`, `IsNewYork()`, `IsLondonNyOverlap()`, `GetGlobexState()`, `GetSessions()`, `MinutesSinceOpen(session)`, `MinutesUntilClose(session)`, `IsOpeningRange(session, minutes)`, `IsRthAfterInitialBalance(ib)`, `TrySessionBounds(...)`, `IsNewGlobexDay()`, `Session` (SessionStats: RTH/ETH/IB, prior-day H/L/C, `IbRange`, `Gap`), `IbMinutes` (60).

**Timing.** Bars are close-stamped. A bar is RTH when its ET close is in `(09:30, value end]`.

**Journal.** The test signal prints `VPF-7 clock globexDate(..) firstBarEt(..) state(..) sessions(..) noRth(..) rth(..)`.

**Tests.** `tests/SessionClockTests`, `tests/CalendarTests` (with `gen.sh`).

**Snippet.** Builds the clock on first use (as examples 1 and 12 do), then on each closed bar finds the Eastern time and Globex date, skips days without a cash session, and measures how far the bar is into RTH. `mins` is minutes since 09:30 ET; `openingRange` is true for bars inside the first 30 RTH minutes.

```csharp
// class field: built lazily because Bars.Info is needed for the chart time zone
private _CmeSessionClock m_clock;
private _CmeSessionClock Clock { get { if (m_clock == null) m_clock = new _CmeSessionClock(resolveBarTimeZone(Bars.Info)); return m_clock; } }

// StartCalc
protected override void StartCalc()
{
	base.StartCalc();
	m_clock = null;                              // rebuilt on the first bar of every run
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	DateTime et = Clock.EasternOf(Bars.Time[0]);       // close stamp in US Eastern
	DateTime gx = Clock.GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);      // Globex trading date (an 18:00 ET bar belongs to the next day)
	if (Clock.IsNoRthDay(gx)) return;                  // NYSE holiday with Globex open: no RTH session today
	DateTime open, close;
	if (Clock.TrySessionBoundsEt(et, _CmeSessionClock.MarketSession.Rth, out open, out close))
	{
		int mins = Clock.MinutesSinceOpenEt(et, _CmeSessionClock.MarketSession.Rth);
		bool openingRange = Clock.IsOpeningRangeEt(et, _CmeSessionClock.MarketSession.Rth, 30);   // first 30 RTH minutes
	}
}
```

### 4.3 M-DAILY `_DailyContext`

**Why it exists.** Intraday strategies usually need daily context: yesterday's high, low and close, a daily ATR for stop sizes, volatility (HV), momentum (TSMOM), the overnight gap. Computing these from intraday bars is error-prone around rolls and holidays, and it is easy to peek at today's unfinished day. M-DAILY builds them from **completed** days only and adjusts its history when the contract rolls.

**When to use it.** Daily levels, ATR-based stops or targets, and volatility filters: examples 9, 10, 17, 20, 27 and 30–33. Regime (§4.4) turns it on automatically.

**Enable.** `NeedsDaily = true` in `Create()` (or in `StartCalc()` before `base.StartCalc()`).

**`DailyConfig` defaults.**
- `dataStream 0` (aggregate Data1 per `tradingDayKey`), `weeklyStream 0`;
- `atrLength 20`, `smaLength 200`, `rsiLength 2`, `tsmomLookbacks {252,126,63}`;
- `hvLengths {}`, `hvAnnualisation 252`, `hvPctLookback 0`, `lbrRsiLength 0`;
- `closeBasis EDailyClose.RthValue`; `HvOn`; `Validate()`.

**API.**

| Member | Plain meaning |
|---|---|
| `Atr20d` | Wilder ATR of daily true range, length `atrLength` (the name is historical). NaN until seeded. |
| `Sma200`, `Rsi2` | Daily SMA (`smaLength`) and RSI (`rsiLength`) of the basis close. |
| `Tsmom(n)` | Time-series momentum: adjusted close / close n days ago − 1. |
| `Hv(n)`, `HvPercentile(n)` | Annualised historical volatility over n days (0.25 = 25%); its percentile (0–100) over the last `hvPctLookback` days. |
| `Roc1`, `Lbr` | One-day change in points; RSI of that change (LBR). |
| `RthCloseAdj`, `RthClosePrev`, `PrevClose` | Roll-adjusted RTH close; unadjusted RTH close; last bar's close of the prior day. |
| `PrevHigh` / `PrevLow` / `PrevDate` / `PrevRange`, `PrevWeekHigh` / `PrevWeekLow` | Prior day's and prior week's levels. |
| `Valid`, `Days` | ATR seeded and a prior day exists; number of completed days. |
| `GapPct(todayOpen)`, host `DailyGapPct` | Today's gap vs the adjusted prior close, as a fraction. |

**Timing.** A day is added when the *next* trading day's first bar arrives, so on every bar `PrevDate` is a completed prior session. With a daily data stream, only the closed daily bar (`HtfClosedIndex`) whose day is before today is used. On a roll (only with RollDetection on) the host calls `OnRoll(day, rollBarOpen - PrevClose)` (`_CustomSignal` line 991): the stored o/h/l/c, RTH closes and weekly values are difference-adjusted, and, by default, the ATR restarts its seed: **`Atr20d` is NaN and `Valid` is false for `atrLength` sessions after every roll.** With `atrLength = 200` and quarterly @ES rolls (about every 63 sessions) the ATR never becomes valid. With RollDetection Off nothing is adjusted and the roll gap enters the ATR.

**ATR across rolls (`DailyConfig.atrRestartOnRoll`, PR #46).** True ranges do not change when a whole history is shifted by the roll offset, so the ATR does not have to restart. Set `DailyConfig.atrRestartOnRoll = false` (default `true`, the old reseed) and the running ATR carries on; only the splice day's true range is skipped, so there is no NaN blackout. Use it when a strategy needs `Atr20d` every day (stops, filters, PS-13-style long ATRs); keep the default for a strict post-roll warm-up. `_Test6` sets it to false (a RegimeGate hash field, `host.atrRestartOnRoll`). Set it before `base.StartCalc()` (lives in `StartCalc` or `Create`):

```csharp
// StartCalc (before base.StartCalc)
protected override void StartCalc()
{
	NeedsDaily = true;
	RollDetection = ERollDetection.Table;          // _Test6 style: fixed roll dates
	for (int y = 2016; y <= 2026; y++)
		for (int m = 3; m <= 12; m += 3) RollDates.Add(EventRules.RollDate(y, m));
	DailyConfig.atrRestartOnRoll = false;          // ATR carries on; only the splice day's TR is skipped
	base.StartCalc();                              // M-DAILY is built here with this config
}
```

**Journal.**
- `DAILY {date} return spans a gap of N business days since {prev}`;
- `DAILY hv{n} first valid {date} …`; `DAILY hv{n}pct first valid …`;
- `DAILY roll reset {date}: history adjusted by {d}, ATR reseeding` (or `…, ATR carried on (splice-day TR skipped)` with `atrRestartOnRoll = false`);
- `DAILY closeBasis=Bar (P3 unverified): …`;
- a Warning: `DAILY Data{n} advanced k bars between Data1 bars`.

**Tests.** `tests/DailyTests` (`gen.sh` generates the calendars).

**Snippet.** Turns M-DAILY on with HV(20) and looks for a close above yesterday's high while volatility is low. `d == null` means M-DAILY is off; `!d.Valid` means warm-up or ATR reseeding after a roll, so the strategy waits.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	NeedsDaily = true;                           // lazy: M-DAILY is built in base.StartCalc only when asked for
	DailyConfig.hvLengths = new[] { 20 };        // also compute HV(20)
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	_DailyContext d = DailyContext;
	if (d == null || !d.Valid) return;           // warm-up, or ATR reseeding after a roll
	bool priorDaysOnly = d.PrevDate < tradingDayKey(Bars.Time[0]);   // always true by design; documents the no-lookahead rule
	if (priorDaysOnly && Bars.Close[0] > d.PrevHigh && d.Hv(20) < 0.25)
	{
		/* breakout above yesterday's high in a calm (HV20 < 25%) market */
	}
}
```

### 4.4 G-R regime engine `_RegimeEngine`

**Why it exists.** The same setup behaves differently in calm and in volatile markets. The regime engine labels each day as one of K market states (0 = calmest) with a hidden-Markov (or Gaussian-mixture) model on daily return, volatility and momentum features. Hysteresis stops the label flipping on noise, and an ATR-tercile fallback covers the warm-up.

**When to use it.** As a filter or a sizing bucket, never as an entry trigger: example 9 (via RegimeGate, §4.5) and `_Test6`.

**Enable.** `RegimeConfig.enabled = true` in `Create()`. This also turns on M-DAILY with HV {20, 63}.

**`RegimeConfig` defaults.**
- Model: `method Hmm`, `K 3`, `kSelect Fixed`, `features {Return, LogHv20, Tsmom63z}`.
- Fitting: `fitSessions 1000`, `minSessions 500`, `refit Monthly`, `refitEveryN 21`, `restarts 10`, `maxIter 200`, `tol 1e-6`, `regCovar 1e-6`, `transPrior 1`.
- Filtering and switching: `warmFilter 63`, `switchProb 0.60`, `switchMargin 0.10`.
- Other: `seed 20261021`, `fallback AtrTercile`, `publish false`, `atrPctLookback 250`, `parkinsonBars 10`, `bicDiagnostic true`, `rollSuspectZ 5`, `gapBusinessDays 4`.
- Methods: `Validate()`, `Copy()`, `IndexOf`.

**Enums.** `ERegimeMethod {Hmm, Gmm}`, `ERegimeKSelect {Fixed, Bic}`, `ERegimeRefit {Monthly, EveryNSessions, Quarterly}`, `ERegimeFallback {AtrTercile, Unknown}`, `ERegimeFeature {Return, LogHv20, Tsmom63z, Parkinson10}`.

**API.** The `Regime` property returns a copy of `RegimeState` (neutral, `Valid=false`, `Label=-1`, when the engine is off).

| Field / method | Plain meaning |
|---|---|
| `Valid`, `Label` | Is there a usable label? The state, 0 = calmest … K−1, −1 = none. |
| `RawArgmax`, `Probs` | Most likely state before hysteresis; the state probabilities. |
| `AsOf`, `FittedThrough` | The day whose close produced the label (usable from the next session); the last day of the fit that produced the model (always before `AsOf`). |
| `K`, `LogLik`, `Refits`, `RelabelConflicts` | Number of states, fit quality, refit count, a model-health count. |
| `UsingFallback` | The label comes from the ATR-tercile fallback, not the model. |
| `Copy()`, `Neutral(K)` | Copy; an empty state. |
| Engine statics `AtrPercentile`, `AtrBucket`, `Hysteresis`, `Argmax` | Building blocks, exposed for tests. |

**Timing.** The engine is fed once per completed M-DAILY day. `AsOf` is that day, and the value is usable from the **next** session. Refits on a roll day are deferred.

**M-PORT.** With `publish = true`, `pm.regime = Label+1` and `pm.regimeP = Probs` are published at session close.

**Journal.** `REGIME {date} label() raw() p() fb()`; `REGIME switch a->b {date}`; `REGIME refit {date} window() K() ll() iters() conv() means()`; `REGIME diag {date} bicK…`; `REGIME fallback reason(…)`; `REGIME refit deferred {date}: roll day`; `REGIME gap …`; `REGIME roll adjust suspect …`; `REGIME relabel-conflict …`.

**Tests.** `tests/RegimeTests` (`reference.py`; `run.sh` can `--export gv`), `tests/GmmTests`.

**Snippet.** Turns the engine on and turns its output into a bucket number. `bucket = -1` means "no usable regime today" (warm-up, fallback, or not yet from a completed day); treat that as its own case rather than as calm.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	RegimeConfig.enabled = true;                 // also turns on M-DAILY with HV {20, 63}
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	RegimeState r = Regime;                      // a copy; neutral (Valid = false, Label = -1) when disabled
	bool usable = r.Valid
		&& !r.UsingFallback                      // HMM label, not the ATR-tercile fallback
		&& r.AsOf < tradingDayKey(Bars.Time[0]); // labelled from completed prior sessions only
	int bucket = usable ? r.Label : -1;          // 0 = calmest; use as a filter or sizing bucket, never as a trigger
}
```

### 4.5 RegimeGate / A2-63 helpers (`_RegimeGate.Function.CS`)

**Why it exists.** To test "does skipping volatile days improve this strategy?" fairly, the rule must be fixed in advance and the gated and ungated runs must be identical apart from the gate. RegimeGate is that pre-registered rule: three arms (U = ungated, G = skip days whose prior-session HMM label is 2, A = skip ATR tercile 2), one decision function, and hashes that prove which configuration produced a run.

**When to use it.** Only for the A2-63 regime experiment: `_Test6` and example 9 (`_Ex09OpenAtrBreakout`). For other filters read `Regime` directly (§4.4).

**Enable.** Build the configs yourself and call `RegimeGate.ApplyArm(RegimeGateConfig, RegimeConfig)` in `StartCalc()` **before** `base.StartCalc()`.

**Defaults.**
- `RegimeGateConfig { arm = ERegimeGateArm.U, placeboSeed = RegimeGate.PlaceboSeed (20261022) }`.
- `A263Config { k 0.5, atrLength 20, rthOpen 09:30, activeEnd 15:30, exitTime 15:55, rthClose 16:00, contracts 1, halfDayExitBefore 5m }`.

**API.**

| Member | Plain meaning |
|---|---|
| `RegimeGate.Decide(arm, RegimeState, session, atrTercile)` → `RegimeGateDecision` | Should this arm trade this session? `allow` is the answer; `reason` (`ERegimeGateReason {Ok, Warmup, Invalid, Fallback}`) says whether the regime was usable; also `session`, `asOf`, `arm`, `label`, `raw`, `atrTercile`, `pFilt`, `fb`, `usable`, `wouldBlock`. |
| `RegimeGate.ApplyArm(gate, regimeConfig)` | Write the arm's settings into `RegimeConfig` (arm A disables the model with `fitSessions = minSessions = 100000`). |
| `RegimeGate.Line`, `TradeFields`, `HashFields`, `Canon`, `Hash`, `Fnv1a64`; `BlockedLabel = 2`, `Version = "A263-RG-2"` | Journal line and canonical hash of the configuration (proves which arm ran). |
| `AtrTercileTracker(lookback, K)`: `.AddDay(date, atr)`, `.Tercile`, `.LastDate` | Which third of its recent range today's daily ATR is in (input for arm A). |
| `A263Core(cfg)`: `IsLatchBar(etClose)`, `Latch(...)`, `Step(etClose, isLong)` → `A263Action {latched, sendStop, stop, exit}`, `HasSignal`, `Threshold`, `Suffix()` | The A2-63 host strategy (a buy stop at the RTH open + k × daily ATR) as a reusable core. |
| `RegimeGateCounts.Add(d, noRth)`, `.Suffix()` | Running counts of usable, warm-up, invalid, fallback, blocked and no-RTH sessions. |

**Hash pins.** U `26b38655261cc0bf`, G `9c1da7b123ca9441`, A `3d9814a5f264c353`.

**Journal.** Header `REGGATE header version() arm() cfg() seed() symbol() bar() barDeviation() firstBar() canon[]`. Per session `REGGATE {date} arm() asOf() label() raw() pFilt() fb() usable() allow() atrT() wouldBlock() why() signal() cfg() seed()`, then `O() atr20d() atrAsOf() atrOk() thr() exitAt() halfDay() noRth()` and the counts `nSess() nUsable() nWarm() nInv() nFb() nBlock() nNoRth()`.

**Tests.** `tests/RegimeGateTests` (`gen.sh`).

**Snippet.** Applies arm G before the engines are built and asks the gate once per bar. `d.allow == false` means "do not trade this session"; the full host (ATR tercile, latch, counts) is example 9.

```csharp
// class field
protected RegimeGateConfig GateCfg { get; } = new RegimeGateConfig();

// StartCalc (the arm must be applied BEFORE base.StartCalc, where the engines are built)
protected override void StartCalc()
{
	GateCfg.arm = ERegimeGateArm.G;              // U = ungated, G = skip HMM label 2, A = skip ATR tercile 2
	string err = GateCfg.Validate();
	if (err != null) throw new ArgumentException(err);
	RegimeGate.ApplyArm(GateCfg, RegimeConfig);  // writes the arm's settings into RegimeConfig
	base.StartCalc();
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	RegimeGateDecision d = RegimeGate.Decide(GateCfg.arm, Regime, Bars.Time[0].Date, -1);   // -1: no ATR tercile (arm A needs one)
	if (!d.allow) return;                        // d.reason says why: Warmup, Invalid, Fallback, or Ok with a blocked label
}
```

### 4.6 M-EVENT `_EventCalendar`

**Why it exists.** Scheduled releases (FOMC, CPI, NFP …) move index futures in seconds, and calendar days (expiry, roll week, half-days, index rebalances) change normal behaviour. A backtest that trades through them without knowing it mixes two different markets. M-EVENT is a calendar with **as-of visibility**: an event is only visible once it was publicly scheduled, so a backtest cannot "know" an unscheduled meeting in advance.

**When to use it.** Blackouts around releases, flattening before a release, or classifying days ("FOMC day", "roll week") for filters or for splitting results: examples 2, 4 and 24.

**Enable.** `EventConfig.enabled = true` in `Create()`.

**`EventConfig` defaults.**
- Scope: `classes Macro|Calendar`, `minTier T3`, `tierOverride {}`, `csvPath ""`, `allowPartialCoverage false`.
- Blackout: `blackoutPreMin 0`, `blackoutPostMin 0` (0/0 = no blackout), `blackoutTier T1`, `blackoutFlatten false`.
- Other: `publish false`, `includeRussell false`; methods `Validate()`, `Canon()`.

**Coverage.** Macro: FOMC, CPI, PPI, NFP, JOLTS, Claims, PCE, GDP, Retail, ISM, Jackson Hole, testimony. Energy: EIA. Calendar: quarterly expiry, roll week, monthly opex, index rebalance, half-day, VIX expiry, Russell recon.

**Enums.**
- `EEventTier {None=0, T3=1, T2=2, T1=3, C=8}` (importance; T1 is the highest);
- `EEventClassMask {None, Macro=1, Calendar=2, Energy=4, All=7}`;
- `EEventKind` (FomcDecision=1 … EiaPetroleum=18, QuarterlyExpiry=30 … RussellRecon=36, Custom=62);
- `EEventFlags` (Sep, Quad, Unscheduled, Scheduled, Delayed, Canceled, Csv, RuleOnly, Pm).

**Records.**
- `EventRec` (one event): `kind`, `tier`, `flags`, `etTime`, `etEnd`, `utc`, `tradingDay`, `knownSinceUtc` (when it became public), `canceledSinceUtc`, `refPeriod`, `name`, `source`; plus `IsSpan`, `Class`, `KindName`.
- `EventDay` (one day's summary): `day`, `maxTier`, `kindsMask`, `hasFomc`, `hasSep`, `isHalfDay`, `isRollWeek`, `isQuarterlyExpiry`, `isOpex`, `isRebalance`, `firstReleaseEt` (TimeSpan?), `count`, `eiaRuleOnly`; plus `Has(kind)`.

**API.**

| Member | Plain meaning |
|---|---|
| Host `EventBlackout`, `EventFlattenNow` | Inside the configured blackout? Should you flatten now (only with `blackoutFlatten`)? False when the engine is off. |
| `TradingDay`, `NowUtc`, `Disabled` | Current trading day and bar-close instant; true only when the table is missing or fails its hash check (all answers are then neutral). |
| `NextEvent(minTier, out e)`, `PrevEvent(minTier, out e)` (+ overloads `(nowUtc, minTier, mask, out e)`) | Next / last known event of at least that tier. |
| `MinutesTo(minTier, mask = Macro)`, `MinutesSince`, `MinutesSinceAt` | Minutes from the bar close to the next event, or since the last one; NaN when none. |
| `IsInWindow(minTier, pre, post, mask = Macro)`, `IsInWindowAt` | Inside the half-open window `[event − pre, event + post)` of a qualifying event? An event not yet announced at the bar (`knownSinceUtc` after the bar open) only opens its post window `[event, event + post)`. On an Unknown-coverage day: true all day (see Timing). |
| `EventsOn(day, mask = All)`, `EventsOnExPost`, `EventsInRange` | Events of a day or range (ExPost = with hindsight, for research only). |
| `Classify(day)`, `Classify(day, asOfUtc)`, `ClassifyExPost(day)` | The day's `EventDay` summary as known at its session open (or at a chosen instant, or with hindsight). |
| `IsRollWeek()`, `IsRollDay()`, `SessionsToExpiry()`, `InSpan(kind)`, `LastT1Instant(out et)` | Roll and expiry position; inside a multi-day span (e.g. roll week)? Latest T1 release at or before now. |
| `ToEt`, `TradingDayOf`, `SessionOpenUtc` | Time conversions. |
| Statics `RuleRollDate`, `RollDateFor`, `EventRules` (`IsNyseBusinessDay`, `ThirdFriday`, `OpexDate`, `RollDate`, `VixExpiry`, `TableSha`) | Calendar rules behind the table. |

**Timing.**
- Stepped once per closed bar with `[open, close)` in UTC.
- An event is visible only once it is known (`knownSinceUtc <= now`) or has happened. Cancellations are honoured as of now.
- The blackout uses the Macro class only, so Energy consumers call `IsInWindow(..., EEventClassMask.Energy)` explicitly.
- Window queries on daily bars return false.
- **Fail-closed coverage (EV-4.6b).** A day whose coverage is Unknown for a class in the consumer's mask (before the first table row, i.e. pre-2016, after the table end in 2027, or a coverage hole) is treated as a blackout for the whole trading day when `allowPartialCoverage = false`: `EventBlackout` and `IsInWindow`/`IsInWindowAt` return true all day, and with `blackoutFlatten` `EventFlattenNow` fires on its first bar. Start dev runs at or after 2016-01, or set `allowPartialCoverage` knowingly.
- **`EventFlattenNow` fires on the edge bar only:** it is true on the first bar that enters a blackout and false on the following bars of the same blackout (EV-6.7). A position opened or still open later in the blackout (order not filled, re-entry) is not re-flagged; block entries on `EventBlackout` and, if needed, flatten on `EventBlackout && StrategyInfo.MarketPosition != 0`.
- **The base never blocks or sends orders.** Acting on `EventBlackout` / `EventFlattenNow` is your job.

**M-PORT keys** (`publish = true`): `pm.evtTier`, `pm.evtMask` (daily); `pm.evtWin`, `pm.evtNext` (per bar). **CS-1 extra columns:** `evtDayTier`, `evtMinSince`.

**Journal.**
- `EVENT created classes() minTier() blackout(pre/post tier) …`; `EVENT table missing: …`; `EVENT coverage rules(…) …`; `EVENT correction …`;
- `EVENT day {yyyy-MM-dd} at {bar} tier() kinds(Kind@HH:mm,…) firstRelease() [rollWeek] [halfDay] [EiaRuleOnly]`;
- `EVENT blackout on|off at {bar} {Kind} {et} ET pre() post() [flatten]`;
- an Error line ending `…; engine disabled` **only** when the table is missing or its hash mismatches (EV-4.6b);
- `COVGAP {Kind} {from}..{to} Unknown …` once at start, and `COVGAP day {yyyy-MM-dd} Unknown Kind(blocks|belowMinTier|NoEvent)` per affected day.

**EV-4.6b (PRs #28/#29).** `Coverage(kind, day)`, `CoverageBlocks(day, mask, minTier, out kind)`, `CoverageBlocks(day, mask)`, `CoverageReport(from, to, mask[, minTier], out flagged)`, `CoverageStats()`, `EventRules.CoverageHoles()`, `EventRules.TableSha()`, `EventRules.ExpectedTableSha`, `EventConfig.tierOverride`: where the table has no data for a kind, so a "no event" answer would be unknown rather than "none". Details in Appendix G.

**Tests.** `tests/EventTests` (`gen.sh`). Tooling lives in `tools/m-event`.

**Snippet.** A 5-minutes-before / 15-minutes-after blackout around T1 releases, with flattening. On each bar the strategy exits if told to, skips entries inside the blackout, and reads the day's classification and the distance to the next T1 event. `toT1` is NaN when no event is ahead in the table's coverage.

```csharp
// class field
private IOrderMarket m_lx;

// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	m_lx = OrderCreator.MarketNextBar(new SOrderParameters(Contracts.Default, "LX", EOrderAction.Sell));
	EventConfig.enabled = true;
	EventConfig.blackoutPreMin = 5;              // blackout from 5 min before ...
	EventConfig.blackoutPostMin = 15;            // ... to 15 min after each T1 (blackoutTier) Macro event
	EventConfig.blackoutFlatten = true;          // also raise EventFlattenNow during the blackout
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	if (EventFlattenNow && StrategyInfo.MarketPosition != 0) { m_lx.Send(); return; }   // the base never sends orders itself
	if (EventBlackout) return;                   // no new entries inside the window
	_EventCalendar ev = EventCalendar;
	if (ev == null) return;                      // disabled, or the table was missing / failed its hash check
	EventDay today = ev.Classify(ev.TradingDay); // as known at today's session open
	bool fomcDay = today.hasFomc;
	double toT1 = ev.MinutesTo(EEventTier.T1);   // minutes to the next T1 Macro event
	EventRec next;
	if (ev.NextEvent(EEventTier.T2, out next) && journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"next {next.KindName} {next.etTime:HH:mm} ET");
}
```

### 4.7 M-AVWAP `_AvwapManager`

**Why it exists.** An anchored VWAP is the volume-weighted average price since a specific event (a swing low, a gap open, the week's open). It answers "where is the average buyer since that event?", and traders use it, with its σ-bands, as dynamic support and resistance. The manager creates anchors automatically on the events you choose, keeps a few alive, and expires them, without lookahead: a swing anchor exists only once the swing is confirmed.

**When to use it.** Pullback-to-VWAP or band-reversion entries, and "price above or below the event VWAP" filters: example 16 (`AvwapEvents = SwingLow`, `maxAgeSessions 60`, set in `Create`).

**Enable.** `AvwapEvents = …` (flags) in `Create()`; `None` = no manager.

**`EAvwapAnchor` flags.** `SwingHigh=1`, `SwingLow=2`, `Breakout=4`, `GapOpen=8`, `VolumeClimax=16`, `WeekOpen=32`, `MonthOpen=64`, `QuarterOpen=128`, `NewsBar=256` (inert), and `Swing` (= SwingHigh | SwingLow).

**`AvwapLimits` defaults.**
- Anchors: `maxAnchors 6`, `maxAgeSessions 10`, `replaceSameType true` (a new anchor of a type expires the old one).
- Bands and slope: `bandSigmas {1,2,3}`, `slopeBars 5`, `dataStream 1`.
- Detection thresholds: `gapAtrFraction 0.5`, `gapFallbackTrMultiple 3`, `climaxZ 3`, `fallbackLength 20`, `backfillBars 2`.
- Placebos: `placebos EAvwapPlacebo.None`, `p2MinPool 10`.

**API.**

| Member | Plain meaning |
|---|---|
| `Anchors` (`IReadOnlyList<_Avwap>`) | The live anchors, oldest first. |
| `Latest(EAvwapAnchor type)` | Newest live anchor of a type, or null. |
| `LastBar`, `FallbackTrAvg` | Last bar fed; the SMA of true range used when M-DAILY ATR is missing. |
| `_Avwap.Id`, `Type`, `AnchorTime`, `AnchorBar` | Which event, and where the VWAP starts. |
| `UsableFrom` | The first time you may use it (pivot confirmation for swings, the anchor time otherwise). |
| `Direction` | +1 up event (swing low, breakout up, gap up), −1 down, 0 neutral. |
| `Value`, `Sigma`, `Band(k)` | The VWAP, its volume-weighted standard deviation, and `Value + k × Sigma` (`Band(-1)` is the −1σ band). |
| `SlopeTicks`, `SlopeAtr` | Slope per bar over `slopeBars`, in ticks and in ATR. |
| `Valid` | Not expired and has a value. |
| `AgeBars`, `AgeSessions`, `Touched`, `TouchCount`, `Expired`, `ExpireReason` | Life-cycle state. |
| `Tag` (`AV:{Type}:{yyyyMMddHHmm}`), `TagFor(arm)` | Journal tag; tag of a placebo arm. |
| `P1`, `P1Sigma`, `P2`, `P2Sigma`, `p2Degraded` | Placebo twins for research: unweighted mean price (P1) and volume-shuffled VWAP (P2). |

**Timing.** Swing anchors become usable at PA pivot *confirmation* (`UsableFrom`); other anchors at the anchor time. GapOpen uses `isRthBar` and the M-DAILY ATR when available. All anchors are invalidated on a roll.

**Dependencies.** Swing and Breakout force PriceAction on. VolumeClimax and P2 force TOD Volume on.
