### 5.8 F-CAL — CME holidays / half-days (VPF-6), CmeSessionClock (VPF-7)

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Done on main.

**Why it exists.** Globex days start at 18:00 ET and CME holidays shorten sessions; dates and windows must follow the exchange clock, not the calendar date.

**Where.** `_CmeHolidays.Function.CS`, `_NyseHolidays.Function.CS`, `_CmeSessionClock.Function.CS` · `_CmeSessionClock`; host `GlobexDate`, `ToEastern`, `tradingDayKey`, `sessionClock`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
public sealed class _CmeSessionClock   // _CmeSessionClock · _CmeSessionClock:24
protected DateTime ToEastern(DateTime barTime)   // _VolumeProfileFuturesBase · _VolumeProfileFuturesBase:204
protected DateTime GlobexDate(DateTime barTime)   // _VolumeProfileFuturesBase · _VolumeProfileFuturesBase:255
protected override DateTime tradingDayKey(DateTime barTime)   // _VolumeProfileFuturesBase · _VolumeProfileFuturesBase:356
protected override TimeSpan sessionClock(DateTime barTime)   // _VolumeProfileFuturesBase · _VolumeProfileFuturesBase:359
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn08`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Converts the bar time to the Globex trading day and Eastern time, and logs the RTH open. A bar at 18:00 ET already belongs to the next day.

```csharp
// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	DateTime day = GlobexDate(Bars.Time[0]);       // 18:00 ET bars belong to the NEXT Globex day
	TimeSpan et = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay;
	TimeSpan clock = sessionClock(Bars.Time[0]);   // the clock CS-7 windows use
	if (journalOn() && et == new TimeSpan(9, 30, 0))
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"{day:yyyy-MM-dd} RTH open, session clock {clock}");
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 1 (§3.1), example 2 (§3.2), example 13 (§3.3), example 12 (§3.4), example 3 (§3.6), example 14 (§3.7), example 18 (§3.8), example 20 (§3.9), example 17 (§3.10), example 4 (§3.11), example 5 (§3.12), example 11 (§3.14), example 15 (§3.15), example 19 (§3.16), example 23 (§3.18), example 24 (§3.19), example 25 (§3.20), example 37 (§3.21), example 6 (§3.23), example 8 (§3.24), example 22 (§3.25), example 26 (§3.26), example 27 (§3.27), example 29 (§3.28), example 30 (§3.29), example 38 (§3.30), example 31 (§3.31), example 7 (§3.33), example 10 (§3.34), example 28 (§3.35), example 32 (§3.36), example 36 (§3.39), example 39 (§3.40), example 41 (§3.41); inherited by example 21, example 40, example 35.

**Pitfalls.**

- A check like `tod >= 15:55` is also true at 19:00 on a Globex chart; bound it (`&& tod < 17:00`) or use `IsRth()`.
- Half-days close RTH at 13:00 ET (13:15 for equity futures); fixed-time exits must respect them.

### 5.9 F-OF — Order-flow helpers (VPF-5) and the FlowQuality chain

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Done on main (MC-BidAsk tier on main since PR #19, off by default; per-stream earliest-tick tagging partial, VERIFY).

**Why it exists.** Bid/ask volume shows who was aggressive inside a bar; stacked imbalances mark one-sided pressure across several prices.

**Where.** `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase.Strategy.CS` · `OrderFlowEnabled`, `BarDelta`, `CumDelta`, `DiagonalImbalances`, `StackedImbalances`, `Absorption`; `_OrderFlow.Function.CS` · `FlowValue`, `FlowQuality`, `FlowSource`, `StackedResult`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
public enum FlowQuality { Exact = 0, High = 1, Medium = 2, Low = 3, Unknown = 4 }   // _OrderFlow · _OrderFlow:38
protected bool OrderFlowEnabled { get; set; }   // _VolumeProfileFuturesBase · _VolumeProfileFuturesBase:588
public _OrderFlow.FlowValue BarDelta(int barsAgo)   // _VolumeProfileFuturesBase · _VolumeProfileFuturesBase:874
public _OrderFlow.FlowValue CumDelta(int barsAgo)   // _VolumeProfileFuturesBase · _VolumeProfileFuturesBase:882
public _OrderFlow.StackedResult StackedImbalances(int barsAgo, double ratio = 3.0, double minVol = 10, int minStack = 3)   // _VolumeProfileFuturesBase · _VolumeProfileFuturesBase:898
public _OrderFlow.FlowValue Absorption(int barsAgo, _OrderFlow.Side side, int edgeTicks = 2, double volMult = 2.0, int holdTicks = 1)   // _VolumeProfileFuturesBase · _VolumeProfileFuturesBase:906
protected _OrderFlow.FlowConfig FlowCfg { get; }   // _VolumeProfileFuturesBase · _VolumeProfileFuturesBase:591
protected _OrderFlow.FlowEngine Flow { get; }   // _VolumeProfileFuturesBase · _VolumeProfileFuturesBase:612
```

**Bounded window (PR #48).** The engine keeps at most `FlowCfg.MaxBars` footprint bars (default 5000, minimum 10; `Flow.BarCap` is the effective cap) and drops the oldest beyond that; `Flow.DroppedBars` is the running total of dropped bars over the whole run (it never resets at a session/RTH reset). `CumDelta` sums its reset period (the Globex session, or RTH with `FlowCfg.ResetMode = Rth`) from those bars, so when that period started before the oldest kept bar the value is short and `FlowValue.Truncated` is true (`ToString()` adds ` trunc`; only CumDelta sets it). Use the flag to skip or down-weight session-delta signals on long sessions with fine bars; raise `MaxBars` in `Create()` if it fires often. The engine also journals the first quality downgrade per session (`OF quality downgrade session() bar() from->to source() …`, Journal) and warns once when the cap is first hit (`OF MaxBars cap N hit at bar …`, Warning).

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn09`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Turns order flow on (with the default 5000-bar window set explicitly), reads the bar delta, and logs stacked imbalances (≥ 3 prices, ratio 3:1, ≥ 10 contracts). Bars whose flow quality is below `High` (Medium, Low, Unknown) are skipped. It then refuses a truncated session `CumDelta` and journals how many bars the window has dropped.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	OrderFlowEnabled = true;                       // lazy: no footprints otherwise
	FlowCfg.MaxBars = 5000;                        // footprint window (default 5000, min 10); older bars are dropped
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	_OrderFlow.FlowValue d = BarDelta(0);
	if (!d.IsAvailable || d.Quality > _OrderFlow.FlowQuality.High) return;   // Exact/High only for signals
	_OrderFlow.StackedResult st = StackedImbalances(0, 3.0, 10, 3);
	if (st.Found && journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"delta {d.Value} stacked runs {st.Runs.Count} longest {st.Longest}");
	_OrderFlow.FlowValue cd = CumDelta(0);
	if (cd.Truncated) return;                      // session CumDelta lost its oldest bars to the FlowCfg.MaxBars cap
	if (Flow != null && Flow.DroppedBars > 0 && journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"flow window full: {Flow.DroppedBars} bars dropped (cap {Flow.BarCap})");
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 38 (§3.30).

**Pitfalls.**

- Before the tick window only BarUpDown (Low) flow exists; never trade on it. Report results per FlowQuality tier.
- `FlowQuality` is ordered Exact < High < Medium < Low < Unknown; "at least High" is `Quality <= High`.
- Absorption and single-price delta need tier >= 3 (catalogue data-quality rule).
- A `CumDelta` with `Truncated = true` is missing the session's oldest bars; do not compare it with untruncated values.

### 5.10 F-VWAP — VWAP engine, 3 slots with σ

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Done on main; `EVwapType` adds Week, Month, Quarter, RollDate (M-AVWAP-lite).

**Why it exists.** VWAP is the volume-weighted average price institutions benchmark against; its σ bands measure how stretched price is.

**Where.** `_VolumeProfileBase.Strategy.CS` · `Vwap1Type…Vwap3Type` ([Input]s), `Vwap1Length`, `Vwap1FromDate`, `CurrentVwap1…3`, `CurrentVwap1Sigma…`; `_VpVwapEngine.Function.CS`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
public enum EVwapType { Disabled = 0, Session = 1, Length = 2, AdaptiveFromDate = 3, Week = 4, Month = 5, Quarter = 6, RollDate = 7 }   // _VolumeProfileBase · _VolumeProfileBase:59
[Input] public EVwapType Vwap1Type { get; set; }   // _VolumeProfileBase · _VolumeProfileBase:505
public double CurrentVwap1 { get; }   // _VolumeProfileBase · _VolumeProfileBase:1708
public double CurrentVwap1Sigma { get; }   // _VolumeProfileBase · _VolumeProfileBase:1717
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn10`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Sets slot 1 to the session VWAP and slot 2 to the weekly VWAP, and logs closes more than 2σ above the session VWAP: price is stretched.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	Vwap1Type = EVwapType.Session;                 // VP-base [Input]: set in code
	Vwap2Type = EVwapType.Week;
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	double v = CurrentVwap1;                       // session VWAP
	double sd = CurrentVwap1Sigma;                 // its standard deviation
	double w = CurrentVwap2;                       // weekly VWAP
	if (!double.IsNaN(v) && Bars.Close[0] > v + 2 * sd && journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"> +2σ session VWAP; week VWAP {w}");
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 3 (§3.6), example 14 (§3.7), example 20 (§3.9).

**Pitfalls.**

- The slots are `[Input]`s on the VP base; set them in `Create()` so the strategy still exposes no inputs of its own.
- Session VWAP resets on the chart session; on a Globex chart that is 18:00 ET, not 09:30.

### 5.11 F-CONF — Confluence engine

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Done on main (existing).

**Why it exists.** A price where several independent levels cluster is more likely to react than any single level.

**Where.** `_VolumeProfileBase.Strategy.CS` · `GetConfluenceZones`, `GetTopConfluenceZones`, `GetConfluenceTargets`, `ConfluenceZone`, `Confluence*` settings; `_VpConfluenceEngine.Function.CS`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
public List<ConfluenceZone> GetConfluenceZones()   // _VolumeProfileBase · _VolumeProfileBase:1602
public List<ConfluenceZone> GetTopConfluenceZones(int count)   // _VolumeProfileBase · _VolumeProfileBase:1623
public List<ConfluenceTarget> GetConfluenceTargets(double price, EVpDirection direction)   // _VolumeProfileBase · _VolumeProfileBase:1640
protected bool UsesConfluenceZones { get; set; }   // _VolumeProfileBase · _VolumeProfileBase:636
protected internal double ConfluenceSplitAtrFraction { get; set; }   // _VolumeProfileBase · _VolumeProfileBase:626
public static double SplitWidth(double tol, double atr20d, double atrFraction)   // VpConfluenceRules · _VpConfluenceEngine:38
```

**Zone width (PR #47).** Anchors within `ConfluenceAlignTicks` (tol) of each other chain into one cluster. A cluster of ≥ 3 anchors wider than `VpConfluenceRules.SplitWidth` = max(4 × tol, `ConfluenceSplitAtrFraction` × Atr20d) (default fraction 0.03; 4 × tol alone while Atr20d is not ready) is split at its largest internal gap (the lowest one on a tie). The ATR term keeps zones proportional to the market's daily range: on a quiet day 4 × tol rules, on a volatile day a zone may be up to 3% of the daily ATR wide. Atr20d comes only from M-DAILY, so a strategy that reads zones or targets sets `UsesConfluenceZones = true` in `Create()` (or before `base.StartCalc()`); the base then turns on `NeedsDaily` when a confluence slot is enabled. Without it the split uses 4 × tol and a one-time Warning says so (`VPCONF: confluence zones used but UsesConfluenceZones is false …`). Each split is journaled `VPCONF split anchors() range() width() limit() at() gap() atr20d() basis(atr|tol-fallback)`. Enabling two confluence slots on the same data stream now throws in `StartCalc`.

**Developing profile.** Labels from today's unfinished profile are tagged `ConfluenceHit.developing` and also listed in `ConfluenceZone.developingHits` (null when there are none; `HasDeveloping`). `developingScore` is their credit, already included in `rawScore` (report only). `independentSources` counts distinct independent volume masses (stream, profile window); `profileCount` the distinct profiles among them.

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn11`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Declares that it reads zones (so the ATR split is used), then logs the three strongest confluence zones, skipping a zone whose whole score comes from the developing profile. A higher `zScore` and more `independentSources` = a stronger zone.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	UsesConfluenceZones = true;                    // builds M-DAILY so the zone split can use 0.03 x Atr20d
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	foreach (ConfluenceZone z in GetTopConfluenceZones(3))
	{
		if (z.HasDeveloping && z.developingScore >= z.rawScore) continue;   // only today's unfinished profile backs it
		if (journalOn())
			trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"zone {z.low}-{z.high} z {z.zScore:F2} sources {z.independentSources}");
	}
}
```

**Used in examples.** none.

**Pitfalls.**

- Zones score several level sources together; their weights are settings, not fitted values. Treat them as a level list for Gate-0, not as evidence.
- Forgetting `UsesConfluenceZones = true` gives narrower zones (4 × tol only) than the hosts on main (`_DeltaDivRevFuturesLELX`, `_VolumeProfileTest1` with `TraceConfluence`).
- A developing-profile label can move until the session ends; check `HasDeveloping` before treating a zone as settled.

### 5.12 F-TM — Trade-management primitives (CS-7)

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Done on main.

**Why it exists.** Time windows, trade caps and time stops keep a strategy out of the hours and situations it was not designed for.

**Where.** `_CustomSignal.Strategy.CS` · `NoEntryBefore`, `NoEntryAfter`, `FlatBy`, `MaxTradesPerDay`, `DailyLossStopCount`, `DailyLossStopR`, `RiskUnitDollars`, `TimeStopBars`, `entryAllowed(out string)`, `mustFlatten()`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
protected TimeSpan? NoEntryAfter { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2664
protected TimeSpan? FlatBy { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2666
protected int MaxTradesPerDay { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2668
protected int DailyLossStopCount { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2670
protected double DailyLossStopR { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2672
protected double RiskUnitDollars { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2674
protected int TimeStopBars { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2676
protected bool entryAllowed(out string reason)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2730
protected bool mustFlatten()   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2749
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn12`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Sets the entry window, flat time, trade cap and time stop, then gates entries. When in a position, `mustFlatten()` says it is time to exit; when flat, `reason` names the rule that blocked.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	NoEntryAfter = new TimeSpan(14, 30, 0);        // no new entries after 14:30
	FlatBy = new TimeSpan(15, 55, 0);              // flat by 15:55
	MaxTradesPerDay = 2;
	TimeStopBars = 18;                             // exit after 18 bars in the trade
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	if (StrategyInfo.MarketPosition != 0)
	{
		if (mustFlatten()) { /* send the market exit */ }
		return;
	}
	string reason;
	if (!entryAllowed(out reason)) return;         // reason says which rule blocked
	/* entry logic */
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 13 (§3.3), example 12 (§3.4), example 18 (§3.8), example 20 (§3.9), example 17 (§3.10), example 23 (§3.18), example 25 (§3.20), example 37 (§3.21), example 40 (§3.22), example 22 (§3.25), example 26 (§3.26), example 27 (§3.27), example 29 (§3.28), example 30 (§3.29), example 38 (§3.30), example 31 (§3.31), example 28 (§3.35), example 32 (§3.36), example 36 (§3.39); inherited by example 24, example 35.

**Pitfalls.**

- The CS-7 rules act only where you call them: `entryAllowed` before entries and `mustFlatten` while in a position.
- `DailyLossStopCount` counts all losing closed trades of the day (not consecutive ones); `DailyLossStopR` needs `RiskUnitDollars` > 0 and uses closed P&L.
- Windows use `sessionClock`, which on the futures base is ET wall time.

### 5.13 F-JRNL — Journal: R, MFE/MAE, run id (CS-1/CS-3); sizeForRisk (CS-4); cost filter

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Done on main.

**Why it exists.** A per-trade record in R (risk units) with MFE/MAE shows how good the entries and exits actually were.

**Where.** `_CustomSignal.Strategy.CS` · `journalOn()`, `journalArm`, `trace`, `sizeForRisk`, `equityAvailable`; `_SignalJournal.Function.CS`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
protected void journalArm(string entryOrderName, double plannedEntry, double initialStop, double target, IDictionary<string, string> context = null)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2198
protected double equityAvailable(double maxCustomRiskEquityPerPosPercent = 0.0d)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2355
protected int sizeForRisk(IInstrument bars, double entry, double stop, double riskFraction, SizingLimits limits)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2791
protected double equityAvailable(double fraction, bool includeOpenPnl)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2829
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn13`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Turns the journal on, skips trades whose dollar risk exceeds 1 % of available equity, and records the planned entry, stop and 1R target next to the Send.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	TraceLevel = ETraceLevel.Journal;              // journal on; no [Input]
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	double entry = Bars.Close[0];
	double stop = entry - 14 * tickSize();         // 14-tick stop
	double riskDollars = Bars.Info.BigPointValue * (entry - stop);
	if (riskDollars > equityAvailable(0.01)) return;   // skip, never resize
	if (journalOn())
		journalArm("LE", entry, stop, entry + (entry - stop), null);   // call next to the entry Send
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 1 (§3.1), example 2 (§3.2), example 13 (§3.3), example 12 (§3.4), example 16 (§3.5), example 3 (§3.6), example 14 (§3.7), example 18 (§3.8), example 20 (§3.9), example 17 (§3.10), example 5 (§3.12), example 9 (§3.13), example 11 (§3.14), example 15 (§3.15), example 19 (§3.16), example 23 (§3.18), example 25 (§3.20), example 37 (§3.21), example 6 (§3.23), example 22 (§3.25), example 26 (§3.26), example 27 (§3.27), example 29 (§3.28), example 30 (§3.29), example 38 (§3.30), example 31 (§3.31), example 7 (§3.33), example 10 (§3.34), example 28 (§3.35), example 32 (§3.36), example 33 (§3.37), example 34 (§3.38), example 36 (§3.39); inherited by example 21, example 24, example 40, example 35.

**Pitfalls.**

- `journalArm` must be called on the bar the entry order is sent, with the planned stop, so R and MFE/MAE are measured against it.
- User rule: a trade that breaches the 0.01 × equity risk budget is skipped, never resized; `sizeForRisk` is shown in §4 but not used by the examples.
- **The floor is example code, not library behaviour.** The stop floor (stop = max(structural, 14 ticks); skip if q × widened stop × $50 > 0.01 × equity; never shrink q) is Ettienne's decided rule (2026-10-23), implemented **per strategy**: main (a07257a) has no stop floor or widening, and each example implements it locally (`costFloorTicks = 14`, `riskBudgetFraction = 0.01`, a local `overRiskBudget` or an inline check using `equityAvailable(fraction)`). The library part is **pending**: a library `CostConfig` is only specified in `instrument-profile-spec.md` IP-3.2/IP-8.1 (not built). The only library cost rule is `CostFilterMaxFractionOfR` (default 0 = off), which skips and never widens. Consequence: a strategy that sizes at exactly 1% risk skips every stop under 14 ticks (any widening breaches the budget); the floor widens without a skip only for fixed size or sizing below 1%. The examples trade a fixed 1 contract, so a 14-tick stop ($175) is skipped only when equity < $17,500.

### 5.14 F-EVT — logSignal forward MFE/MAE + ControlMode (CS-8)

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Done on main; this is the Gate-3 event-study harness.

**Why it exists.** Measuring what happens after every signal, traded or not, separates edge from execution, and control modes test it against randomised signals.

**Where.** `_CustomSignal.Strategy.CS` · `logSignal`, `ControlMode`, `ControlShiftTicks`, `ControlDelayBars`, `ControlSeed`, `ControlRandomProbability`, `controlAdjustLevel`, `controlAdjustDelayBars`, `controlRandomEntry`, `SignalHorizons`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
public enum EControlMode { None = 0, RandomEntry = 1, ShiftLevels = 2, DelayEntryBars = 3 }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2518
protected EControlMode ControlMode { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2523
protected int ControlShiftTicks { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2528
protected int ControlDelayBars { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2530
protected void logSignal(string tag, int direction, double price, double stop, IDictionary<string, string> context, bool taken)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2552
protected bool controlRandomEntry()   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2642
protected double controlAdjustLevel(double level)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2648
protected int controlAdjustDelayBars()   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2654
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn14`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Logs every breakout of the prior high as a signal with a 14-tick stop; the journal then reports its forward MFE/MAE at the configured horizons whether or not it was traded.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	TraceLevel = ETraceLevel.Journal;              // logSignal is a no-op below Journal
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	double level = controlAdjustLevel(Bars.High[1]);   // identity unless ControlMode = ShiftLevels
	if (Bars.Close[0] > level)
		logSignal("BRK", +1, Bars.Close[0], Bars.Close[0] - 14 * tickSize(), null, false);   // forward MFE/MAE at SignalHorizons
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 1 (§3.1), example 2 (§3.2), example 13 (§3.3), example 12 (§3.4), example 16 (§3.5), example 3 (§3.6), example 14 (§3.7), example 20 (§3.9), example 5 (§3.12), example 11 (§3.14), example 15 (§3.15), example 24 (§3.19), example 6 (§3.23), example 29 (§3.28), example 7 (§3.33), example 10 (§3.34), example 32 (§3.36); inherited by example 21, example 35.

**Pitfalls.**

- `logSignal` writes `MCEvents_<runId>.csv`; it records every event, taken or not, so log the event before skip filters if you study the filter.
- Controls (random entry, shifted levels, delayed entries) are the placebo arms; run them with the same code path.

### 5.15 M-AVWAP — AVWAP anchor manager

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Available on main (PR #7): NewsBar anchor reserved and inert; P1/P2 placebo twins built in (PR #19); roll invalidation only on the futures base.

**Why it exists.** A VWAP anchored at a swing or gap shows the average price of everyone who traded since that event, a common pullback level.

**Where.** `_AvwapManager.Function.CS` · `_AvwapManager`, `_Avwap`, `EAvwapAnchor`, `AvwapLimits`, `EAvwapPlacebo`; `_CustomSignal.AvwapEvents`, `AvwapLimits`, `AvwapManager`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
protected EAvwapAnchor AvwapEvents { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:820
protected AvwapLimits AvwapLimits { get; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:822
internal _AvwapManager AvwapManager   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:828
public enum EAvwapAnchor { None = 0, SwingHigh = 1 << 0, SwingLow = 1 << 1, Breakout = 1 << 2, GapOpen = 1 << 3, VolumeClimax = 1 << 4, WeekOpen = 1 << 5, MonthOpen = 1 << 6, QuarterOpen = 1 << 7, NewsBar = 1 << 8, Swing = SwingHigh | SwingLow }   // _AvwapManager · _AvwapManager:15
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn15`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Anchors VWAPs at swing lows and gap opens and logs a touch of the newest swing-low VWAP. `Band(1)` is the +1σ band.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	AvwapEvents = EAvwapAnchor.SwingLow            // lazy: no anchors unless set
		| EAvwapAnchor.GapOpen;
	AvwapLimits.maxAgeSessions = 5;
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	_AvwapManager am = AvwapManager;
	_Avwap a = am != null ? am.Latest(EAvwapAnchor.SwingLow) : null;   // newest swing-low anchor
	if (a != null && a.Valid && Bars.Low[0] <= a.Value && journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"touch {a.Tag} {a.Value:F2} ±1σ {a.Band(1):F2}");
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 16 (§3.5).

**Pitfalls.**

- The `Breakout` anchor fires on a PRICEACTION break-of-structure, not on a profile/composite edge break; Ex28 hand-rolls a break-anchored VWAP for that reason.
- Swing anchors are usable only from the pivot's confirmation bar (`UsableFrom`); earlier values would be lookahead.

### 5.16 M-SWING — PRICEACTION swing / structure engine

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Available on main (PR #6); extra streams via `ExtraPriceAction` (PR #19); MC acceptance trace (Q3) not yet run.

**Why it exists.** Swing pivots and break of structure (BOS) define trend in price-action terms; a second engine on a higher timeframe gives the bigger trend.

**Where.** `_PriceActionEngine.Function.CS` · `_PriceActionEngine`, `PriceActionConfig`, `EStructure`; `_CustomSignal.PriceActionConfig`, `PriceActionEngine`, `ExtraPriceAction`, `PriceActionFor`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
protected PriceActionConfig PriceActionConfig { get; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:380
internal _PriceActionEngine PriceActionEngine   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:385
protected List<PriceActionConfig> ExtraPriceAction { get; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:724
internal _PriceActionEngine PriceActionFor(int dataStream, string role = null)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:735
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn16`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Runs PRICEACTION on Data1 and Data2 and logs a Data1 break of structure up while the Data2 structure is up: a pullback-continuation setup.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	PriceActionConfig.enabled = true;              // Data1 engine (futures preset applied by the base)
	ExtraPriceAction.Add(new PriceActionConfig     // a second engine on Data2 (must be on the chart)
	{
		enabled = true,
		dataStream = 2,
		role = "htf"
	});
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	_PriceActionEngine pa = PriceActionEngine;     // Data1
	_PriceActionEngine htf = PriceActionFor(2, "htf");
	if (pa == null || htf == null) return;
	if (htf.Structure(0) == EStructure.Up && pa.BosUp && journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"BOS up, last swing low {pa.LastLow().price}");
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 19 (§3.16), example 26 (§3.26), example 31 (§3.31), example 28 (§3.35), example 34 (§3.38).

**Pitfalls.**

- Pivots are confirmed `strength` bars after the extreme; use the engine's confirmed values, never `Bars.Low[k]` of an unconfirmed pivot.
- Call `PriceActionFor` with the same role string used in `ExtraPriceAction`.
- The snippet requires Data2 on the chart (the `dataStream = 2` engine); without it `StartCalc` throws `ArgumentException` (`requireDataStream`, `_CustomSignal` L764/L2333).

### 5.17 M-MTF — Multi-TF via native BarsOfData(n)

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Available on main (PR #8); Probe P2 still to be run in MC.

**Why it exists.** Higher-timeframe levels must come from closed bars only, or a backtest sees the future.

**Where.** `_CustomSignal.Strategy.CS` · `HtfStreams`, `HtfClosed`, `HtfClosedBar`, `HtfForming`; `_MtfMath.Function.CS`; probe `_MtfProbe.Strategy.CS`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
protected int[] HtfStreams { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1658
protected double HtfClosed(int ds, int ago = 0)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1665
protected void HtfClosedBar(int ds, int ago, out DateTime time, out double open, out double high, out double low, out double close, out double volume)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1667
protected double HtfForming(int ds)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1674
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn17`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Reads the last closed Data2 bar and logs a Data1 close above its high. The forming Data2 bar is never used.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	HtfStreams = new[] { 2 };                      // Data2 is read as closed bars only
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	DateTime t;
	double o, h, l, c, v;
	HtfClosedBar(2, 0, out t, out o, out h, out l, out c, out v);   // last CLOSED Data2 bar
	if (Bars.Close[0] > h && journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"above last closed HTF high {h} ({t:HH:mm})");
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 2 (§3.2), example 13 (§3.3), example 14 (§3.7), example 5 (§3.12), example 11 (§3.14), example 6 (§3.23), example 7 (§3.33), example 10 (§3.34), example 36 (§3.39).

**Pitfalls.**

- `BarsOfData(2).Close[0]` is the FORMING higher-TF bar during the HTF bar; use `HtfClosed`/`HtfClosedBar` for rules.
- Orders go to Data1 only.

### 5.18 M-TOD — Time-of-day normalisers

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Available on main (PR #9); delta fed only by the futures base (BarUpDown → NaN).

**Why it exists.** Volume and range follow a strong daily pattern; comparing a bar with the same minute of prior sessions shows whether it is truly unusual.

**Where.** `_TodNormaliser.Function.CS` · `_TodNormaliser` (`Z`, `Median`, `Pct`, `ZCum`), `ETodMetric`; `_CustomSignal.TodMetrics`, `TodSessions`, `TodMinSessions`, `TodNormaliser`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
protected ETodMetric TodMetrics { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:843
protected int TodSessions { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:845
internal _TodNormaliser TodNormaliser   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:855
public enum ETodMetric { None = 0, Volume = 1 << 0, Delta = 1 << 1, Range = 1 << 2, TrueRange = 1 << 3 }   // _TodNormaliser · _TodNormaliser:15
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn18`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Enables volume and range normalisation over 20 sessions and logs bars whose volume is ≥ 2σ above the same minute's norm.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	TodMetrics = ETodMetric.Volume                 // lazy
		| ETodMetric.Range;
	TodSessions = 20;
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close || TodNormaliser == null) return;
	double vz = TodNormaliser.Z(ETodMetric.Volume);       // vs the same clock minute over the prior sessions
	if (vz >= 2 && journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"volume z {vz:F2}, range pct {TodNormaliser.Pct(ETodMetric.Range):F2}");
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 4 (§3.11), example 19 (§3.16), example 25 (§3.20).

**Pitfalls.**

- Z is NaN until `TodMinSessions` sessions exist for that clock minute.
- Half-days are excluded from the norms by the futures base.

### 5.19 M-DAILY — Daily context: ATR20d, SMA200, RSI(2), TSMOM, HV

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Available on main (PR #10; HV and LBR since PR #19); Probe P3 (dxFeed daily = settlement?) open.

**Why it exists.** Daily ATR, momentum and volatility scale stops and filters to the current market.

**Where.** `_DailyContext.Function.CS` · `_DailyContext` (`Atr20d`, `Sma200`, `Rsi2`, `Tsmom(n)`, `Hv(n)`, `HvPercentile`, `Lbr`, `PrevHigh/Low/Close`, `Valid`), `DailyConfig`; `_CustomSignal.NeedsDaily`, `DailyConfig`, `DailyContext`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
protected bool NeedsDaily { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:908
protected DailyConfig DailyConfig { get; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:910
internal _DailyContext DailyContext   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:920
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn19`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Requests the daily stream before `base.StartCalc()` and logs 20-day ATR, momentum and volatility once `Valid`.

```csharp
// StartCalc (before base.StartCalc)
protected override void StartCalc()
{
	NeedsDaily = true;                             // must be set BEFORE base.StartCalc()
	DailyConfig.hvLengths = new[] { 20 };
	base.StartCalc();
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close || DailyContext == null || !DailyContext.Valid) return;
	double atr = DailyContext.Atr20d;              // 20-day ATR
	double mom = DailyContext.Tsmom(20);           // 20-day time-series momentum
	double hv = DailyContext.Hv(20);               // 20-day historical volatility
	if (journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"ATR20d {atr:F2} TSMOM20 {mom:P1} HV20 {hv:F3}");
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 20 (§3.9), example 17 (§3.10), example 9 (§3.13), example 27 (§3.27), example 30 (§3.29), example 31 (§3.31), example 10 (§3.34), example 32 (§3.36), example 33 (§3.37); inherited by example 35.

**Pitfalls.**

- Set `NeedsDaily` in `StartCalc()` before `base.StartCalc()` (or in `Create()`); later is too late.
- With RollDetection on, ATR is reseeded at each roll and is NaN for `atrLength` sessions, unless `DailyConfig.atrRestartOnRoll = false` (PR #46; §4.3), which carries it on and skips only the splice day's TR.

### 5.20 M-EVENT — Scheduled-event calendar (M-NEWS / M-EVENT)

**Status on main a07257a.** ◐ Scheduled-event calendar available on main (PRs #25, #27–#29); unscheduled news not covered; `EAvwapAnchor.NewsBar` inert; GDP/PCE coverage holes 2016–24 (fail-closed).

**Why it exists.** Tier-1 releases (CPI, FOMC, NFP) cause jumps a normal strategy cannot handle; the calendar blocks entries and can flatten around them.

**Where.** `_EventCalendar.Function.CS` · `_EventCalendar`, `EventConfig`, `EEventKind`, `EEventTier`; `_CustomSignal.EventConfig`, `EventCalendar`, `EventBlackout`, `EventFlattenNow`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
protected EventConfig EventConfig { get; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1071
internal _EventCalendar EventCalendar   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1078
protected bool EventBlackout { get; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1087
protected bool EventFlattenNow { get; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1089
public bool EventBlackout { get; }   // _EventCalendar · _EventCalendar:1748
public bool EventFlattenNow { get; }   // _EventCalendar · _EventCalendar:1749
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn20`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Enables a 15-minute blackout around tier-1 events: no new entries inside it, and flatten when `EventFlattenNow` is set.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	EventConfig.enabled = true;                    // lazy
	EventConfig.blackoutPreMin = 15;               // no entries 15 min before ...
	EventConfig.blackoutPostMin = 15;              // ... and 15 min after a release
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	if (EventFlattenNow)
	{
		/* flatten */
		return;
	}
	if (EventBlackout) return;                     // no new entries around tier-1 releases
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 2 (§3.2), example 4 (§3.11), example 24 (§3.19).

**Pitfalls.**

- With `allowPartialCoverage = false` (default) the calendar blocks days with unknown coverage; about 15–18 % of 2016–22 days under the Macro mask (catalogue EV-4.6b).
- Unscheduled news is not covered.

**Status on main a07257a.** ◐ Available on main for counts/timing (PR #12); trade-size outputs off until `sizeOutputsVerified`.


**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn21`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Requests tick data and logs bars with a print burst (z ≥ 3), with print count and the busiest second.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	TickConfig.enabled = true;                     // lazy DataLoader 1-tick Trade request for Data1's symbol
	TickConfig.burstZ = 3.0;
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	TickBarStats t = TickStats;                    // aggregate of the last closed Data1 bar
	if (t != null && t.valid && t.burst && journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"burst z {t.burstZ:F1}, {t.prints} prints, max {t.maxPrintsPerSecond}/s");
}
```

### 5.22 M-XMKT — Second-instrument stream

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Available on main (PR #11); VIX/ZN depend on dxFeed bundles; orders on Data1 only.

**Why it exists.** A related market (NQ for ES) confirms or contradicts a move; correlation, beta and relative strength quantify that.

**Where.** `_XmktLeg.Function.CS` · `_XmktLeg` (`Available`, `LogReturn`, `Correlation`, `Beta`, `RsZ`), `XmktLegConfig`; `_CustomSignal.XmktLegs`, `XmktLeg(role)`

```csharp
internal _XmktLeg XmktLeg(string role)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1166
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn22`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Adds NQ on Data2 as a cross-market leg and logs its rolling correlation, beta and relative-strength z. `rsZ` negative = NQ weak vs Data1.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	XmktLegs.Add(new XmktLegConfig
	{
		role = "NQ",                               // name used by XmktLeg("NQ")
		dataStream = 2,                            // NQ on Data2
		corrLength = 60                            // bars in the rolling correlation
	});
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	_XmktLeg nq = XmktLeg("NQ");
	if (nq != null && nq.Available && journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"rho {nq.Correlation:F2} beta {nq.Beta:F2} rsZ {nq.RsZ:F2}");
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 39 (§3.40).

**Pitfalls.**

- Check `Available` (staleness) before reading values.
- The correlation is of bar returns, not of strategy P&L (see Ex39).

### 5.23 M-PORT — Portfolio-level sizing/throttles

**Status on main a07257a.** ◐ Coded on main (PRs #14, #17, #22); Probe P5b live checks (L1–L9) not yet run.

**Why it exists.** Several strategies in Portfolio Trader share one risk budget; publishing each strategy's state lets one money manager size the whole book.

**Where.** `_CustomSignal.Strategy.CS` · `PortfolioPublish`, `PortfolioFamily`, `PortfolioRegime`, `PortfolioScore`, `PortfolioHalted`, `PortfolioCluster` (virtuals), `portfolioArm`, `portfolioStop`; `_PortMM.Function.CS` · `_PortMM`, `PortMmConfig`; `_PortfolioRiskManager.Strategy.CS`

```csharp
protected virtual bool PortfolioPublish { get; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1513

protected virtual bool PortfolioHalted { get; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1530

```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn23`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Names the strategy's family and announces each planned long entry to the portfolio money manager; outside Portfolio Trader `portfolioArm` does nothing.

```csharp
// class member (property override)
protected override string PortfolioFamily { get { return "PS"; } }

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	double entry = Bars.Close[0];
	double stop = entry - 14 * tickSize();
	portfolioArm(+1, entry, stop, 1);              // declare the pending entry (no-op outside Portfolio Trader)
	/* m_le.Send(1); while in position: portfolioStop(currentStop); */
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 37 (§3.21), example 40 (§3.22).

**Pitfalls.**

- Publishing is on only in Portfolio Trader; chart runs publish nothing.
- The book-level daily kill and flatten live in `_PortfolioRiskManager` (`PortMmConfig.dailyLossStop`, `dailyLossFlatten`), not in the strategy.

### 5.24 M-COST — Style-dependent slippage, p90 stop slippage, haircut

**Status on main a07257a.** ◐ Not a module: MC native Commission/Slippage + the optional cost filter; style, p90 and haircut are not coded.

**Why it exists.** Short-stop trades can lose most of their edge to commission and slippage; the filter skips trades whose round-trip cost is too large a share of R.

**Where.** `_CustomSignal.Strategy.CS` · `CostFilterMaxFractionOfR`, `roundTripCostPerContract()` (virtual), `costFilterPasses`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
protected double CostFilterMaxFractionOfR { get; set; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2846
protected virtual double roundTripCostPerContract()   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2852
protected bool costFilterPasses(IInstrument bars, double entry, double stop, out string reason)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:2858
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn24`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Skips any trade whose round-trip cost exceeds 0.10 R. With ES costs ($17.00 round trip) a 14-tick stop ($175) costs ≈ 0.097 R and passes.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	CostFilterMaxFractionOfR = 0.10;               // illustrative: skip if the round trip costs > 0.10 R
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	double entry = Bars.Close[0];
	double stop = entry - 14 * tickSize();         // 14-tick stop
	string why;
	if (!costFilterPasses(Bars, entry, stop, out why)) return;   // why = cost and R, traced
}
```

**Used in examples.** none.

**Pitfalls.**

- The filter reads MC's Commission and Slippage settings ($ per contract per side); set them in the strategy properties to $4.50 commission and 0.5 tick slippage so the ES round trip is $17.00.
- `CostFilterMaxFractionOfR` only skips; it never widens a stop. At 0.10 with a 14-tick stop the trade passes: $17.00 / (14 × $12.50 = $175) = 0.097 R ≤ 0.10. A stop of 13 ticks ($162.50, 0.105 R) would be skipped.
- **The floor is example code, not library behaviour.** The stop floor (stop = max(structural, 14 ticks); skip if q × widened stop × $50 > 0.01 × equity; never shrink q) is Ettienne's decided rule (2026-10-23), implemented **per strategy**: main (a07257a) has no stop floor or widening, and each example implements it locally (`costFloorTicks = 14`, `riskBudgetFraction = 0.01`, a local `overRiskBudget` or an inline check using `equityAvailable(fraction)`). The library part is **pending**: a library `CostConfig` is only specified in `instrument-profile-spec.md` IP-3.2/IP-8.1 (not built). The only library cost rule is `CostFilterMaxFractionOfR` (default 0 = off), which skips and never widens. Consequence: a strategy that sizes at exactly 1% risk skips every stop under 14 ticks (any widening breaches the budget); the floor widens without a skip only for fixed size or sizing below 1%. The examples trade a fixed 1 contract, so a 14-tick stop ($175) is skipped only when equity < $17,500.

### 5.25 M-GMM — Bootstrap POC, KDE sweep, GMM/BIC, entropy, KL

**Status on main a07257a.** ✓ Available on main (PRs #18, #21, #24).

**Why it exists.** Whether a POC is stable and a profile one- or two-humped decides whether its levels can be trusted.

**Where.** `_ProfileStats.Function.CS` · `_ProfileStats` (`BootstrapPoc`, `BootstrapSeed`, `KdeSweep`, `Entropy`, `Histogram`, `ProfileMixture`, `MixtureSeed`, `MixtureLevels`), `_GmmProfileTracker`, `BootstrapResult`, `KdeResult`, `ProfileMixtureResult`; `_Gmm.Function.CS`; host `ProfileStats`, `ProfileEntropy`, `SessionBars`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
public EntropyResult ProfileEntropy(VpExtra e, double mmN)   // _VolumeProfileBase · _VolumeProfileBase:3458
public List<RingBar> SessionBars(DateTime tradingDay, bool rthOnly)   // _VolumeProfileBase · _VolumeProfileBase:3468
public static ulong BootstrapSeed(DateTime tradingDay, int block, int draws)   // _ProfileStats · _ProfileStats:495
public static BootstrapResult BootstrapPoc(IList<RingBar> bars, double tick, int distribution, int block, int draws, ulong seed)   // _ProfileStats · _ProfileStats:503
public static KdeResult KdeSweep(IList<RingBar> bars, double tick, int distribution, double mcPoc = double.NaN, double medFloorTicks = 1, int folds = 5, ECvFolding folding = ECvFolding.Contiguous)   // _ProfileStats · _ProfileStats:559
public static ulong MixtureSeed(DateTime tradingDay)   // _ProfileStats · _ProfileStats:724
public static ProfileMixtureResult ProfileMixture(double[] prices, double[] vols, int kMax, ulong seed, EGmmNeff neff, int bars, double tick, GmmFitConfig cfg = null)   // _ProfileStats · _ProfileStats:742
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn25`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Enables profile stats, takes yesterday's RTH bars and logs POC stability (bootstrap) and the KDE verdict (`Bimodal` = two humps, `Stable`, `Flat`). How `KdeSweep` decides (`_ProfileStats` L594–609): it refits the KDE POC over a sweep of bandwidths and measures the POC jumps between neighbouring bandwidths, with the median jump floored at `medFloorTicks` ticks. σ = 0 (one traded row) → `Stable`; largest jump > 5 × median → `Bimodal`; largest jump < 2 × median **and** POC range < 0.15σ → `Stable`; otherwise `Flat`.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	ProfileStats.enabled = true;                   // lazy: Data1 ring (ringBars) + range profiles
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	DateTime day = Bars.Time[0].Date.AddDays(-1);  // illustrative: use the real prior trading day (see Ex35)
	List<RingBar> bars = SessionBars(day, true);
	if (bars.Count < 20) return;
	double tick = tickSize();
	BootstrapResult b = _ProfileStats.BootstrapPoc(bars, tick, 2, 5, 500,
		_ProfileStats.BootstrapSeed(day, 5, 500));   // deterministic seed per day
	KdeResult k = _ProfileStats.KdeSweep(bars, tick, 2);
	if (journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"POC stability {b.stability:F2} ci90 {b.ci90Ticks}t KDE {k.verdict}");
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 8 (§3.24), example 27 (§3.27), example 35 (§3.32), example 36 (§3.39).

**Pitfalls.**

- Use the deterministic seeds (`BootstrapSeed`, `MixtureSeed`) so reruns give the same draws.
- `SessionBars` is empty unless `ProfileStats.enabled` (or `RangeProfiles.enabled`) and the ring holds that day.
- Compute session statistics once per day; they are not cheap.

Enables range, weekly and FVG helpers and logs the POC of the last 20 bars and last week's value area.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	RangeProfiles.enabled = true;                  // VP-1 (ring on Data1)
	WeeklyProfiles.enabled = true;                 // VP-2
	Fvgs.enabled = true;                           // VP-5
	Fvgs.minAtrFrac = 0.25;                        // illustrative
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	VpExtra last20 = RangeProfileBars(1, 0, 20);   // profile of the last 20 closed Data1 bars
	VpExtra lastWeek = Week(1);
	if (last20 != null && lastWeek != null && journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"20-bar POC {last20.poc} last-week VA {lastWeek.val}-{lastWeek.vah}");
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 24 (§3.19), example 8 (§3.24), example 35 (§3.32), example 7 (§3.33), example 10 (§3.34), example 33 (§3.37), example 34 (§3.38), example 36 (§3.39).

**Pitfalls.**

- Every helper is lazy and needs its `enabled` flag in `Create()`; the ring length (`ringBars`) bounds how far back range profiles reach.
- Enabling `BarLadders` turns `OrderFlowEnabled` on; the BarUpDown tier spreads volume uniformly, so ladder cluster logic must require `HasBidAsk`.
- Range and weekly profiles can cross a roll (`crossesRoll`); the roll policy decides whether they reject, split or reset.

### 5.28 A2-33 — Matched-control level study; Kaplan–Meier / Cox level death

**Status on main a07257a.** ◐ Available except Cox (PRs #15, #16; Gate-0 verdict PR #20): matched controls, paired edge, Holm, terciles, half-life, Kaplan–Meier; Cox regression not implemented. DVP levels feed the study through the M-DVP D7 hook (PR #42, §5.31): with DVP and `LevelStudyConfig` both on, the G0-d DVP rails and confluence levels are added once per session. `_LevelStudy` itself is unchanged.

**Why it exists.** Tests whether a level type gets more reactions than matched random prices before you trade it.

**Where.** `_LevelStudy.Function.CS` · `_LevelStudy`, `LevelStudyConfig`, `LevelTypeResult`; `_CustomSignal.LevelStudyConfig`, `levelStudyAdd`

**Key signatures.**

```csharp
protected LevelStudyConfig LevelStudyConfig { get; }   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1328
protected bool levelStudyAdd(string type, double price, int ageSessions, double quality = double.NaN, string key = null)   // _CustomSignal · _CustomSignal:1359
```

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn28`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Registers yesterday's POC with the level study at the RTH open; results appear at the end of the run.

```csharp
// Create
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	LevelStudyConfig.enabled = true;               // lazy; harness steps inside base.CalcBar()
}

// CalcBar
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();                                // add levels AFTER base.CalcBar(), on the first bar of the session
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	VpExtra p = PriorRthProfile();
	bool rthOpen = ToEastern(Bars.Time[0]).TimeOfDay == new TimeSpan(9, 30, 0);   // first bar of the session
	if (p != null && rthOpen)
		levelStudyAdd("POC", p.poc, 1);            // prior POC, 1 session old
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 41 (§3.41).

**Pitfalls.**

- The reference price of a level is the close of the bar `levelStudyAdd` is called on; call it once per level per session.
- No Cox model: use the Kaplan–Meier output and age buckets.

### 5.31 M-DVP — Detrended volume-profile engine (Keller B5)

**Status on main a07257a.** ◐ Partially on main a07257a. The spec is m-dvp-spec rev 5.3; main implements the rev 5–5.3 amendments through PR-D0a–D7 (the rev 5.2 rules: `HtfAccepted` with explicit `countTrigger`/`deltaAtr` and N ≥ 2, `NextRailDistAtr` strictly beyond or NaN, `Tradeable` never reads `diagnosticOnly`; the rev 5.3 rules: the per-session `DVP A2-33` Journal line joined by `tradingDayKey`, levels first evaluated on the bar after creation):
- the fit core (D1) and the manifold and rails (D2);
- the Data1 host wiring with the `DVP CREATED/FIT/RUN` journal and cov_fwd (D3, PR #36), and the Fib lattice with the PA bridge (D4, PR #37);
- **D5 (PRs #39, #40):** confluence (`dvpConfluence`, `dvpFamiliesAt`, `DvpConf` rails), acceptance (`dvpTriggered`/`dvpRunBeyond`/`dvpBackInside`/`dvpAccepted`), the 8-bar rail history, the `DVP RAIL` journal and `dvpZClose`;
- **D6 (PR #41):** Data2/Data3 HTF streams of the same root, the Probe P2 gate (`dvpTradeable`), `dvpHtfAccepted` and `dvpHtfNextRailDistAtr`.
- **D7 (PR #42):** the A2-33 hook. With `LevelStudyConfig.enabled` and DVP on, the first closed Data1 bar of each new `tradingDayKey` sends every G0-d level (`DvpPoc/Vah/Val/Hvn/Lvn/Sigma/Conf`) of `dvpRails(1)` and `dvpConfluence(1)` to `levelStudyAdd` (age 1, the rail's quality and tag). Roll-crossing and roll-window levels are skipped, and `DvpFib` is counted but not added. Each level is first evaluated on the next bar. At Journal level one `DVP A2-33 runId() tradingDayKey(yyyy-MM-dd) halfDay() rails() conf() rollSkipped() fibSkipped()` line per session joins the LevelStudy CSV by day.

**Pending:** D8 the KD02 canary and Calibrate study; D9 the frozen d\* (`DvpFrozenDegree.Table` is empty, so `DevCoverageFrozen` falls back to Calibrate with reported d = 1 and one `DVP DEGREE not frozen` warning); `rollMode` (DV-7.0, waits for the instrument profile); Probe P2 itself (`DvpConst.P2_PASSED = false`, so HTF streams are not tradeable). The host still has no `NextRail(ds, …)` or `Cone(ds, …)` wrapper. The catalogue row still says "not yet coded".

**Why it exists.** A profile fitted around a trend line (detrended) gives value rails that move with the trend instead of a flat VA.

**Where.** `_CustomSignal.Strategy.CS`:
- **Configuration and engine:** `DvpConfig` (protected property, L397), `Dvp` (internal, L405), `rthKnown()` (L415, overridden to true in `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase` L515).
- **Fit and rails:** `dvpSnapshot` (L425), `dvpRails` (L434), `dvpRailPrice` (L610), `dvpZ` (L619).
- **D5 confluence and acceptance:** `dvpConfluence` (L485), `dvpFamiliesAt` (L499/L510), `dvpTriggered` (L526), `dvpRunBeyond` (L528), `dvpBackInside` (L530), `dvpAccepted` (L533), `dvpZClose` (L539).
- **D6 HTF:** `dvpTradeable` (L582), `dvpHtfAccepted` (L588), `dvpHtfNextRailDistAtr` (L597).
- **D7 A2-33 hook (private):** `dvpLevelStudyStep` (L467, called from `CalcBar` L2100 after `stepLevelStudy`).
- **Private wiring:** `startDvp` (L665, called from `startEngines` L1842), `stepDvp` (L698, from `stepEngines` L1906, after the rings), `dvpRailStep` (L551, `DVP RAIL`, from `stepEngines` L1914 after every PA step), and `m_dvp.Finish()` (L2101, `DVP RUN` lines on the last historical bar).

`_DvpEngine.Function.CS`:
- `DvpConfig`, `DvpFit`, `DvpManifold`, `DvpRails`, `DvpSnapshot`, `DvpStream`, `_DvpEngine` (incl. `Tradeable` L1249).
- The D4 classes `DvpPivots`, `DvpLattice` and `DvpPaBridge.Fill` (internal).
- The D5 classes `DvpConfluence` (L1513), `DvpAccept` (L1665), `DvpRailHistory` (L1728) and `DvpRailJournal` (L1842).
- The D6 class `DvpHtf` (L1983).
- The D7 class `DvpA233` (L1947: `Line`, `IsG0dType`, `Sample`).

`_LevelRef.Function.CS`: `ELevelKind.DvpPoc…DvpConf`.

**Snippet** (compiled as `_Sn31`, base `_VolumeProfileFuturesBase`).

Enables DVP with Fibonacci rails and logs the fit quality, the close's z-distance from the trend-adjusted value, the VAH and +2σ rails, and the rail count.

```csharp
// Create: configuration only. DvpConfig is a protected get-only property; set its fields, never an [Input].
protected override void Create()
{
	base.Create();
	DvpConfig.enabled = true;                      // lazy: engine, ring and rail lists exist only when enabled
	DvpConfig.useFib = true;                       // D4 lattice: hosts fibRanges PA pivot engines (strengths 20/40/80)
}
// StartCalc: nothing to do. base.StartCalc() -> startEngines() -> startDvp() validates DvpConfig and builds the engine.

// CalcBar: query after base.CalcBar() (stepDvp has run); the fit is as of t-1 for bar t (no lookahead).
protected override void CalcBar()
{
	base.CalcBar();
	if (Bars.Status != EBarState.Close) return;
	DvpSnapshot s = dvpSnapshot(1);                // null: disabled, warm-up, or a window that crosses a roll
	if (s == null || !s.valid) return;
	double z = dvpZ(1, Bars.Close[0]);             // (price - yhat(u_t)) / sigma
	double vah = dvpRailPrice(1, ELevelKind.DvpVah, 0);     // NaN if absent
	double up2 = dvpRailPrice(1, ELevelKind.DvpSigma, 2);   // +2 sigma rail, NaN if absent
	IReadOnlyList<LevelRef> rails = dvpRails(1);   // reused list: copy what you keep
	int fib = 0;
	for (int i = 0; i < rails.Count; i++)
	{
		if (rails[i].kind == ELevelKind.DvpFib) fib++;
	}
	if (journalOn())
		trace(ETraceLevel.Journal, $"dvp deg {s.deg} r2 {s.r2:F2} z {z:F2} vah {vah} +2s {up2} rails {rails.Count} fib {fib}");
}
```

**Used in examples.** example 42 (§3.42).

**Pitfalls.**

- **Streams.** `DvpConfig.streams` may list Data1 and the HTF streams Data2/Data3. Each must exist (`requireDataStream`) and carry the Data1 root, or `StartCalc` throws `ArgumentException` (`DvpHtf.RootError`, DV-7.0).
- **HTF streams are not tradeable yet.** `dvpTradeable(ds)` is false for ds ≥ 2 (one `used before Probe P2` warning) until `DvpConst.P2_PASSED`, which is `false` on main. MT-07 trades the 60m stream only; 240m is diagnostic by consumer rule (DV-7.5). `Tradeable` does not hard-code a timeframe.
- **When to set the config.** Set `DvpConfig` fields in `Create()`, or in `StartCalc()` before `base.StartCalc()`. `startDvp` validates once in `StartCalc` and throws `ArgumentException` on a bad config.
- **`fibAnchor`.** `fibAnchor = ExtremeInLookback` is rejected: `Validate()` reports "fibAnchor … not supported (MostRecentPivot only, spec rev 5 §2)" (`_DvpEngine` L107, PR #39 prelude 334dc0b). Leave it at `MostRecentPivot`.
- **When to query.** Query only after `base.CalcBar()`. The host picks the window end itself (`dvpEndAgo` → `DvpHtf.EndAgo`), so a snapshot on bar t is the fit as of t−1. On an HTF stream only closed HTF bars are read; a forming HTF bar never reaches a snapshot.
- **Reused lists.** `dvpRails` and `dvpConfluence` return lists (and `LevelRef` objects) reused by the next call. Copy anything you keep across bars. `LevelRef.quality` on a rail is the number of families in its cluster (DV-6.5).
- **Rail lookup.** `dvpRailPrice` matches z exactly for `DvpSigma`/`DvpHvn`/`DvpLvn`/`DvpFib`; for `DvpPoc`/`DvpVah`/`DvpVal` z is ignored. Acceptance history matches HVN/LVN to the nearest same-kind rail within |dz| ≤ 0.25 (`DvpRailHistory.NodeZTol`).
- **Confluence.** A `DvpConf` level is a cluster with at least `DvpConst.MinFamilies` = 3 families (manifold, sigma, lattice) within `confTol` (0.30 ATR) (`DvpConfluence.Qualifies`).
- **Acceptance semantics.** `dvpAccepted(ds, rl, dir, N, countTrigger, deltaAtr)` is true on the completing bar.
  - Trigger: a close beyond its own rail by more than `DvpAccept.Delta` = 0.10 ATR, where the bar before closed not beyond (`Triggered`).
  - countTrigger = true: the trigger close **plus N further closes**, all beyond the rail by more than deltaAtr·ATR. False: N closes in total, including the trigger.
  - N must be 1..6 (`MaxN`), else `ArgumentOutOfRangeException`. There is no default convention (DV-8.3), so pass both explicitly. PS-25 uses N = 2, countTrigger = true, deltaAtr = 0. Per the spec, KD-06/KD-10 pass countTrigger = false.
  - It returns false for `DvpFib` and `DvpConf` levels, which have no rail history (DV-10.4).
- **HTF acceptance.** `dvpHtfAccepted` is the same test on an HTF stream's closed bars: it needs ds ≥ 2 and N in 2..6, else it throws. `dvpHtfNextRailDistAtr(ds, dir)` is the distance from Data1's C(t−1) to the nearest HTF rail or lattice level strictly beyond it, in the HTF ATR. It returns NaN when there is no fit or no such rail.
- **d\* is not frozen (D9).** With the default `DevCoverageFrozen`, the reported degree is 1 and `s.calibrating` is true. Do not read results by degree until PR-D9.
- **No trade verdict yet.** Since D7 the A2-33 study can score DVP level types (G0-d), but no DVP type has a verdict yet, and nothing gates trades on one. The rails, confluence and acceptance are research levels and primitives, not validated trade triggers. The A2-33 `halfDay` split needs Journal trace level (the `DVP A2-33` line is written only then). The `DVP RAIL` journal logs interactions to `logSignal` with `taken: false`.
- **Disabled engine.** Calling `Dvp` while it is disabled returns null and logs one Warning; the host queries then return null, empty lists, NaN or false. The direct `DvpFit` API (fit core) is still public for offline sketches.
- **`diagnosticOnly` never gates `Tradeable` (by design).** Rev 5.2 (DV-7.0, DV-7.4) makes `diagnosticOnly` a run-level verdict, reported in the `DVP RUN` line only; a stream stays tradeable under rollMode None. Do not expect `dvpTradeable` to turn false on it (`_DvpEngine` L1249).
- **Still pending (m-dvp-spec rev 5.3; not on main a07257a).**
  - D8 the KD02 canary and Calibrate study, and D9 the frozen d\*.
  - Probe P2 (which flips `P2_PASSED`) and `rollMode` (DV-7.0) are also pending. `rollMode` None waits for the instrument profile.
  - There is no winLen sweep, and no 240m stream for trading (DV-1.1, DV-7.5).
  - The DVP-hosted PA engines use `minBreakoutLayer = 0` (`_CustomSignal` L647), unlike the main engine's default 1 (§4.8).


---

## Appendices

### A. `ETraceLevel`

| Level | Value | Enables |
|---|---|---|
| None | 0 | nothing (the default after `Create`) |
| Error | 1 | errors (for example the Event coverage failure) |
| Warning | 2 | + one-time "engine queried but not enabled" warnings, config warnings |
| Info | 3 | + RUN / RUN-END, `entry blocked:`, roll detected, XMKT leg, PORT publish |
| Journal | 4 | + engine facts, `MCJournal_<runId>.csv`, `MCEvents_<runId>.csv` |
| Verbose | 5 | + equity and account details |
| Debug | 6 | + engine debug sinks (for example PA `trace`), sizing reasons |

### B. Files and formats

All files go to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\MCSignalLogs\` (fallback: `%TEMP%`).

- **Trace log** `MCSignal{yyyyMMdd-HHmmss}-{pid}.csv`, one per process. Columns: `level,yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss {zone},signalName,symbol,runId,message`.
- **Trade journal** `MCJournal_<runId>.csv`, with the header:
  `runId,strategy,symbol,tag,side,qty,entryTime,entryPrice,exitTime,exitPrice,exitOrder,barsHeld,profit,commission,armed,plannedEntry,entrySlipTicks,initialStop,target,riskPerUnit,riskTicks,riskDollars,rMultiple,mfeDollars,maeDollars,mfeTicks,maeTicks,mfeR,maeR,excursionScope,context`
  plus `,evtDayTier,evtMinSince` when M-EVENT is on. The `context` column holds the `journalArm` pairs as `k=v|k=v`.
- **Signal events** `MCEvents_<runId>.csv`: `runId,strategy,symbol,tag,direction,time,bar,price,stop,riskPerUnit,taken,controlMode`, then `,mfeR_{h},maeR_{h}` for each horizon h, then `,completedBy,context` (exact header checked in `_CustomSignal`). The context is `k=v;`.
- **How lines are written (PR #49).** Every trace, journal and event line is one `File.AppendAllText` (UTF-8, no BOM) under a lock; no file handle stays open between lines. A log deleted, moved or emptied while MultiCharts runs is recreated on the next line (the journal and event CSVs get their header again when the file is missing). Write errors (e.g. the CSV open in Excel) go to the calling signal's Output window and that line is dropped; they never reach the strategy. There is nothing to close in `Destroy()`.
- **Which file is mine?** The trace log is process-wide, so its `Log file created …` line appears once, in the Output of whichever signal traced first. Each signal instance also prints its own path once to its own Output when it first traces: `{SignalClass} {Symbol}: trace log {path}`.
- **Engine line prefixes:** `DAILY`, `REGIME`, `REGGATE`, `EVENT`, `AV`, `PA{ds}[-role]`, `TOD`, `XMKT`, `TICK`, `MTF`, `RING`, `RP`, `LADDER`, `VPB-1`, `VPF-4`, `VPF-7`, `PORT`, `A2-33`.

### C. Running the tests

All tests need the .NET 8 SDK. Each `tests/<Name>/run.sh` does `dotnet run -c Release` in its folder. Where a `gen.sh` exists, it generates `Generated/*.cs` from the real study sources (for example it extracts the `// <calendar-rules …>` blocks), and the build runs it itself.

```bash
cd MultiCharts.Net
for t in tests/*/run.sh; do echo "== $t"; bash "$t" || break; done
bash tests/RegimeTests/run.sh          # also supports the reference.py export path
bash tests/EventTests/gen.sh            # regenerate only
```

Suites: Avwap, Calendar, Daily, Event, Gate0, Gmm, LevelStudy, Mtf, OrderFlow, PortMM, PriceAction, Profile, RegimeGate, Regime, Roll, SessionClock, Tick, Tod, VpHelper, Xmkt. `gen.sh` exists in Calendar, Daily, Event, Gate0, Gmm, OrderFlow, Profile and RegimeGate.

### D. Glossary

- **AsOf**: the date whose completed data produced a value. It is usable strictly after that date.
- **AVWAP**: anchored VWAP from an event bar.
- **BOS / CHoCH**: break of structure / change of character (a BOS against the prior structure).
- **Closed bar**: `Bars.Status == EBarState.Close`. Engines commit only on these.
- **Globex date**: the CME trading date. The 18:00 ET evening session belongs to the next date.
- **Half-day**: a CME early RTH close (`RthCloseEt`, 13:15 ET) or a holiday halt.
- **HTF**: a higher-timeframe stream of the same instrument (`HtfStreams`).
- **Leg**: a cross-market stream (`XmktLegs`).
- **No-RTH day**: Globex is open but there is no cash session (an NYSE holiday).
- **Placebo (P1/P2)**: an AVWAP twin with neutralised volume, used for falsification.
- **Roll splice**: the first bar of a new contract on a continuous unadjusted series.
- **RTH value window**: `(09:30, 16:00]` ET, or up to the early close.
- **Tier**: event importance (T1 > T2 > T3, C = calendar).
- **TOD**: time-of-day normalisation per session-minute bucket.

### F. Open VERIFY items and discrepancies

**Still VERIFY (could not be settled from the code; most need MultiCharts itself):**

1. **TraceLevel override.** The default `None` is set in the `_CustomSignal` constructor (PR #49). Whether MC applies the Format Signal dialog value of `TraceLevel` after a value set in code in the constructor.
2. **Example trading day.** `_Test6` is settled (PR #46: Globex key via `_CmeSessionClock.GlobexTradingDayEt`). The M-DAILY examples 9, 10, 17, 20, 27, 30, 31, 32, 33 (and 35 via 32) that derive from a plain `_CustomSignal` still do not override `tradingDayKey` (and leave `TradingDayRoll` at Calendar). On a Globex template their M-DAILY days are calendar days, not Globex days. Decide if that is intended.
3. **EventBlackout timing.** `EventBlackout` is computed on the bar close for `[open, close)`. Whether an order sent on that bar (filled next bar) should also check the next bar's window.
4. **M-TICK reflection.** The DataLoader member names that `_TickStream` reaches by reflection (they only resolve inside MC).
5. **HTF bar-end stamping (MC Probe P2).** Examples 2, 5, 6, 7, 10, 11, 13, 14 and 36 (all M-MTF users, E2) assume HTF and 1m bars are stamped at their end. Run Probe P2 first, as the catalogue says.
6. **G0-a cost model.** The A2-40 cost model used by Gate 0 (including the 2-tick slippage for 09:30–09:35 fills) is VERIFY against the catalogue's A2-40/A2-59 text (§2.5).
7. **Example 10 zone break.** After the touch and before the 60m trigger, one RTH 1m close below the pullback zone kills the week's setup. That is a very strict coding choice; the catalogue is silent.
8. **MC API stubs.** The examples compile against stubs I wrote for the MC API. A member MC names differently only shows in the PowerLanguage editor.

9. **v4 examples (11–42).** Same caveat as item 8: compiled against my stubs only. PLACEHOLDER values (e.g. example 38's minimum imbalance volume, standing in for the catalogue's P70) must be calibrated.
10. **Newest-first order of `RecentCompletedProfiles`.** Example 41 ages naked POCs by their index in that list; the order is assumed newest first.

**Discrepancies found (code vs catalogue vs v1 manual):**

1. **M-DAILY at rolls — partly RESOLVED by PR #46.** v1 said "roll gaps are removed" from the ATR. On `main`, a roll reseeds the ATR by default: `Atr20d` is NaN for `atrLength` sessions after each roll (only when RollDetection is on). PR #46 added `DailyConfig.atrRestartOnRoll = false`: the ATR carries on and only the splice day's true range is skipped, which matches the catalogue's A2-63 "Atr20d, roll-gap removed"; `_Test6` uses it with `Table` detection. Examples that keep RollDetection Off (e.g. example 9) still let the gap enter the ATR.
2. **PS-13's 200-day ATR.** The catalogue's M-DAILY `atrLength = 200` never becomes valid with RollDetection on and the default `atrRestartOnRoll = true`, because @ES rolls about every 63 sessions. Example 7 computes its own ATR200d instead; `atrRestartOnRoll = false` (PR #46) would now also make the M-DAILY value usable (example 7 not changed).
3. **DV-03 follow-through** (≥ 1 box width before the retest) is not coded in example 7 (the PS-13 version).
4. **A2-29 gating.** The G0-d table lists the A2-25…A2-32 range as G0-d-gated, but A2-29's own entry says "Gate (v2): none (classifier)".
5. **`_Test1` vs `_TestGate0`.** The catalogue (§3.0: "A2-33 and A2-39 run in `_Test1`"; also the Batch-1 slot table) names `_Test1` as the host, and so does the `LevelStudyConfig` comment in `_CustomSignal` ("e.g. `_Test1`"). `_LevelStudy` itself doesn't name a host. `_Test1` is a plain `SignalObject`; Gate 0 is hosted by `_TestGate0`.
6. **KD-02 canary.** The catalogue's G0-b canary list includes KD-02; `EGate0Canary` does not have it.
7. **Cost per round trip (G0-a conflict) — RESOLVED by PR #30.** The `_TestGate0` header's MC settings are now $2.25 commission and 0.5 tick slippage ($6.25), each per side, so an ES round trip costs $4.50 + $12.50 = **$17.00**, matching the catalogue's cost floor (§2.1) assumption of **$16.98** (IBKR round trip + 1 tick in total). At $17.00 the 14-tick floor ($175) keeps costs at about 0.097 R, within ≤ 0.10 R. Earlier canary runs at the old setting were over-costed.
8. **Example file names — RESOLVED in v4.** Example numbers are now the file numbers; the §3 table gives the reading order.
9. **Cost floor: max or skip — RESOLVED** (decided by Ettienne 2026-10-23: widen, then skip only on a budget breach; size is never shrunk). Catalogue §2.1 said both "every idea's stop is max(structural stop, cost floor)" and "trades whose structural stop sits under the floor are logged as *skipped* rather than resized". Ruling: the stop distance is max(structural, floor), so a short structural stop is widened to the 14-tick floor; "skipped rather than resized" is about position size: if the widened stop breaches the per-trade risk budget, the trade is skipped rather than taken at a smaller size. v3.1 changed examples 5, 6, 7 and 10 (current file numbers) to widen-then-skip-on-budget-breach; all v4 stop-based examples follow the same rule; the budget is `riskBudgetFraction` = 0.01, 1% of equity (decided by Ettienne 2026-10-23; PF-04's 0.5% cap (§3.11) is not the default for the floor rule). The catalogue §2.1 text is reworded to match.
10. **AV-08 at rolls (example 3).** The catalogue says multi-day anchors reset at rolls; the Week/Month VWAP slots don't (`_VpVwapEngine` resets them only at a new week or month). Example 3 turns the layer off until the next clean period (`wtdClean`/`mtdClean`). Re-anchoring at `rollOn(1)` is the alternative.
11. **A2-57 at rolls (example 5).** The catalogue is silent on the 60m EMA at a roll. Example 5 re-seeds it at `rollOn(1)` (3 × 13 closed 60m bars of warm-up); ignoring turns for that long without re-seeding is the alternative.
12. **TS-01 market (example 2).** The catalogue tests MES on 30m/60m bars (kill $2.47). Example 2 is written for @ES on 1m.
13. **Example 10 zone break.** See VERIFY item 7: one 1m close below the zone after the touch cancels the week, which is stricter than the catalogue's text.
14. **M-DVP row.** The catalogue's §2.4 says M-DVP is "not yet coded". Main a07257a has spec rev 5.3 PR-D0a–D7: the fit core, manifold and rails, Data1 host wiring with the journal and cov_fwd (PR #36), the Fib lattice with the PA bridge (PR #37), D5 confluence and acceptance (PRs #39, #40: `dvpConfluence`, `dvpFamiliesAt`, `dvpTriggered`/`dvpRunBeyond`/`dvpBackInside`/`dvpAccepted`, `DvpConf` rails, `DVP RAIL`), and D6 HTF streams (PR #41: same-root Data2/3, `dvpTradeable` false for ds ≥ 2 until Probe P2, `dvpHtfAccepted`, `dvpHtfNextRailDistAtr`), and D7 the A2-33 hook (PR #42: `dvpLevelStudyStep`, `DvpA233`). `fibAnchor` other than `MostRecentPivot` is rejected. **Still pending:** D8 the canary/Calibrate study; D9 the frozen d\*; Probe P2; `rollMode`. Example 42 uses the D3/D4 host API only and remains a diagnostic until D8/D9.
15. **A2-33 row.** Kaplan–Meier is on main; Cox regression is not.
16. **Example 36 histogram.** A2-27 asks for the mixture on the session profile; the example builds the histogram from the Data1 5m ring (`SessionBars`), a coarser input.
17. **Example 40 vs A2-78.** The catalogue's kill switch is book-level; the strategy-level `DailyLossStop*` rules count one strategy's closed trades. The book-level rule is `_PortfolioRiskManager` / `PortMmConfig`.
18. **Example 39 vs PF-03.** PF-03 measures sleeve-P&L correlation; the example journals the bar-return correlation of the two instruments as a proxy.

**Known issues (open on main a07257a):**

1. **RollDetection.Series over-detects on intraday charts (open; fix in progress).** On 1-minute @ES 2016–2024, `Series` flags 72 rolls against about 33 real quarterly rolls. The extras are on weekend opens around roll week. The fix ("RollDetection.Series: key by Globex trading day, one splice per contract") is in PR #51, not yet merged. Until it is merged, use `RollDetection = Table` with `RollDates = EventRules.RollDate(y, 3/6/9/12)` on intraday charts (as `_Test6` does), and treat `Series`-based `rollOn`, Daily `OnRoll` and AVWAP/PA roll resets as unreliable. See §1.7 and §4.13.


### G. EV-4.6b — event-table coverage (PRs #28/#29)

**What changed.** Before EV-4.6b, any coverage gap in the event table disabled the whole engine. Now:

- The engine is **disabled only** when the compiled table is missing or its hash differs from `EventRules.ExpectedTableSha` (`"228cdee716a6eafa"` on a07257a, unchanged since 628542f). It writes an Error line ending `; engine disabled`, does not throw, and every query answers neutrally (no events, no blackout).
- Known holes in the table (`EventRules.CoverageHoles()`, part of the hash) become a third state per kind and day: `EEventCoverage { NoEvent = 0, Event = 1, Unknown = 2 }`, read with `Coverage(kind, day)`.

**Using it.**

```csharp
// in CalcBar, before acting on an event filter
_EventCalendar ev = EventCalendar;
if (ev == null || ev.Disabled) { /* fail closed: a disabled engine says "no event" */ return; }
EEventKind hole;
if (ev.CoverageBlocks(day, EEventClassMask.Macro, EEventTier.T1, out hole)) { /* unknown coverage: skip the day */ return; }
```

- `CoverageBlocks(day, mask, minTier, out kind)` returns true if some kind in `mask` is Unknown on that day and its (effective) tier passes `minTier`. It returns false when the engine is disabled or `EventConfig.allowPartialCoverage` is true. `CoverageBlocks(day, mask)` uses `EEventTier.None`.
- **PR #29:** an Unknown kind whose tier is below the query's `minTier` never blocks, and the tier test uses the **effective** tier (after `EventConfig.tierOverride`).
- `CoverageReport(from, to, mask[, minTier], out flagged)` reports, per kind, the share of Unknown-blocked trading days in `[from, to]`. `CoverageStats()` returns one `COVSHARE run {Kind} blocked {n}/{days} {p}%` line per kind, with ` FLAG` above 5%.

**Journal.** Once at start: `COVGAP {Kind} {from}..{to} Unknown …`. Then, per affected trading day: `COVGAP day {yyyy-MM-dd} Unknown Kind(blocks|belowMinTier|NoEvent)`.

**Where the manual uses it.** Example 2 (TS-01) skips Unknown T1 macro days and fails closed when the engine is disabled. Example 4 (A2-50) tags Unknown days in its journal instead of calling them report or non-report days. The catalogue's TS-01 caveat about GDP/PCE holes in 2016–24 is the case this handles.

### F2. Optional follow-ups (not required, not scheduled)

These come from the PR #47 and PR #48 reviews. They are optional, and none of them is a known defect.

From PR #47 (`specs/pr47-review-analyst1.md`, cheap non-blocking test pins):
1. Strip `/* */` as well as `//` in the pin `strip` helper.
2. Pin each of the four confluence adapter getter lines exactly once.
3. Pin `nSources =`, `zone.developingHits =`, `zone.developingScore =`, `m_prices.Clear()` and `UsesConfluenceZones =` (in the base) to an exact count.
4. Pin `double atrFraction = m_owner.ConfluenceSplitAtrFraction;`.
5. The remaining evasions (an early return, a builder returning null, a swallowed throw) need an MC-free engine harness or acceptance as a known limit of source pins.
6. Developing hits still score and count in `independentSources`; whether to document or exclude them is Ettienne's decision.

From PR #48 (`specs/pr48-review-analyst1.md`, `specs/pr48-review-analyst2.md`):
1. `FlowEngine` has no `Reset()`; if a reset path is added, it must clear `m_dropped`, `m_droppedResetKey` and `m_q*` (worth a comment).
2. Lowering `MaxBars` on a live engine drops one bar per CloseBar, so the window shrinks slowly (predates #48).
3. `DroppedBars` is cumulative over the run and never decreases (stated in §5.9).
4. Test: ResetKey at default and no drops → CumDelta not Truncated (kills the `m_dropped>0` survivor).
5. Test: an A,B,A ResetKey sequence, only if non-monotonic keys are possible (roll or replay).
